praxis
/ˈpræksɪs/
核心：（哲学，正式）实践：把一种理论、一门学问实际用起来、做出来，跟 theory 相对。不可数。
① The essay argues that theory is worthless unless it leads to praxis.
= 那篇文章认为理论若不通向实践就毫无价值。
praxis = 实践
书面词，哲学、教育学里常见。
② The course joins the study of teaching to the praxis of the classroom.
= 那门课把教学研究跟课堂实践结合起来。
the praxis of the classroom = 课堂实践
来自希腊语「做」。

pray
/preɪ/
核心：动词：祈祷、祷告：pray to 加神、pray for 加人或事、pray that；引申为极力盼望：pray for rain。
副词：（旧，正式）请：用在请求、提问前面，如今多带讽刺。
① They knelt down and prayed to God for help.
= 他们跪下来向上帝祈求帮助。
pray to God = 向上帝祈祷
pray for somebody = 为某人祈祷
② The whole village prayed for the safe return of the fishing boats.
= 全村人都为渔船平安归来祈祷。
pray for something = 为某事祈祷
③ We are praying that the weather will hold until the harvest is in.
= 我们巴望着天气能撑到庄稼收完。
pray that = 巴望、祈祷
这一支不一定跟宗教有关。
④ And what, pray, do you think you are doing in my garden?
= 请问，你以为你在我的花园里干什么？
pray = 请（旧式，带讽刺）

prayer
/preə(r)/
核心：名词：祷词、祈祷文，可数：say a prayer；祈祷（这件事），不可数：kneel in prayer；prayers 是祈祷仪式；也指心里极盼的事。
习语：not have a prayer 是（口语）毫无成功的希望；on a wing and a prayer 是全凭侥幸、勉强撑着；the answer to somebody's prayers 是某人求之不得的人、物。
形容词 prayerful 是虔诚祈祷的。
复合词：prayer book 是祈祷书；prayer meeting 是祷告会；prayer rug、prayer mat 是（穆斯林的）礼拜毯；prayer wheel 是（藏传佛教的）转经筒。并入本条。
① The children say their prayers before they go to bed.
= 孩子们睡前做祷告。
say your prayers = 做祷告
a prayer for something = 为某事作的祷告
② The old woman knelt in prayer before the altar.
= 老太太跪在祭坛前祈祷。
in prayer = 在祈祷
这一支不可数。
③ The school begins each day with prayers in the hall.
= 那所学校每天以礼堂里的祈祷开始。
prayers = 祈祷仪式
morning prayers = 晨祷
④ The team does not have a prayer against the champions.
= 那支球队对上冠军毫无胜算。
not have a prayer = 毫无成功的希望
⑤ The pilot brought the damaged plane home on a wing and a prayer.
= 飞行员全凭侥幸把受损的飞机开了回来。
on a wing and a prayer = 全凭侥幸
⑥ A flat near the station was the answer to our prayers.
= 车站附近的一套公寓正是我们求之不得的。
the answer to somebody's prayers = 某人求之不得的东西
⑦ The service ended with a prayerful silence of two minutes.
= 礼拜以两分钟虔诚的默祷结束。
prayerful = 虔诚祈祷的
⑧ She carried her grandmother's prayer book to church every Sunday.
= 她每个星期天带着奶奶的祈祷书去教堂。
a prayer book = 祈祷书
⑨ The women hold a prayer meeting in the hall on Wednesday evenings.
= 妇女们星期三晚上在礼堂里开祷告会。
a prayer meeting = 祷告会
⑩ He unrolled his prayer rug and turned towards the east.
= 他铺开礼拜毯，转身朝向东方。
a prayer rug = 礼拜毯
⑪ The pilgrims turned the prayer wheels as they walked round the temple.
= 朝圣的人绕着寺庙走，一边转动转经筒。
a prayer wheel = 转经筒

pre-Columbian
/ˌpriː kəˈlʌmbiən/
核心：前哥伦布时期的：一四九二年哥伦布到达以前的美洲的。只放在名词前。
① The museum has a fine collection of pre-Columbian pottery from Peru.
= 那家博物馆藏有一批精美的秘鲁前哥伦布时期陶器。
pre-Columbian pottery = 前哥伦布时期的陶器
pre-Columbian art = 前哥伦布时期的艺术
② The road follows the route of a pre-Columbian trail across the mountains.
= 那条公路沿着一条前哥伦布时期翻山小道的路线走。
a pre-Columbian trail = 前哥伦布时期的小道
pre-Columbian America = 哥伦布到达以前的美洲

pre-empt
/priˈempt/
核心：及物。一是抢先行动以防止（某事发生）、先发制人；二是抢在（某人）前头做、说，让他做不成；三是（美式）（电视台）用别的节目顶掉（原定的节目）。
名词 pre-emption，不可数；形容词 pre-emptive 是先发制人的：a pre-emptive strike。并入本条。
① The government raised wages in an attempt to pre-empt a strike.
= 政府涨了工资，想抢先一步把罢工挡住。
pre-empt a strike = 抢先挡住罢工
pre-empt something = 抢先防止某事
② She was about to apologize, but he pre-empted her by walking out.
= 她正要道歉，他却抢先一步走了出去。
pre-empt somebody = 抢在某人前头
③ The film was pre-empted by a special report on the election.
= 那部片子被一档选举特别报道顶掉了。
be pre-empted by something = 被某节目顶掉
这一支是美式说法。
④ The general ordered a pre-emptive strike against the enemy airfields.
= 将军下令对敌方机场实施先发制人的打击。
a pre-emptive strike = 先发制人的打击
pre-emptive action = 先发制人的行动
⑤ The right of pre-emption allows the tenant to buy the land before anyone else.
= 优先购买权让佃户可以先于别人买下那块地。
pre-emption = 抢先、优先购买
the right of pre-emption = 优先购买权

pre-loved
/ˌpriː ˈlʌvd/
核心：（口语）二手的、别人用过的：商家、卖主爱用的好听说法，说东西原先有人珍爱过。
① The shop sells pre-loved designer dresses at a third of the original price.
= 那家店卖二手名牌连衣裙，价钱是原价的三分之一。
pre-loved dresses = 二手连衣裙
pre-loved = 二手的
② We found a good home for our pre-loved pram through an advertisement in the paper.
= 我们靠报上的一则广告，给家里用过的童车找了个好人家。
a pre-loved pram = 用过的童车
是 second-hand 的委婉说法。

pre-owned
/ˌpriː ˈəʊnd/
核心：（尤其美式）二手的、别人用过的：商家卖车、卖表时爱用的说法。
① The garage has a large stock of pre-owned cars with low mileage.
= 那家车行有一大批里程数低的二手车。
a pre-owned car = 二手车
pre-owned = 二手的
② He bought a pre-owned watch from a dealer he had known for years.
= 他从一个认识多年的商人那里买了一块二手表。
a pre-owned watch = 二手表
比 used、second-hand 听着体面。

pre-qualifying
/ˌpriː ˈkwɒlɪfaɪɪŋ/
核心：（体育）预选赛的、资格赛之前的：为了取得参加资格赛、正赛的资格而进行的。只放在名词前。
① She won three matches in the pre-qualifying rounds of the tournament.
= 她在那项赛事的预选轮里赢了三场。
the pre-qualifying rounds = 预选轮
a pre-qualifying round = 预选轮
② Twelve drivers took part in the pre-qualifying session on Friday morning.
= 星期五上午有十二名车手参加了预选排位。
a pre-qualifying session = 预选排位
a pre-qualifying tournament = 预选赛

pre-teen
/ˌpriː ˈtiːn/
核心：形容词：十三岁以前的、十到十二岁左右的，只放在名词前。名词，可数：十来岁、还没到十三岁的孩子。
① The magazine is aimed at pre-teen girls who are interested in music.
= 那本杂志是办给喜欢音乐的十来岁女孩看的。
pre-teen girls = 十来岁的女孩
the pre-teen years = 十三岁以前的那几年
② The cinema was full of noisy pre-teens eating popcorn.
= 电影院里坐满了吃着爆米花、吵吵嚷嚷的十来岁孩子。
a pre-teen = 还没到十三岁的孩子
指九到十二岁上下。

preach
/priːtʃ/
核心：动词：布道、讲道：（神职人员）在礼拜上宣讲教义；宣扬、鼓吹（一种主张、做法）；（贬）说教、唠唠叨叨地教训人：preach at、to。
习语：preach to the converted 是对已经信了的人传道、白费口舌；practise what you preach 是言行一致。
派生：preacher 是传道人、牧师；preachy 是（口语，贬）爱说教的。并入本条。
① The vicar preached on forgiveness to a church that was almost empty.
= 牧师对着几乎空无一人的教堂讲了一篇关于宽恕的道。
preach on something = 就某题目布道
preach a sermon = 讲道
② She preaches the benefits of a diet without meat or sugar.
= 她宣扬不吃肉、不吃糖的饮食的好处。
preach the benefits of something = 宣扬某事的好处
③ I wish he would stop preaching at me about my smoking.
= 我真希望他别再为我抽烟的事教训我。
preach at somebody = 教训某人
这一支带贬义：说教。
④ Telling teachers that books matter is preaching to the converted.
= 跟教师讲书很重要，是对着信徒传道。
preach to the converted = 对已经信了的人传道
⑤ The preacher spoke for an hour without looking at his notes.
= 那位传道人讲了一个钟头，没看一眼笔记。
a preacher = 传道人
多指新教里讲道有感染力的人。
⑥ The film is spoiled by a preachy ending about the evils of money.
= 那部片子被一个说教味很重、讲金钱之恶的结尾糟蹋了。
preachy = 爱说教的

preamble
/priˈæmbl/
核心：（正式）序言、前言、开场白：正文之前说明目的、缘由的那一段。可数也可不可数；without preamble 是开门见山地。
① The preamble to the treaty sets out the aims of the two governments.
= 条约的序言阐明了两国政府的目标。
the preamble to a treaty = 条约的序言
宪法、法律文件开头的那段也叫这个。
② She told me without preamble that the firm was closing.
= 她开门见山地告诉我公司要关门了。
without preamble = 开门见山地

prearranged
/ˌpriːəˈreɪndʒd/
核心：预先安排好的、事先约定的。
① At a prearranged signal the lights went out and the band began to play.
= 一个事先约好的信号一发，灯灭了，乐队奏起来。
a prearranged signal = 事先约好的信号
也写作 pre-arranged。
② The two men met at a prearranged spot behind the station.
= 两人在车站后面一个预先约定的地点碰了头。
a prearranged spot = 预先约定的地点
pre- 是「在……之前」。

prebuttal
/priˈbʌtl/
核心：（口语）预先反驳：政客在对手还没开口批评之前，抢先作出的回应。可数；由 pre- 和 rebuttal 拼成。
① The minister issued a prebuttal an hour before the report was published.
= 报告发表前一个钟头，部长就发了一份抢先的反驳。
a prebuttal = 预先的反驳
新闻、政治评论里的新词。
② The campaign team spent the morning preparing prebuttals to the rival's speech.
= 竞选班子一上午都在准备对对手演讲的预先反驳。
prepare a prebuttal = 准备预先的反驳

precancerous
/ˌpriːˈkænsərəs/
核心：（医学）癌前的：（细胞、病变）不处理就可能变成癌的。
① The test found precancerous cells that were removed the same week.
= 检查发现了癌前细胞，当周就切除了。
precancerous cells = 癌前细胞
a precancerous condition = 癌前病变
② The doctor explained that the growth was precancerous and should not be left.
= 医生解释说那个肿物是癌前病变，不能放着不管。
be precancerous = 是癌前的

precariat
/prɪˈkeəriət/
核心：不稳定无产者：没有固定工作、没有保障、靠零工和短期合同过活的阶层。单数；由 precarious 和 proletariat 拼成。
① The book describes a growing precariat of drivers and cleaners with no fixed hours.
= 那本书写的是一个日益壮大、由没有固定工时的司机和清洁工组成的不稳定阶层。
a growing precariat = 日益壮大的不稳定阶层
社会学的新词，带批评意味。
② Many graduates now join the precariat instead of finding a career.
= 许多大学毕业生如今找不到一份事业，沦入不稳定阶层。
join the precariat = 沦入不稳定阶层

precarious
/prɪˈkeəriəs/
核心：两支。一是（处境）不稳定的、没保障的、岌岌可危的；二是（位置）不稳固的、随时会掉、会倒的。
副词 precariously；名词 precariousness，不可数。并入本条。
① He earns a precarious living as a street musician.
= 他当街头乐手，过着朝不保夕的日子。
a precarious living = 朝不保夕的生计
a precarious existence = 朝不保夕的生活
② The ladder was in a precarious position on the edge of the roof.
= 梯子搁在屋檐边上，位置很悬。
a precarious position = 很悬的位置
也用来说处境。
③ The vase was balanced precariously on a pile of books.
= 那只花瓶摇摇欲坠地架在一摞书上。
precariously = 摇摇欲坠地
④ The strike showed the precariousness of the government's majority.
= 那场罢工显出政府的多数席位有多不牢靠。
precariousness = 不稳、岌岌可危

precast
/ˌpriːˈkɑːst/
核心：（混凝土构件）预制的：先在工厂里浇成一块块，再运到工地拼装。多作定语。
① The walls of the car park are made of precast concrete panels.
= 那座停车场的墙是预制混凝土板拼的。
precast concrete = 预制混凝土
cast 是「浇铸」。
② The bridge was built in a month from precast sections.
= 那座桥是用预制的构件一个月搭起来的。
precast sections = 预制构件

precaution
/prɪˈkɔːʃn/
核心：预防措施、防备：为免出事预先做的事。可数，多用复数：take precautions、as a precaution。
形容词 precautionary 是预防性的。并入本条。
① You should take sensible precautions against theft when you travel alone in a crowded city.
= 一个人在拥挤的城市里出行，应当采取合理的防盗措施。
take precautions against something = 采取防备某事的措施
take the precaution of doing something = 为保险起见做某事
② After the fall she was kept in hospital overnight as a precaution, although no bones were broken.
= 摔了那一跤以后，虽然没骨折，为了稳妥起见她还是被留院观察了一夜。
as a precaution = 为了稳妥起见
③ The school was closed for a day as a precautionary measure after two pupils fell ill.
= 两名学生病倒之后，作为预防措施，学校停课一天。
a precautionary measure = 预防措施

precede
/prɪˈsiːd/
核心：（正式）在……之前发生、出现；走在……前面；precede A with B 是在甲之前先来个乙。及物。
形容词 preceding 只作定语：在前的、前面的：the preceding chapter。并入本条。
① A long and uncomfortable silence preceded her answer to the lawyer's last question.
= 她回答律师最后一个问题之前，有一阵长长的、令人难受的沉默。
precede something = 在某事之前发生
反义是 follow。
② The mayor entered the crowded hall, preceded by two guards in red coats carrying silver staffs.
= 市长走进挤满人的大厅，前面有两名穿红外套、手持银杖的卫兵开路。
be preceded by somebody = 前面有某人走着
③ He preceded his long speech on taxes with a joke about the weather that nobody understood.
= 讲那篇关于税的长话之前，他先说了个谁也没听懂的天气笑话。
precede A with B = 在甲之前先来乙
④ The reasons for the change of plan were explained at length in the preceding chapter.
= 改变计划的原因在前一章已经详细讲过。
the preceding chapter = 前一章
别跟 proceed（继续进行）混。

precedence
/ˈpresɪdəns/
核心：优先、优先权：因为更重要而排在前面。不可数：take precedence over、give precedence to；in order of precedence 是按尊卑先后。
① The safety of the passengers takes precedence over everything else.
= 乘客的安全高于一切。
take precedence over something = 优先于某事
give precedence to something = 让某事优先
② The guests were seated in order of precedence.
= 客人按尊卑先后入座。
in order of precedence = 按尊卑先后

precedent
/ˈpresɪdənt/
核心：先例、前例：可以拿来当依据的、以前有过的事；（法律）判例。可数也可不可数：set a precedent、without precedent。
① The ruling sets a precedent for other cases of the same kind.
= 那项裁决为同类的其他案件立下了先例。
set a precedent = 立下先例
create a precedent = 开先例
② The floods were without precedent in the history of the town.
= 那场洪水在该镇历史上前所未有。
without precedent = 前所未有
形容词是 unprecedented。
③ There is no precedent for a president resigning in his first year.
= 总统上任头一年就辞职，没有先例。
a precedent for something = 某事的先例

precept
/ˈpriːsept/
核心：（正式）戒律、准则、箴言：教人怎样立身处世的一条规矩。可数也可不可数。
① The children were taught the precept that honesty is the best policy.
= 孩子们受的教训是：诚实为上。
a precept = 准则、箴言
书面词，日常说 rule、principle。
② He taught more by example than by precept.
= 他言传少，身教多。
by precept = 靠言传

precinct
/ˈpriːsɪŋkt/
核心：三支，可数。一是（英式）（城里专作某种用途的）区域：a shopping precinct 是步行购物区；二是（美式）警区、选区，也指警区的分局；
三是 precincts：（大教堂、学院等）围墙以内的地方、周围的场地，复数。
① The new shopping precinct has no cars and plenty of seats.
= 新建的步行购物区不通汽车，座位很多。
a shopping precinct = 步行购物区
a pedestrian precinct = 步行街区
② The detective had worked in the same precinct for twenty years.
= 那位侦探在同一个警区干了二十年。
a police precinct = 警区
③ Smoking is forbidden within the precincts of the hospital.
= 医院院内禁止吸烟。
the precincts of a place = 某处的院内

precious
/ˈpreʃəs/
核心：形容词：贵重的、值钱的：precious metals、precious stone 是宝石；宝贵的、珍贵的：因为稀少、要紧而不能浪费；珍爱的、心爱的；
（贬）矫揉造作的、过分讲究的；（口语，只作定语）表示不屑、恼火：your precious car。
副词：（口语）precious little、precious few 是少得可怜。名词：（口语）宝贝，用作称呼。
派生：preciousness、preciosity 是矫揉造作，后者是书面词。并入本条。
① The thieves took gold, silver and other precious metals from the safe.
= 窃贼从保险柜里拿走了金、银和别的贵金属。
precious metals = 贵金属
指金、银、铂这几种。
② The ring is set with a single precious stone.
= 那枚戒指镶着一颗宝石。
a precious stone = 宝石
③ Clean water is a precious resource in the desert.
= 在沙漠里，干净的水是宝贵的资源。
a precious resource = 宝贵的资源
precious time = 宝贵的时间
④ We wasted two precious hours waiting for the bus.
= 我们等公共汽车白白耗了两个钟头的宝贵时间。
precious hours = 宝贵的时间
⑤ The photographs are very precious to her, because they are all she has of her father.
= 那些照片对她非常珍贵，她父亲留下的就只有这些。
precious to somebody = 对某人珍贵
⑥ His poems are a little too precious for my taste.
= 他的诗对我的口味来说有点太矫情。
be precious = 矫揉造作
⑦ I never touched your precious car!
= 你那辆宝贝车我碰都没碰！
your precious car = 你那辆宝贝车
⑧ There is precious little evidence that the scheme has worked.
= 能证明那个方案奏效的证据少得可怜。
precious little = 少得可怜
这里是副词，只跟 little、few 连用。
⑨ Precious few of the passengers knew how to open the emergency door.
= 知道怎么开紧急出口的乘客没几个。
precious few = 没几个
⑩ Critics complained about the preciousness of the dialogue.
= 评论家抱怨对白太矫情。
preciousness = 矫揉造作
preciosity = 矫揉造作

precipice
/ˈpresəpɪs/
核心：悬崖、峭壁：山、岩石极陡的一面。可数；比喻危急的关头：on the edge of a precipice。
① The path runs along the edge of a precipice with no fence.
= 那条小路沿着一道没有护栏的悬崖边走。
a precipice = 悬崖
比 cliff 更强调陡、险。
② The country stood on the edge of a precipice in the last days of the crisis.
= 危机的最后几天，那个国家站在悬崖边上。
on the edge of a precipice = 在悬崖边上、危在旦夕

precipitate
/prɪˈsɪpɪteɪt/（动）/prɪˈsɪpɪtət/（形、名）
核心：动词：（正式）使（坏事）突然发生、加速（危机）的到来；precipitate somebody、something into 是使……猛然陷入；（化学）使沉淀。
形容词：（正式）仓促的、鲁莽的。副词 precipitately。名词：（化学）沉淀物，可数。
① The minister's resignation precipitated a crisis in the government.
= 那位部长的辞职引发了政府的一场危机。
precipitate a crisis = 引发危机
宾语多是坏事。
② The murder precipitated the country into civil war.
= 那起谋杀让国家猛然陷入内战。
precipitate something into something = 使某物猛然陷入某种状态
③ We must not take any precipitate action before the facts are known.
= 事实弄清之前，我们不可贸然行动。
precipitate action = 贸然的行动
形容词末音节读 /ət/。
④ He left the meeting precipitately and did not come back.
= 他匆匆离开了会场，没再回来。
precipitately = 仓促地
⑤ A white precipitate formed at the bottom of the tube.
= 试管底部形成了白色沉淀。
a precipitate = 沉淀物

precipitation
/prɪˌsɪpɪˈteɪʃn/
核心：三支，不可数。一是（气象）降水：雨、雪、冰雹的总称，也指降水量；二是（化学）沉淀；三是（正式）仓促、鲁莽。
① The region has an annual precipitation of less than ten centimetres.
= 那个地区的年降水量不到十厘米。
annual precipitation = 年降水量
气象预报的术语，日常说 rain、snow。
② Precipitation will fall as snow on the hills tonight.
= 今晚山上的降水将以雪的形式落下。
precipitation = 降水
③ He acted with a precipitation that he later regretted.
= 他当时做得很仓促，后来后悔了。
with precipitation = 仓促地

precipitous
/prɪˈsɪpɪtəs/
核心：（正式）三支。一是险峻的、陡峭的；二是（变化）急剧的、骤然的；三是仓促的、贸然的。副词 precipitously，并入本条。
① The castle stands on a precipitous rock above the river.
= 那座城堡耸立在河上方一块险峻的岩石上。
a precipitous rock = 险峻的岩石
从 precipice 来。
② There has been a precipitous fall in the number of visitors.
= 游客人数急剧下降。
a precipitous fall = 急剧的下降
③ The land drops precipitously to the sea on the western side.
= 西面的地势陡然落向大海。
precipitously = 陡然地、急剧地

precis
/ˈpreɪsiː/
核心：摘要、梗概：把一篇文章、一次讲话的要点压成短短的一段。可数，复数不变，读作 /ˈpreɪsiːz/；也写作 précis。动词：写……的摘要。
① The pupils had to write a precis of the article in a hundred words.
= 学生们得用一百个词写出那篇文章的摘要。
a precis of something = 某物的摘要
② She precised the report for the minister in a single page.
= 她把那份报告给部长压成了一页摘要。
precis a report = 给报告写摘要
来自法语，末尾的 s 不发音。

precise
/prɪˈsaɪs/
核心：三支。一是准确的、精确的；二是恰好的、正是那个的，只作定语：at that precise moment；三是（人）严谨的、一丝不苟的。
to be precise 是确切地说。副词 precisely：精确地；恰恰、正是；用来表示完全同意：Precisely.
① Can you give me a more precise idea of the cost before I speak to the bank?
= 我跟银行谈之前，你能就花费给我个更确切的数吗？
a precise idea = 确切的概念
② At that precise moment the telephone rang, and the cat jumped off the table in fright.
= 就在那一刻，电话响了，猫吓得从桌上跳了下去。
at that precise moment = 就在那一刻
③ He is very precise in everything he does, from folding his shirts to filling in his tax form.
= 他做什么都一丝不苟，从叠衬衫到填税表。
be precise = 严谨、一丝不苟
④ There were about forty people there, or thirty-eight to be precise.
= 当时大约有四十人，确切地说是三十八人。
to be precise = 确切地说
⑤ The meeting will begin at two o'clock precisely, and the doors will be closed at five past.
= 会议两点整开始，两点零五分关门。
at two o'clock precisely = 两点整
⑥ That is precisely what I have been trying to tell you for the last half hour.
= 这半个钟头我一直想跟你说的正是这个。
precisely what = 正是……的
⑦ When I asked whether the bank had refused the loan after all, she answered, "Precisely."
= 我问银行到头来是不是拒了那笔贷款，她答道：「正是。」
Precisely. = 正是。

precision
/prɪˈsɪʒn/
核心：精确、准确、精密。不可数：with precision；常作定语：precision instruments 是精密仪器。
① The surgeon cut with great precision round the nerve that controls the movement of the hand.
= 那位外科医生十分精准地绕着控制手部活动的神经下刀。
with precision = 精准地
② The small factory outside the town makes precision instruments for hospitals all over Europe.
= 城外的那家小工厂给全欧洲的医院生产精密仪器。
precision instruments = 精密仪器
③ The forty dancers moved across the stage with military precision and never once collided.
= 四十名舞者像军人一样整齐划一地穿过舞台，一次也没撞上。
military precision = 军人般的整齐划一

preclude
/prɪˈkluːd/
核心：（正式）排除、妨碍、使不可能：preclude something、preclude somebody from doing。及物。
① Lack of time precludes any further discussion.
= 时间不够，没法再往下讨论。
preclude something = 使某事不可能
② His injury precluded him from playing for the rest of the season.
= 他的伤让他这个赛季余下的比赛都打不了了。
preclude somebody from doing something = 使某人不能做某事

precocious
/prɪˈkəʊʃəs/
核心：（孩子）早熟的、早慧的：某些本事发展得比同龄人早；有时含贬义：人小鬼大、老成得让人不舒服。
副词 precociously；名词 precocity，不可数，也说 precociousness。并入本条。
① She was a precocious child who could read at the age of three.
= 她是个早慧的孩子，三岁就能阅读。
a precocious child = 早慧的孩子
② He showed a precocious talent for the violin and gave his first concert at the age of nine.
= 他早早显出了拉小提琴的才能，九岁就开了头一场音乐会。
a precocious talent = 早露的才能
③ The boy was precociously clever and corrected his teachers' spelling in front of the whole class.
= 那男孩聪明得早，会当着全班的面纠正老师的拼写。
precociously = 早熟地
④ The precocity of the young composer astonished the court.
= 那位年轻作曲家的早慧让宫廷上下惊叹。
precocity = 早熟、早慧

precognition
/ˌpriːkɒɡˈnɪʃn/
核心：（正式）预知、先知：事情发生之前就知道它会发生的本事，科学解释不了。不可数。
① She claimed to have had a moment of precognition in the car a minute before the accident.
= 她声称出事前一分钟在车里有过一瞬间的预知。
a moment of precognition = 一瞬间的预知
② No carefully controlled experiment has ever shown that precognition exists outside stories and films.
= 没有哪个严格控制的实验证明过，预知在故事和电影之外确实存在。
precognition = 预知

preconceived
/ˌpriːkənˈsiːvd/
核心：（看法、想法）事先形成的、先入为主的：还没了解实情就有了的。只作定语。名词 preconception 是先入之见、成见，可数，多用复数。并入本条。
① She arrived in the village with no preconceived ideas about country people.
= 她来到那个村子的时候，对乡下人没有先入为主的看法。
preconceived ideas = 先入为主的看法
pre- 是「预先」，conceive 是「形成想法」。
② The jury was told to put aside any preconceived notions about the accused.
= 陪审团被告知要抛开对被告的一切成见。
preconceived notions = 成见
③ The film challenges our preconceptions about life in a small prison.
= 那部片子挑战了我们对小监狱里生活的先入之见。
preconceptions about something = 对某事的先入之见
a preconception = 一个先入之见

precondition
/ˌpriːkənˈdɪʃn/
核心：先决条件、前提：一件事要发生，先得满足的条件，介词用 for、of。可数。
① A ceasefire is a precondition for any talks between the two sides.
= 停火是双方进行任何会谈的先决条件。
a precondition for something = 某事的先决条件
谈判、合同里常见。
② The bank made the sale of the old house a precondition of the loan.
= 银行把卖掉老房子定为放贷的前提。
a precondition of something = 某事的前提
也说 prerequisite。

preconscious
/ˌpriːˈkɒnʃəs/
核心：（心理学）前意识的：眼下不在意识里、一想就能想起来的（念头、记忆）。也作名词：the preconscious 是前意识，单数。
① The name was stored in his preconscious mind and came back as soon as he saw her face.
= 那个名字存在他的前意识里，一见她的脸就想了起来。
the preconscious mind = 前意识
介于 conscious（意识）和 unconscious（无意识）之间。
② The lecture explained the difference between preconscious memories and those that are truly buried.
= 那场讲座讲了前意识的记忆跟真正被埋起来的记忆有什么不同。
preconscious memories = 前意识的记忆

precooked
/ˌpriːˈkʊkt/
核心：预先煮熟的：事先做好、吃的时候只要热一热的。
① The canteen serves precooked meals that are heated up in a microwave.
= 那家食堂供应预先做好、用微波炉加热的饭菜。
precooked meals = 预制的熟食
也写作 pre-cooked。
② Use precooked rice if you have only ten minutes to make the dish.
= 要是只有十分钟做这道菜，就用预先煮熟的米饭。
precooked rice = 预先煮熟的米饭

precursor
/priˈkɜːsə(r)/
核心：（正式）先驱、前身、先兆：在同类的人、事物出现之前就有的、为它开了路的那一个，介词用 of、to。可数。
① The machine was a precursor of the modern computer.
= 那台机器是现代计算机的前身。
a precursor of something = 某物的前身
字面是「跑在前面的」。
② The small strikes in the spring were a precursor to the unrest of the summer.
= 春天的那几次小罢工是夏天动荡的先兆。
a precursor to something = 某事的先兆
说事件时用 to 更多。

predate
/ˌpriːˈdeɪt/
核心：（在时间上）早于、先于：比……更早出现、建造。及物。反义是 postdate。
① The church predates the castle by at least two hundred years.
= 那座教堂比城堡至少早两百年。
predate something = 早于某物
也写作 pre-date，跟动物的捕食无关。
② Some of the customs of the village predate the arrival of the Romans.
= 那个村子的一些习俗比罗马人到来还早。
predate the Romans = 早于罗马人到来

predator
/ˈpredətə(r)/
核心：两支，可数。一是捕食性动物、食肉动物：靠捕杀别的动物为食的；二是（贬）掠夺者：利用别人的弱点为自己谋利、害人的人、公司。
派生：predatory 是捕食性的、掠夺成性的；predation 是（术语）捕食，不可数。
predatory pricing 是掠夺性定价：故意压低价钱把对手挤垮；predatory lending 是掠夺性放贷：以苛刻条件借钱给还不起的人。并入本条。
① The young birds are safe from predators on the cliff.
= 雏鸟在悬崖上不怕捕食的动物。
a predator = 捕食性动物
被它捕食的叫 prey。
② The firm was bought by a corporate predator who sold its factories one by one.
= 那家公司被一个企业掠夺者买下，工厂被一家一家地卖掉。
a corporate predator = 企业掠夺者
a sexual predator = 性侵惯犯
③ The owl is a predatory bird that hunts at night.
= 猫头鹰是夜里捕猎的猛禽。
a predatory bird = 猛禽
也说 a bird of prey。
④ Predation by foxes has reduced the number of rabbits on the island.
= 狐狸的捕食让岛上的兔子少了。
predation = 捕食
⑤ The airline was fined for predatory pricing on the routes of its smaller rival.
= 那家航空公司因为在小对手的航线上搞掠夺性定价被罚了款。
predatory pricing = 掠夺性定价
⑥ The new law is meant to protect poor families from predatory lending.
= 新法律是为了保护穷苦人家不受掠夺性放贷之害。
predatory lending = 掠夺性放贷

predawn
/ˌpriːˈdɔːn/
核心：黎明前的。只作定语；美式用得多。
① The fishermen set out in the predawn darkness and were back by noon.
= 渔民们在黎明前的黑暗里出发，中午就回来了。
the predawn darkness = 黎明前的黑暗
英式多说 before dawn。
② The police arrested four men in a predawn raid on the flat.
= 警察在黎明前突袭那套公寓，抓了四个人。
a predawn raid = 黎明前的突袭

predecease
/ˌpriːdɪˈsiːs/
核心：（法律，正式）先于……死去。及物。
① The will says that the house should go to his nephew if his wife predeceases him.
= 遗嘱里写明，妻子万一先他而去，房子就归他侄子。
predecease somebody = 先于某人死去
遗嘱、法律文书用语。
② She had no children, and both her brothers predeceased her, so the farm passed to a distant cousin.
= 她没有孩子，两个兄弟又都先她去世，农场就落到了一个远房表亲手里。
predeceased her = 先她去世

predecessor
/ˈpriːdɪsesə(r)/
核心：两支，可数。一是前任：在某人之前担任同一职位的人；二是（被取代的）前一个东西、前身。反义是 successor。
① The new manager is far more popular than her predecessor.
= 新经理比她的前任受欢迎得多。
somebody's predecessor = 某人的前任
接任的人叫 successor。
② The latest model is lighter and cheaper than its predecessor.
= 最新的型号比前一代轻，也便宜。
its predecessor = 它的前身

predestination
/ˌpriːdestɪˈneɪʃn/
核心：（宗教）宿命论、预定论：认为一切、包括谁得救谁沉沦，都由神早早定下。不可数。
形容词 predestined 是命中注定的：predestined to do。并入本条。
① The preacher believed firmly in the doctrine of predestination.
= 那位传道人坚信预定论的教义。
the doctrine of predestination = 预定论的教义
pre- 加 destine「注定」。
② The two reformers quarrelled bitterly about predestination and free will.
= 那两位改革家为预定论和自由意志吵得很凶。
predestination = 预定论
③ It seemed that the two friends were predestined to become rivals.
= 那两个朋友好像命中注定要成为对手。
predestined to do something = 命中注定要做某事
日常话里不带宗教意味。

predetermine
/ˌpriːdɪˈtɜːmɪn/
核心：（正式）预先决定、事先安排：让一件事的结果早早定下。及物，多用被动。分词 predetermined 作形容词：预先定好的。并入本条。
① The sex of the child is predetermined at the moment of conception.
= 孩子的性别在受孕的那一刻就定下了。
be predetermined = 预先定下
多用被动。
② Critics say that the result of the inquiry was predetermined by the choice of its chairman.
= 批评的人说，选谁当主席，那场调查的结果就已经定了。
be predetermined by something = 由某事预先决定
③ The guards changed places at a predetermined signal.
= 一个预先定好的信号一发，卫兵们就换了岗。
a predetermined signal = 预先定好的信号
a predetermined time = 预先定好的时间

predeterminer
/ˌpriːdɪˈtɜːmɪnə(r)/
核心：（语法）前位限定词：放在冠词等限定词前面的词，如 all the books 里的 all、both my hands 里的 both。可数。
① In "half the cake", the word "half" is a predeterminer.
= 在「half the cake」里，「half」是前位限定词。
a predeterminer = 前位限定词
常见的有 all、both、half、such、what。
② The exercise asks pupils to underline every predeterminer in the paragraph.
= 那道练习要学生把段落里的前位限定词都划出来。
every predeterminer = 每个前位限定词

predicament
/prɪˈdɪkəmənt/
核心：困境、窘境：尴尬、难办、不知如何是好的处境。可数。
① We found ourselves in an awkward predicament when the last bus had gone.
= 末班车一走，我们就陷入了尴尬的境地。
in a predicament = 身处困境
比 difficulty 多一层「左右为难」。
② The loss of the key left him in a real predicament.
= 钥匙一丢，他真是进退两难。
a real predicament = 真正的困境

predicate
/ˈpredɪkət/（名）/ˈpredɪkeɪt/（动）
核心：名词：（语法）谓语：句子里说明主语怎么样、做什么的那部分，包括动词，可数。
动词：（正式）be predicated on 是以……为基础、取决于。
语法上的 predicative 是表语的、作表语用的：（形容词）放在 be、seem 等动词后面，跟 attributive（作定语的）相对；副词 predicatively。并入本条。
① In "The dog barked loudly", the predicate is "barked loudly".
= 在「The dog barked loudly」里，谓语是「barked loudly」。
the predicate = 谓语
名词末音节读 /ət/，动词读 /eɪt/。
② The whole plan is predicated on the assumption that prices will rise.
= 整个计划建立在物价会上涨的假定上。
be predicated on something = 以某事为基础
③ "Asleep" is a predicative adjective and cannot be used before a noun.
= 「asleep」是只作表语的形容词，不能用在名词前。
a predicative adjective = 作表语的形容词
afraid、alive、asleep、alone 都是这一类。
④ The word "afraid" is nearly always used predicatively.
= 「afraid」这个词几乎总是作表语用。
predicatively = 作表语地

predict
/prɪˈdɪkt/
核心：预言、预测、预报：说出将来会发生什么：predict something、predict that、predict what。及物。
派生：predictive 是预测的、有预测力的：predictive text 是（手机的）联想输入；predictor 是预示某事的东西、指标。并入本条。
① Nobody could have predicted the result of the election.
= 那场选举的结果谁也预料不到。
predict the result = 预测结果
字面是「先说出来」。
② The report predicts that sales will double within five years.
= 那份报告预测销量五年内翻一番。
predict that = 预测
③ It is impossible to predict what the weather will do in the mountains.
= 山里的天气会怎样，没法预料。
predict what = 预测……会怎样
④ The phone's predictive text changed "home" into "good".
= 手机的联想输入把「home」改成了「good」。
predictive text = 联想输入
这一支是手机上的用法。
⑤ A child's vocabulary at five is a good predictor of success at school.
= 孩子五岁时的词汇量很能预示日后学业的好坏。
a predictor of something = 预示某事的指标

predictable
/prɪˈdɪktəbl/
核心：两支。一是可以预料的、料得到的；二是（贬）老一套的、毫无新意的。反义是 unpredictable。
副词 predictably；名词 predictability，不可数。并入本条。
① The result of the vote was entirely predictable.
= 表决的结果完全在意料之中。
be predictable = 在意料之中
entirely predictable = 完全在意料之中
② The film has a predictable ending in which the hero gets the girl.
= 那部片子的结尾毫无新意：主人公抱得美人归。
a predictable ending = 毫无新意的结尾
这一支带贬义。
③ Predictably, the trains were late on the first day of snow.
= 不出所料，下雪的头一天火车晚点了。
predictably = 不出所料
④ Children need a certain predictability in their daily lives.
= 孩子的日常生活需要有一定的规律可循。
predictability = 可预见性、有规律

prediction
/prɪˈdɪkʃn/
核心：预言、预测、预报。可数也可不可数：make a prediction、a prediction about、of。
① The weatherman made a prediction of heavy snow for the weekend.
= 气象员预报周末有大雪。
make a prediction = 作出预测
a prediction of something = 对某事的预报
② Her predictions about the price of oil turned out to be correct.
= 她对油价的预测后来证明是对的。
predictions about something = 对某事的预测

predigested
/ˌpriːdaɪˈdʒestɪd/
核心：（贬）（信息、材料）被人事先嚼碎、简化过的：容易懂，却不用自己动脑。
① The textbook offers predigested summaries instead of the original documents.
= 那本教科书给的是嚼碎了的摘要，不是原始文献。
predigested summaries = 嚼碎了的摘要
本义是替人先消化过的食物。
② Viewers are fed predigested opinions by the evening news.
= 晚间新闻把现成的观点喂给观众。
predigested opinions = 现成的观点

predilection
/ˌpriːdɪˈlekʃn/
核心：（正式）偏爱、嗜好：对某样东西特别喜欢，介词用 for。可数，多用单数。
① He has a predilection for long words that nobody else understands.
= 他偏爱别人都听不懂的大词。
a predilection for something = 对某物的偏爱
书面词，日常说 liking、fondness。
② Her predilection for bright colours shows in every room of the house.
= 她对鲜艳颜色的偏爱，房子的每个房间里都看得出来。
somebody's predilection for something = 某人对某物的偏爱

predispose
/ˌpriːdɪˈspəʊz/
核心：（正式）两支，及物。一是使……预先倾向于（某种看法、做法）：predispose somebody to、towards；二是使易患（某种病）：predispose somebody to。
名词 predisposition：倾向、（患病的）易感体质，可数也可不可数。并入本条。
① His childhood in the mountains predisposed him to like quiet places.
= 在山里度过的童年让他天生就喜欢安静的地方。
predispose somebody to do something = 使某人倾向于做某事
主语多是经历、出身。
② Smoking predisposes people to infections of the chest.
= 吸烟让人容易得胸部感染。
predispose somebody to something = 使某人易患某病
③ Some families have a genetic predisposition to heart disease.
= 有些家族有易患心脏病的遗传体质。
a predisposition to something = 易患某病的体质
a genetic predisposition = 遗传倾向

predominant
/prɪˈdɒmɪnənt/
核心：两支。一是最显著的、最突出的；二是占主导地位的、占优势的。副词 predominantly 是主要地、绝大多数；名词 predominance，不可数或单数。并入本条。
① Blue is the predominant colour in all the paintings that she made during her years by the sea.
= 她住在海边那些年画的画里，蓝色都是主色。
the predominant colour = 主色
比 main 正式。
② Farming is still the predominant way of life in the valley.
= 务农仍是那个山谷里最主要的生活方式。
the predominant way of life = 最主要的生活方式
③ The audience at the concert was predominantly female and under thirty, with a few puzzled fathers.
= 那场音乐会的观众绝大多数是三十岁以下的女性，夹着几个摸不着头脑的父亲。
predominantly = 绝大多数、主要地
④ The predominance of English on the Internet worries some governments.
= 英语在互联网上占主导，让一些国家的政府不安。
the predominance of something = 某物的主导地位

predominate
/prɪˈdɒmɪneɪt/
核心：（正式）两支，不及物。一是（在数量上）占多数、居多；二是占上风、起主导作用：predominate over。
副词 predominately 同 predominantly。并入本条。
① Pine trees predominate in the cold forests of the north, where few other trees survive the winter.
= 北方寒冷的森林里松树居多，别的树没几种熬得过冬天。
predominate = 居多
是动词；形容词是 predominant。
② In the end, common sense predominated over fear, and the villagers agreed to let the doctors in.
= 最后理智压倒了恐惧，村民们同意让医生进村。
predominate over something = 压倒某物
③ The staff of the village school is predominately local, and most of the teachers were once pupils there.
= 那所乡村学校的教职员大多是本地人，多数教师当年就是那儿的学生。
predominately = 大多、主要地

preemie
/ˈpriːmi/
核心：（美式口语）早产儿。可数；也拼作 preemy；premature baby 的昵称。
① Their son was a preemie who spent his first six weeks in hospital.
= 他们的儿子是早产儿，头六个星期是在医院过的。
a preemie = 早产儿
英式口语没有对应的简称，说 premature baby。
② The nurses on the ward care for preemies who weigh less than a bag of sugar.
= 那个病房的护士照料的是体重还不到一袋糖的早产儿。
care for preemies = 照料早产儿

preen
/priːn/
核心：动词：（鸟）用嘴整理（羽毛）；（贬）（人）精心打扮、对着镜子臭美：preen yourself；沾沾自喜：preen yourself on。
① The swan sat on the bank preening its feathers in the sun.
= 天鹅蹲在岸上，在阳光里用嘴梳理羽毛。
preen its feathers = 梳理羽毛
说人是从鸟的动作引申来的。
② He spends half an hour preening himself in front of the mirror every morning.
= 他每天早上要在镜子前臭美半个钟头。
preen yourself = 精心打扮
③ The minister preened himself on the success of a plan that was not his own.
= 那位部长为一个并不是他自己的计划的成功沾沾自喜。
preen yourself on something = 为某事沾沾自喜

prefab
/ˈpriːfæb/
核心：（口语）预制装配式房屋：墙板等在工厂做好、运到现场拼起来的简易房，英国战后盖过许多。可数。
形容词 prefabricated 是预制的、装配式的；名词 prefabrication，不可数。并入本条。
① Her grandparents lived in a prefab for twenty years after the war.
= 战后，她的祖父母在一座装配式简易房里住了二十年。
a prefab = 装配式简易房
是 prefabricated building 的截短。
② The classrooms are prefabricated huts put up in a single weekend.
= 那些教室是一个周末就搭起来的预制板房。
prefabricated = 预制的、装配式的
③ Prefabrication allowed the firm to build the bridge in half the usual time.
= 预制拼装让那家公司用一半的时间建成了桥。
prefabrication = 预制拼装

preface
/ˈprefəs/
核心：名词：序言、前言：书的正文之前作者写的说明，可数。
动词：给……作序；在（讲话）前面先说、先做：preface A with B、by doing。形容词 prefatory 是序言的、开场的，只作定语。并入本条。
① In the preface the author thanks the villagers who told her their stories.
= 作者在序言里感谢给她讲故事的村民。
the preface = 序言
多是作者自己写的；别人写的叫 foreword。
② He prefaced his remarks with an apology for arriving late.
= 他先为迟到道了个歉，才开始讲话。
preface A with B = 在甲之前先来乙
③ After a few prefatory words from the chairman the debate began.
= 主席说了几句开场白，辩论就开始了。
prefatory = 序言的、开场的
读音是 /ˈprefəs/，不读 face。

prefect
/ˈpriːfekt/
核心：两支，可数。一是（英国一些学校里的）级长、学长：有权管低年级学生的高年级学生；
二是（法国、意大利、日本等国的）省长、地方行政长官。
① As a prefect she had to keep order in the corridors at lunchtime.
= 身为级长，她得在午饭时间维持走廊的秩序。
a prefect = 级长
② The prefect of the region ordered the roads to be closed.
= 那个大区的行政长官下令封路。
the prefect of a region = 大区的行政长官

prefecture
/ˈpriːfektʃə(r)/
核心：（法国、意大利、日本等国的）省、县、府：一级地方行政区；也指这级长官的官署。可数。
① The earthquake struck three prefectures in the north of Japan.
= 那场地震袭击了日本北部的三个县。
a prefecture = 县、省
② You must apply for the permit at the prefecture in the centre of the town.
= 那种许可证得到镇中心的省政府去申请。
at the prefecture = 在省政府

prefer
/prɪˈfɜː(r)/
核心：更喜欢、宁愿：两样里更中意其中一样。及物，不用进行时；过去式 preferred。
句型：prefer A to B；prefer to do、prefer doing；prefer somebody to do；would prefer to do；would prefer it if。
（法律）prefer charges against 是对……提出指控。
① I prefer a pot of weak tea to strong coffee first thing in the morning.
= 一大早，比起浓咖啡我更爱喝一壶淡茶。
prefer A to B = 比起乙更喜欢甲
介词用 to，不用 than。
② She prefers to work at night when the house is quiet.
= 她宁愿夜里家里安静的时候干活。
prefer to do something = 宁愿做某事
③ He prefers walking the two miles to the office to taking the crowded bus.
= 那两英里路，比起挤公共汽车，他更愿意走着去办公室。
prefer doing A to doing B = 比起做乙更愿意做甲
④ I would prefer you not to mention this to anyone in the office until the contract is signed.
= 合同签下来之前，我希望你别跟办公室里任何人提这件事。
would prefer somebody not to do something = 希望某人别做某事
⑤ The builder said that he would prefer it if we paid in cash at the end of each week.
= 建筑工说，我们要是每到周末付现金，他会更乐意。
would prefer it if = 希望
⑥ The police decided not to prefer charges against the boy.
= 警方决定不对那男孩提出指控。
prefer charges = 提出指控

preferable
/ˈprefrəbl/
核心：更可取的、更合意的：比别的好、更合适。不用在名词前；介词用 to，不说 more preferable。副词 preferably 是最好、更可取地。并入本条。
① Anything is preferable to another night in that hotel.
= 什么都比在那家旅馆再住一夜强。
preferable to something = 比某事可取
② It would be preferable to settle the matter without going to court.
= 不上法庭把事情了结更可取。
it would be preferable to do something = 做某事更可取
③ We are looking for a new house, preferably one near the school.
= 我们在找新房子，最好离学校近。
preferably = 最好
重音在第一个音节。

preference
/ˈprefrəns/
核心：三支。一是偏爱、更喜欢：a preference for，可数也可不可数；二是偏爱的东西、更中意的那个，可数；
三是优先、优待，不可数：give preference to。in preference to 是优先于、而不选。
① She has a strong preference for quiet restaurants with few tables and no music.
= 她格外偏爱桌子不多、不放音乐的安静餐馆。
a preference for something = 对某物的偏爱
② The cook can do either fish or chicken tonight, so tell me what your preference is.
= 厨子今晚做鱼做鸡都行，告诉我你更想要哪个。
your preference = 你更中意的
③ The school gives preference to children who live in the village.
= 那所学校优先收住在本村的孩子。
give preference to somebody = 优先照顾某人
④ He chose the slow train along the coast in preference to the faster motorway bus.
= 他宁选沿海岸走的慢车，没坐更快的高速大巴。
in preference to something = 优先于某物

preferential
/ˌprefəˈrenʃl/
核心：优先的、优惠的、优待的。只作定语。副词 preferentially，并入本条。
① Members of the club receive preferential treatment when tickets go on sale.
= 开始售票时，俱乐部会员享有优先待遇。
preferential treatment = 优先待遇、优待
② The bank offers a preferential rate of interest to students.
= 那家银行给学生优惠利率。
a preferential rate = 优惠利率
③ The drug is taken up preferentially by the cells of the liver.
= 那种药优先被肝细胞吸收。
preferentially = 优先地

preferment
/prɪˈfɜːmənt/
核心：（正式）提升、晋升：尤指在教会、政府里升到更高的职位。不可数。
① The young priest had little hope of preferment in so poor a parish.
= 在那么穷的一个堂区，那位年轻神父没多少晋升的指望。
hope of preferment = 晋升的指望
② He owed his preferment at court to the favour of the queen.
= 他在宫廷里得到提拔，靠的是王后的青睐。
preferment at court = 在宫廷里得到提拔

prefigure
/ˌpriːˈfɪɡə(r)/
核心：（正式）预示、预兆：早早地显出后来才出现的事物的样子。及物。
① The small strike of 1905 prefigured the upheaval that came twelve years later.
= 一九〇五年的那次小罢工预示了十二年后的大动荡。
prefigure something = 预示某事
② Her early sketches prefigure the great paintings of her last years.
= 她早期的速写预示了晚年的那些杰作。
prefigure the paintings = 预示那些画作

prefix
/ˈpriːfɪks/
核心：名词，可数：（语法）前缀：加在词头改变意思的字母组合，如 un-、pre-；（号码、名称前的）前置数字、字母；（旧）（人名前的）称谓，如 Dr。
动词：给……加上前缀、在……前面加上：prefix A to B。
① Young children soon learn that the prefix "un-" turns a word like "happy" into its opposite.
= 小孩子很快就学会，前缀「un-」能把「happy」这样的词变成反义词。
a prefix = 前缀
加在词尾的叫 suffix。
② All telephone numbers in the capital have the prefix 01, which you can leave out inside the city.
= 首都的电话号码都以 01 打头，在市内拨可以省掉。
the prefix 01 = 打头的 01
③ From abroad you must dial the country code prefixed to the number on the card.
= 从国外打，得把国家代码加在名片上那个号码的前面拨。
prefix A to B = 把甲加在乙前面

pregnant
/ˈpreɡnənt/
核心：两支。一是怀孕的、妊娠的：be pregnant with 加孩子，get pregnant 是怀上；二是（正式）意味深长的、饱含（意义、感情）的：a pregnant pause；pregnant with。
名词 pregnancy 是怀孕、妊娠期，可数也可不可数；英式口语的 preggers 是怀孕的，带玩笑。并入本条。
① She is five months pregnant with her second child and still cycles to work every day.
= 她怀着第二个孩子，五个月了，还天天骑车上班。
five months pregnant = 怀孕五个月
② My sister got pregnant a year after the wedding and gave up her job at the bank.
= 我姐姐婚后一年怀上了，辞了银行的工作。
get pregnant = 怀上
③ There was a pregnant pause before he answered the question.
= 他回答那个问题之前，有一阵意味深长的停顿。
a pregnant pause = 意味深长的停顿
④ After the will was read, the silence in the lawyer's office was pregnant with meaning.
= 遗嘱念完，律师办公室里的沉默饱含深意。
pregnant with meaning = 饱含深意
⑤ She gave up smoking during her first pregnancy and has never touched a cigarette since.
= 她怀头一胎时戒了烟，从此再没碰过香烟。
a pregnancy = 一次怀孕
⑥ "Did you know that Anna is preggers again?" said her aunt.
= 她姑妈说：「你知道安娜又有了吗？」
preggers = 怀孕的（口语）

preheat
/ˌpriːˈhiːt/
核心：预热（烤箱）：放食物进去之前先把它烧到规定的温度。及物。
① Preheat the oven to two hundred degrees before you begin to mix the cake.
= 动手拌蛋糕糊之前，先把烤箱预热到两百度。
preheat the oven = 预热烤箱
② She forgot to preheat the grill, and the toast came out pale.
= 她忘了预热烤架，吐司烤出来颜色很浅。
preheat the grill = 预热烤架
菜谱里的固定说法。

prehensile
/prɪˈhensaɪl/
核心：（术语）（动物的尾巴、脚、鼻子）能抓握的、能缠住东西的。
① The monkey hung from the branch by its prehensile tail.
= 那只猴子用能缠握的尾巴吊在树枝上。
a prehensile tail = 能缠握的尾巴
② An elephant's trunk is a prehensile organ that can pick up a single nut.
= 大象的鼻子是能抓握的器官，连一颗坚果都拈得起来。
a prehensile organ = 能抓握的器官
来自拉丁语「抓」。

prehistoric
/ˌpriːhɪˈstɒrɪk/
核心：两支。一是史前的：有文字记载以前的；二是（口语，玩笑）老掉牙的、过时得可笑的。
名词 prehistory 是史前时期、史前史，不可数；也指（一件事的）前史。并入本条。
① The cave is famous for its prehistoric paintings of deer and horses.
= 那个洞穴以画着鹿和马的史前壁画出名。
prehistoric paintings = 史前壁画
② The office still uses a prehistoric computer that takes ten minutes to start.
= 那间办公室还在用一台开机要十分钟的老掉牙电脑。
a prehistoric computer = 老掉牙的电脑
③ The museum tells the story of the island from prehistory to the present day.
= 那家博物馆讲述那座岛从史前到今天的历史。
prehistory = 史前时期

prejudge
/ˌpriːˈdʒʌdʒ/
核心：（正式）过早地判断、未了解全部事实就下结论。及物。
① We should not prejudge the result of the inquiry before the witnesses have spoken.
= 证人还没说话，我们不该预先给调查下结论。
prejudge the result = 预先下结论
② The newspapers had prejudged the case and called the man a killer.
= 报纸早早给那桩案子定了性，把那人叫作凶手。
prejudge a case = 预先给案子定性

prejudice
/ˈpredʒudɪs/
核心：名词：偏见、成见：没有道理的不喜欢，尤指对某个种族、宗教、性别的，介词用 against，可数也可不可数。
动词：使有偏见：prejudice A against B；（正式）损害、不利于（某人的权益、机会）。
without prejudice 是（法律）不损害（合法权利）地。
派生：prejudiced 是有偏见的；prejudicial 是（正式）有损害的、不利的：prejudicial to。并入本条。
① There is still a great deal of prejudice against older workers.
= 对年纪大的员工仍有很深的偏见。
prejudice against somebody = 对某人的偏见
② The book set out to challenge the prejudices of its readers.
= 那本书的本意是挑战读者的种种成见。
prejudices = 成见
③ The newspaper reports had prejudiced the jury against the accused.
= 报纸的报道让陪审团对被告有了成见。
prejudice A against B = 使甲对乙有成见
④ Any delay could prejudice the child's chances of recovery.
= 任何拖延都可能损害孩子康复的机会。
prejudice somebody's chances = 损害某人的机会
⑤ The offer is made without prejudice to our client's rights.
= 提出这项提议，不损害我方当事人的权利。
without prejudice = 不损害合法权利地
⑥ He is deeply prejudiced against anyone with a foreign accent.
= 他对口音带外国腔的人成见很深。
prejudiced against somebody = 对某人有偏见
⑦ The judge ruled that the evidence was prejudicial to a fair trial.
= 法官裁定那项证据有碍公正审判。
prejudicial to something = 有损于某事

prelate
/ˈprelət/
核心：（正式）高级教士：主教、大主教、枢机主教这一级的神职人员。可数。
① The prelates walked in procession to the cathedral in their red robes.
= 高级教士们身穿红袍，列队走向大教堂。
a prelate = 高级教士
② The king quarrelled with the most powerful prelate in the country.
= 国王跟国内最有权势的高级教士闹翻了。
a powerful prelate = 有权势的高级教士

preliminary
/prɪˈlɪmɪnəri/
核心：形容词：初步的、预备的、开端的：在正事之前、为它作准备的，介词用 to。
名词：预备性的事、初步的步骤，可数，复数 preliminaries；（体育）预赛。口语里预赛、预考说 prelim。并入本条。
① The police have completed their preliminary inquiries into the fire.
= 警方已经完成了对火灾的初步调查。
preliminary inquiries = 初步调查
② The talks are preliminary to a full peace conference in the spring.
= 这些会谈是为春天的正式和平会议作准备的。
preliminary to something = 为某事作准备的
③ After the usual preliminaries the chairman came to the main business.
= 照例的开场事宜过后，主席进入了正题。
the preliminaries = 开场事宜
④ She was knocked out of the race in the prelims.
= 她在预赛里就被淘汰了。
the prelims = 预赛
a prelim = 一场预赛、预考

preload
/ˌpriːˈləʊd/
核心：动词：预先装入、预装：把软件、数据事先装进设备，多用被动；英式口语里指出门去酒吧之前先在家喝一通。名词：预装的东西。
① The tablet comes preloaded with a dictionary and six games.
= 那台平板电脑预装了一部词典和六款游戏。
be preloaded with something = 预装了某物
② The students preload on cheap wine at home before they go out.
= 那些学生出门之前先在家灌一通便宜葡萄酒。
preload on something = 出门前先喝某种酒

prelude
/ˈpreljuːd/
核心：两支，可数。一是（音乐）前奏曲、序曲：一部大作品开头的短曲，也指独立成篇的钢琴短曲；
二是序幕、前奏：为更大的事开了头的事，介词用 to。
① The pianist played three preludes by Chopin as an encore.
= 那位钢琴家加演了三首肖邦的前奏曲。
a prelude = 前奏曲
② The arrests were the prelude to a wider attack on the newspapers.
= 那些逮捕是对报界更大规模打压的序幕。
a prelude to something = 某事的序幕

premarital
/ˌpriːˈmærɪtl/
核心：婚前的。只作定语。
① The church still teaches that premarital sex is wrong.
= 那个教会至今仍教导说婚前性行为是错的。
premarital sex = 婚前性行为
② The couple signed a premarital agreement about the house and the business.
= 那对男女就房子和生意签了一份婚前协议。
a premarital agreement = 婚前协议

premature
/ˈpremətʃə(r)/
核心：三支。一是过早的、提前的：比正常的、预料的时间早；二是（婴儿）早产的；三是仓促的、为时过早的：下结论、作决定太急。
副词 prematurely，并入本条。
① Smoking is a common cause of premature death.
= 吸烟是早死的常见原因。
premature death = 早死
② The baby was six weeks premature and spent a month in hospital.
= 那婴儿早产六个星期，在医院住了一个月。
six weeks premature = 早产六个星期
③ It would be premature to say that the danger is over.
= 现在说危险已经过去还为时过早。
it would be premature to do something = 做某事为时过早
④ His hair went grey prematurely when he was only twenty-five.
= 他才二十五岁，头发就早早白了。
prematurely = 过早地

premedication
/ˌpriːmedɪˈkeɪʃn/
核心：（医学）术前用药：手术麻醉之前先给病人用的镇静药。不可数；口语缩成 premed。
① The nurse gave him his premedication an hour before the operation.
= 手术前一个钟头，护士给他用了术前药。
premedication = 术前用药
② The premedication made her drowsy before she reached the theatre.
= 还没到手术室，术前药就让她昏昏欲睡了。
the premedication = 术前药

premeditated
/ˌpriːˈmedɪteɪtɪd/
核心：（罪行、坏事）预谋的、蓄意的：事先打算好的，不是一时冲动。名词 premeditation，不可数。并入本条。
① The judge said that the attack was premeditated and showed no mercy.
= 法官说那次袭击是预谋的，量刑毫不留情。
be premeditated = 是预谋的
② He was found guilty of premeditated murder.
= 他被判蓄意谋杀罪成立。
premeditated murder = 蓄意谋杀
③ The lawyer argued that there had been no premeditation, only a moment of panic.
= 律师辩称并无预谋，只是一时惊慌。
premeditation = 预谋

premenstrual
/ˌpriːˈmenstruəl/
核心：经前的：月经来之前那几天的。只作定语。premenstrual syndrome 是经前综合征：经前的烦躁、乏力、身上不舒服，简称 PMS；英式也说 premenstrual tension。
① She suffers from premenstrual syndrome for a few days every month.
= 她每个月都有几天受经前综合征之苦。
premenstrual syndrome = 经前综合征
② The doctor suggested exercise as a way of easing premenstrual tension.
= 医生建议用锻炼来缓解经前的紧张不适。
premenstrual tension = 经前紧张

premier
/ˈpremiə(r)/
核心：名词：总理、首相：一些国家政府的首脑，新闻里也这样称英国首相；加拿大、澳大利亚的省长、州长。可数。
形容词，只作定语：首要的、最重要的、第一流的。名词 premiership 是总理的职位、任期；英格兰足球的顶级联赛叫 the Premier League。并入本条。
① The two premiers signed the trade agreement at a ceremony in the capital.
= 两国总理在首都的一个仪式上签了贸易协定。
a premier = 总理
② The town is the premier holiday resort on the south coast.
= 那座城是南岸首屈一指的度假胜地。
the premier resort = 首屈一指的度假地
③ The country grew rich during the ten years of his premiership.
= 在他当总理的十年里，那个国家富了起来。
his premiership = 他的总理任期
④ The club was promoted to the Premier League after forty years in the lower divisions.
= 那家俱乐部在低级别联赛待了四十年，终于升入英超。
the Premier League = 英格兰超级联赛

premiere
/ˈpremieə(r)/
核心：名词：（电影、戏剧的）首映、首演，可数。动词：首映、首演，及物或不及物。来自法语，也写作 première。
① The stars arrived for the premiere in a line of black cars.
= 明星们坐着一长串黑色轿车来出席首映式。
a premiere = 首映、首演
② The world premiere of the opera will take place in Vienna next spring.
= 那部歌剧的全球首演将于明年春天在维也纳举行。
the world premiere = 全球首演
③ The play premiered in a tiny theatre above a pub before it moved to the capital.
= 那出戏先在一家酒吧楼上的小剧场首演，后来才进了首都。
premiere = 首演

premise
/ˈpremɪs/
核心：名词：（正式）前提、假定：推理、论证所依据的那个说法，可数，英式也拼作 premiss。
动词：be premised on 是以……为前提。
复数的 premises 是另一回事：（一家店、一家公司占用的）房屋连同地皮、营业场所：on the premises 是在店内、在场所内。并入本条。
① The argument rests on the false premise that prices will go on rising.
= 那个论证建立在物价会一直上涨这个错误的前提上。
a false premise = 错误的前提
② The whole scheme is premised on the idea that people will pay more for quiet.
= 整个方案的前提是人们愿意为清静多掏钱。
be premised on something = 以某事为前提
③ The firm has moved to larger premises on the edge of the town.
= 那家公司搬到了城边更大的场所。
larger premises = 更大的场所
④ Alcohol may not be drunk on the premises.
= 店内不得饮酒。
on the premises = 在店内、在场所内
premises 永远是复数，动词也用复数。

premium
/ˈpriːmiəm/
核心：名词，可数：保险费：定期缴给保险公司的钱；额外的费用、加价、溢价：pay a premium for。
形容词，只作定语：高价的、优质的：premium prices、premium beer。
习语：at a premium 是奇缺、紧俏，也指以高于正常的价钱；put、place a premium on 是高度重视、看重。
① The premiums on my car insurance have gone up again this year.
= 我的汽车保险费今年又涨了。
an insurance premium = 保险费
② Buyers are willing to pay a premium for a house near a good school.
= 买房的人愿意为靠近好学校的房子多掏钱。
pay a premium for something = 为某物多掏钱
③ The shop charges premium prices for fruit flown in from abroad.
= 那家店对从国外空运来的水果收高价。
premium prices = 高价
④ Space is at a premium in the crowded old town.
= 在拥挤的老城里，空间十分紧俏。
at a premium = 奇缺、紧俏
⑤ The school puts a premium on good manners and neat handwriting.
= 那所学校很看重礼貌和工整的字迹。
put a premium on = 高度重视
put a premium on something = 看重某事

premodifier
/ˌpriːˈmɒdɪfaɪə(r)/
核心：（语法）前置修饰语：放在被修饰的名词前面的词、短语，如 a very old stone bridge 里的 very old stone。可数。
① In "the tall thin man", the adjectives "tall" and "thin" are premodifiers of the noun.
= 在「the tall thin man」里，形容词「tall」和「thin」是那个名词的前置修饰语。
a premodifier = 前置修饰语
② English allows a long string of premodifiers before a single noun.
= 英语允许一个名词前面排上长长一串前置修饰语。
a string of premodifiers = 一串前置修饰语
放在后面的叫 postmodifier。

premolar
/ˌpriːˈməʊlə(r)/
核心：（解剖）前臼齿、前磨牙：犬齿和臼齿之间的那几颗牙，成人上下各四颗。可数。
① The dentist had to take out one of her upper premolars to make room for the others.
= 牙医只好拔掉她一颗上前磨牙，给别的牙腾地方。
a premolar = 前磨牙
② The child's first premolars came through at the age of ten.
= 那孩子的第一前磨牙是十岁时长出来的。
the first premolars = 第一前磨牙

premonition
/ˌpriːməˈnɪʃn/
核心：（不祥的）预感：觉得要出事，多半是坏事，介词用 of、that。可数。形容词 premonitory 是预兆的、预警的，正式。并入本条。
① She had a premonition of danger as soon as she stepped on to the bridge.
= 她一踏上那座桥，就预感到有危险。
a premonition of danger = 有危险的预感
② He had a sudden premonition that he would never see the house again.
= 他忽然预感到自己再也见不到那座房子了。
a premonition that = ……的预感
③ The headaches were a premonitory sign of the illness that followed.
= 那些头疼是后来那场病的预兆。
a premonitory sign = 预兆

prenatal
/ˌpriːˈneɪtl/
核心：（美式）产前的、孕期的。只作定语；英式说 antenatal。
① She goes to the clinic once a month for prenatal care.
= 她每月去诊所做一次产前检查。
prenatal care = 产前保健
② The hospital runs prenatal classes for parents who are expecting their first child.
= 那家医院给头一回要孩子的父母开产前课。
prenatal classes = 产前课

preoccupation
/priˌɒkjuˈpeɪʃn/
核心：两支。一是心事、老惦记着的事：占满了一个人心思的那件事，可数；二是全神贯注、心事重重（的状态），不可数或单数：preoccupation with。
① His main preoccupation at the moment is finding a job before the baby is born.
= 他眼下最大的心事是赶在孩子出生前找到工作。
a main preoccupation = 最大的心事
② She spoke slowly, with an air of preoccupation.
= 她说得很慢，一副心事重重的样子。
an air of preoccupation = 心事重重的样子
③ The newspapers criticized the government's preoccupation with its own image.
= 报纸批评政府一门心思只顾自己的形象。
preoccupation with something = 一门心思顾着某事

preoccupy
/priˈɒkjupaɪ/
核心：使全神贯注、占满……的心思：让人老想着一件事，顾不上别的。及物；过去式 preoccupied。
分词 preoccupied 作形容词：心事重重的、心不在焉的：preoccupied with。并入本条。
① The question of money has preoccupied him ever since he lost his job.
= 自从丢了工作，钱的问题就一直占着他的心思。
preoccupy somebody = 占满某人的心思
② She was too preoccupied with her own troubles to notice that he was ill.
= 她光顾着自己的烦心事，没注意到他病了。
preoccupied with something = 一心想着某事
③ He gave me a preoccupied nod and went on reading the letter.
= 他心不在焉地冲我点了点头，接着看那封信。
a preoccupied nod = 心不在焉的点头

preoperative
/ˌpriːˈɒpərətɪv/
核心：（医学）术前的。只作定语；口语缩成 pre-op。
① The nurse explained the preoperative tests that he would have the day before surgery.
= 护士讲了手术前一天他要做的术前检查。
preoperative tests = 术前检查
② Patients are asked not to eat for twelve hours as part of their preoperative care.
= 术前护理的一项是让病人十二个钟头不进食。
preoperative care = 术前护理

preordained
/ˌpriːɔːˈdeɪnd/
核心：（正式）命中注定的、早已由神、由命运安排好的。
① The old woman believed that the day of every person's death was preordained.
= 那位老太太相信每个人的死期都是命里注定的。
be preordained = 命中注定
② The film follows its hero to a preordained end that surprises nobody.
= 那部片子跟着主人公走向一个早就注定、谁也不觉得意外的结局。
a preordained end = 注定的结局

prep
/prep/
核心：几个截短的口语词。名词，不可数：（英式）（寄宿学校的）家庭作业、自习时间；（美式）准备工作。
动词：（美式）为……作准备：给（病人）做术前准备、预备（食材）；也指作准备。过去式 prepped。
prep school 是 preparatory school 的简称。并入本条。
① The boys do their prep in the library between six and eight every evening.
= 男孩子们每天晚上六点到八点在图书馆做作业。
do your prep = 做作业
② The cooks begin their prep three hours before the restaurant opens.
= 餐馆开门前三个钟头，厨师们就开始备料。
prep = 准备工作
③ The nurses prepped the patient for surgery while the surgeon washed his hands.
= 外科医生洗手的时候，护士们给病人做术前准备。
prep a patient = 给病人做术前准备
④ He was sent away to prep school at the age of eight.
= 他八岁就被送去上预备学校。
prep school = 预备学校

prepaid
/ˌpriːˈpeɪd/
核心：预付的、已预先付费的：a prepaid envelope 是邮资已付的信封。名词 prepayment 是预付、预付的款项，可数也可不可数。并入本条。
① Please return the form in the prepaid envelope that came with it.
= 请用随表附上的邮资已付信封把表寄回。
a prepaid envelope = 邮资已付的信封
② She uses a prepaid card so that she cannot spend more than she has.
= 她用预付卡，这样花的钱不会超过手里有的。
a prepaid card = 预付卡
③ The hotel asks for prepayment of the first night when you book a room.
= 订房时，那家旅馆要求预付第一晚的房费。
prepayment = 预付

preparation
/ˌprepəˈreɪʃn/
核心：三支。一是准备、预备（这件事、这个过程），不可数：in preparation for；二是 preparations：（为某事作的）各项准备工作，复数：make preparations；
三是（特制的）配制品、制剂：药、化妆品、食品，可数。
① The team trained in the mountains in preparation for the long race.
= 为准备那场长距离比赛，那支队伍在山里训练。
in preparation for something = 为准备某事
② Careful preparation is the secret of a good speech.
= 认真准备是讲好话的诀窍。
careful preparation = 认真准备
③ The preparations for the wedding took nearly a year.
= 婚礼的各项准备花了将近一年。
preparations for something = 为某事作的准备工作
④ The chemist sells a preparation for dry skin made from oats.
= 药店卖一种用燕麦做的治皮肤干燥的制剂。
a preparation = 制剂

preparatory
/prɪˈpærətri/
核心：（正式）预备的、准备性的：为后面的事作准备的。只作定语；preparatory to 是在……之前、为……作准备。
preparatory school 是预备学校：在英国是为七到十三岁孩子办的私立小学，在美国是为上大学作准备的私立中学。并入本条。
① The committee held a preparatory meeting to agree on the agenda.
= 委员会开了一次预备会议，商定议程。
a preparatory meeting = 预备会议
② He cleared his desk preparatory to leaving the firm.
= 他收拾好办公桌，准备离开公司。
preparatory to doing something = 在做某事之前、为做某事作准备
③ Both brothers went to the same preparatory school in the hills.
= 兄弟俩上的是山里的同一所预备学校。
a preparatory school = 预备学校

prepare
/prɪˈpeə(r)/
核心：动词：准备、预备：把东西弄好备用：prepare a room、a report；做（饭）、调制；使（自己、别人）作好准备：prepare for、prepare to do、prepare somebody for。
习语 prepare the ground (for) 是为……铺路、打基础。
分词 prepared 作形容词：有准备的：be prepared for；愿意的：be prepared to do；事先准备好的：a prepared statement。名词 preparedness 是有备（的状态），不可数。并入本条。
① The nurse prepared a room for the new patient.
= 护士给新来的病人收拾出一间屋子。
prepare a room = 收拾出一间屋子
② She spent the whole afternoon preparing a meal for twelve people.
= 她花了一下午给十二个人做饭。
prepare a meal = 做饭
③ The students are preparing for their final examinations in June.
= 学生们在为六月的期末考试作准备。
prepare for something = 为某事作准备
④ The army was preparing to cross the river at dawn.
= 军队准备在拂晓渡河。
prepare to do something = 准备做某事
⑤ Nothing had prepared her for the shock of seeing the empty house.
= 看到那座空房子时的震惊，她事先毫无准备。
prepare somebody for something = 使某人对某事有准备
⑥ The early talks prepared the ground for a treaty the following year.
= 早期的会谈为第二年的条约铺了路。
prepare the ground = 铺路、打基础
⑦ We were not prepared for such a cold night in the mountains.
= 山里的夜晚这么冷，我们没有准备。
be prepared for something = 对某事有准备
⑧ I am not prepared to pay that much for a second-hand car.
= 我不愿意为一辆二手车出那么多钱。
be prepared to do something = 愿意做某事
⑨ The minister read a prepared statement and refused to answer questions.
= 部长念了一份事先准备好的声明，不肯回答提问。
a prepared statement = 事先准备好的声明
⑩ The floods exposed the country's lack of preparedness for disaster.
= 那场洪水暴露出该国对灾害毫无准备。
preparedness = 有备

preponderance
/prɪˈpɒndərəns/
核心：（正式）（数量、力量上的）优势、多数。单数：a preponderance of。形容词 preponderant，副词 preponderantly。并入本条。
① There is a preponderance of older people in the villages along the coast.
= 沿海的那些村子里老年人占多数。
a preponderance of something = 某类占多数
② The preponderance of the evidence suggests that the fire was an accident.
= 证据大多表明那场火是意外。
the preponderance of the evidence = 证据的大多数
③ Wheat is the preponderant crop on the plains of the north.
= 小麦是北方平原上最主要的庄稼。
preponderant = 占优势的
④ The staff of the hospital is preponderantly female.
= 那家医院的员工以女性为主。
preponderantly = 主要地、以……为主

preposition
/ˌprepəˈzɪʃn/
核心：（语法）介词、前置词：放在名词、代词前面，表示地点、时间、方式等关系的词，如 in、on、at、with。可数。
形容词 prepositional：a prepositional phrase 是介词短语。并入本条。
① Learners of English often choose the wrong preposition after a verb.
= 学英语的人常在动词后面用错介词。
a preposition = 介词
② Some teachers still say that a sentence should not end with a preposition.
= 有些老师至今还说句子不该以介词结尾。
end with a preposition = 以介词结尾
③ "In the morning" is a prepositional phrase that tells us when.
= 「in the morning」是一个说明时间的介词短语。
a prepositional phrase = 介词短语

prepossessing
/ˌpriːpəˈzesɪŋ/
核心：（正式）给人好印象的、讨人喜欢的：多说相貌、外表，常用在否定句里。反义是 unprepossessing。
① The hotel was not a prepossessing building, but the rooms were clean and cheap.
= 那家旅馆的楼不大起眼，可房间干净又便宜。
a prepossessing building = 给人好印象的建筑
② He was a prepossessing young man with an open face and a firm handshake.
= 他是个讨人喜欢的年轻人，一脸坦诚，握手有力。
a prepossessing young man = 讨人喜欢的年轻人

preposterous
/prɪˈpɒstərəs/
核心：（正式）两支。一是荒谬的、荒唐的：完全不合情理；二是怪诞的、离谱得可笑的。副词 preposterously，并入本条。
① The idea that the earth is flat seems preposterous to us now.
= 地球是平的这种想法，如今在我们看来荒谬透顶。
seem preposterous = 显得荒谬
② It is preposterous to suggest that the nurses caused the delay.
= 说延误是护士造成的，简直荒唐。
it is preposterous to do something = 做某事是荒唐的
③ She wore a preposterous hat covered with feathers and wax fruit.
= 她戴了一顶插满羽毛和蜡制水果的怪帽子。
a preposterous hat = 离谱的怪帽子
④ The houses in that street are preposterously expensive.
= 那条街上的房子贵得离谱。
preposterously = 离谱地

preppy
/ˈprepi/
核心：（美式口语）形容词：预科生派头的：像上贵族私立学校的富家子弟那样，穿着整洁考究、传统。名词：这样的年轻人，可数。也拼作 preppie。
① He wore the preppy uniform of a blue blazer and pale trousers.
= 他穿一身预科生的标配：蓝色便西装配浅色长裤。
preppy = 预科生派头的
② The bar was full of preppies talking about sailing and law school.
= 酒吧里满是谈帆船、谈法学院的富家子弟。
a preppy = 预科生派头的年轻人

preprandial
/ˌpriːˈprændiəl/
核心：（正式，或玩笑）饭前的、餐前的。只作定语。
① The guests gathered in the library for a preprandial drink.
= 客人们聚在藏书室里喝餐前酒。
a preprandial drink = 餐前酒
② He took a short preprandial walk along the river to work up an appetite.
= 他饭前沿河走了一小段，好开开胃口。
a preprandial walk = 饭前散步
饭后的是 postprandial。

prepuce
/ˈpriːpjuːs/
核心：（解剖）包皮；也指阴蒂包皮。可数；日常说 foreskin。
① The operation removes the prepuce and takes only a few minutes.
= 那种手术切除包皮，只要几分钟。
the prepuce = 包皮
② The textbook has a diagram that labels the prepuce and the surrounding tissue.
= 那本教科书里有一张图，标出了包皮和周围的组织。
label the prepuce = 标出包皮

prequel
/ˈpriːkwəl/
核心：前传：一部书、一部电影成功之后，再拍、再写的讲此前故事的作品。可数；仿 sequel 造的词。
① The prequel shows how the villain of the first film lost his family.
= 那部前传讲的是第一部里的反派是怎么失去家人的。
a prequel = 前传
② The author wrote a prequel twenty years after the novel that made her famous.
= 那部让她成名的小说问世二十年后，作者写了一部前传。
write a prequel = 写前传

preregister
/ˌpriːˈredʒɪstə(r)/
核心：预先登记、提前报名：在正式开始之前先把名字登上。不及物或及物。名词 preregistration，不可数。并入本条。
① Students who preregister for the course get a place before the general public.
= 提前报名那门课的学生比一般公众先得到名额。
preregister for something = 提前报名某事
也写作 pre-register。
② You can preregister on the website up to a month before the conference.
= 大会开幕前一个月之内都可以在网站上预先登记。
preregister on the website = 在网站上预先登记
③ Preregistration closes at midnight on the last day of June.
= 预先登记在六月最后一天的午夜截止。
preregistration = 预先登记

prerequisite
/ˌpriːˈrekwəzɪt/
核心：（正式）名词：先决条件、前提：做一件事之前必须先具备的东西，介词用 for、of、to，可数。形容词：必须先具备的，只作定语。
① A degree in science is a prerequisite for the job at the laboratory.
= 理科学位是得到实验室那份工作的先决条件。
a prerequisite for something = 某事的先决条件
比 requirement 更强调「先」。
② Good health is not a prerequisite of happiness, but it certainly helps.
= 身体好不是幸福的前提，可确实有帮助。
a prerequisite of something = 某事的前提
③ Applicants must show that they have the prerequisite skills in mathematics.
= 申请人须证明自己具备必需的数学本领。
prerequisite skills = 必须先具备的本领

prerogative
/prɪˈrɒɡətɪv/
核心：（正式）特权、专有的权利：某个人、某个集团因地位、身份才有的权利，可数。the royal prerogative 是君主的特权。
① It is the manager's prerogative to decide who works on the night shift.
= 谁上夜班由经理决定，那是经理的权力。
somebody's prerogative = 某人特有的权利
口语里也说「那是你的自由」：That is your prerogative.
② A university education was once the prerogative of a small and wealthy group.
= 上大学曾是一小群有钱人的特权。
the prerogative of somebody = 某类人的特权
③ The power to pardon a prisoner is part of the royal prerogative.
= 赦免犯人的权力是君主特权的一部分。
the royal prerogative = 君主的特权

presage
/ˈpresɪdʒ/
核心：（文学）动词：预示、预兆（多是不好的事）。及物。名词：预兆、预感，可数。
① The dark clouds over the hills presaged a storm before evening.
= 山上的乌云预示着傍晚前有暴风雨。
presage a storm = 预示暴风雨
② The fall in orders was seen as a presage of harder times for the factory.
= 订单减少被看作工厂日子要难过的预兆。
a presage of something = 某事的预兆

presbyopia
/ˌprezbiˈəʊpiə/
核心：（医学）老花眼、老视：人上了年纪，眼睛看近处的东西越来越吃力。不可数。
① Most people begin to notice presbyopia in their middle forties.
= 多数人四十五岁上下开始觉出老花眼。
presbyopia = 老花眼
② Reading glasses are the simplest way of correcting presbyopia.
= 配老花镜是矫正老花眼最简单的办法。
correct presbyopia = 矫正老花眼
presby- 来自希腊语「老人」。

Presbyterian
/ˌprezbɪˈtɪəriən/
核心：形容词：长老会的：基督教新教的一派，教会由同等地位的长老们管理，没有主教，苏格兰的国教就属于这一派。名词：长老会教徒，可数。
这一派、这种教会制度叫 Presbyterianism，不可数。并入本条。
① She was brought up in a strict Presbyterian family in the north of Scotland.
= 她在苏格兰北部一个严格的长老会家庭里长大。
a Presbyterian family = 长老会家庭
② The village has two churches, one for the Presbyterians and one for the Catholics.
= 村里有两座教堂，一座是长老会教徒的，一座是天主教徒的。
a Presbyterian = 长老会教徒
③ Presbyterianism spread to America with settlers from Scotland and Ireland.
= 长老会随着苏格兰和爱尔兰的移民传到了美洲。
Presbyterianism = 长老会、长老制

presbytery
/ˈprezbɪtri/
核心：三支，可数，复数 presbyteries。一是（长老会的）长老会议、教务评议会：管一片地区的教会；二是（天主教）神父住宅；三是（教堂里）祭坛周围的那片地方。
① The dispute between the two ministers was referred to the presbytery.
= 两位牧师之间的争执提交给了长老会议。
the presbytery = 长老会议
② The priest lives in the presbytery next to the church.
= 神父住在教堂旁边的神父住宅里。
the presbytery = 神父住宅

preschool
/ˈpriːskuːl/
核心：形容词，只作定语：学龄前的：a preschool child。名词：（美式）幼儿园：给两岁到五岁孩子办的，可数也可不可数。上幼儿园的孩子叫 preschooler。
① The clinic gives free eye tests to preschool children.
= 那家诊所给学龄前儿童免费检查视力。
preschool children = 学龄前儿童
也写作 pre-school。
② Their daughter goes to preschool three mornings a week.
= 他们的女儿每星期上三个上午的幼儿园。
go to preschool = 上幼儿园
③ The story is written for preschoolers and has only forty words.
= 那个故事是写给学龄前孩子的，只有四十个词。
a preschooler = 学龄前儿童

prescient
/ˈpresiənt/
核心：（正式）有先见之明的、预见到的。名词 prescience，不可数。并入本条。
① Her warning about the bank turned out to be remarkably prescient.
= 她对那家银行的警告后来证明极有先见之明。
remarkably prescient = 极有先见之明
读音 /ˈpresiənt/，跟 science 无关。
② The article of 1910 gives a prescient account of the coming war.
= 那篇一九一〇年的文章对即将到来的战争作了有预见的描述。
a prescient account = 有预见的描述
③ He sold his shares a month before the crash with what seemed like prescience.
= 他在崩盘前一个月卖掉了股票，好像未卜先知。
prescience = 先见之明

prescribe
/prɪˈskraɪb/
核心：两支，及物。一是（医生）开（药）、嘱咐采用（某种疗法）：prescribe something for；二是（正式）（法律、规章）规定、指定。
① The doctor prescribed a course of antibiotics for the infection.
= 医生开了一个疗程的抗生素治感染。
prescribe something for something = 为某病开某药
主语是医生。
② She was prescribed a week of complete rest after the operation.
= 手术后，医生嘱咐她彻底休息一个星期。
be prescribed something = 被嘱咐采用某疗法
③ The law prescribes a fine of up to a thousand pounds for the offence.
= 法律规定那种违法行为最高罚款一千英镑。
prescribe a fine = 规定罚款
别跟 proscribe（禁止）混：意思几乎相反。

prescription
/prɪˈskrɪpʃn/
核心：三支。一是处方、药方：医生开的那张单子，可数：a prescription for；on prescription 是凭处方；二是按处方配的药，可数；
三是（正式）（解决问题的）方案、诀窍：a prescription for，可数。
① The doctor wrote me a prescription for sleeping pills.
= 医生给我开了一张安眠药的处方。
a prescription for something = 某药的处方
write a prescription = 开处方
② The drug is available only on prescription.
= 那种药只能凭处方买。
on prescription = 凭处方
③ I am going to the chemist's to collect my prescription.
= 我去药店取处方上的药。
collect a prescription = 取处方药
④ Cutting taxes and spending more at the same time is a prescription for disaster.
= 一边减税一边增加开支，是自找灾祸。
a prescription for disaster = 自找灾祸的做法
⑤ The prescription charge has gone up again, to almost ten pounds for each item.
= 处方费又涨了，每一样药差不多要十英镑。
a prescription charge = （英国）处方费
在英国凭处方取药时交的一笔定额的钱。

prescriptive
/prɪˈskrɪptɪv/
核心：（正式）两支。一是规定性的、指示人该怎么做的：多含「管得太死」的意思；二是（语言学）规定性的：规定语言该怎么用，
而不是描述人们实际怎么用，跟 descriptive（描写性的）相对。
① The new guidelines are less prescriptive and leave more to the teacher.
= 新的指导方针管得没那么死，给教师留的余地多了。
be prescriptive = 规定得死
从 prescribe「规定」来。
② The old grammar book takes a prescriptive approach and lists forty errors to avoid.
= 那本旧语法书采取规定性的路子，列了四十条要避免的错。
a prescriptive approach = 规定性的路子

preseason
/ˌpriːˈsiːzn/
核心：（体育）赛季前的：正式比赛开始之前那段训练、热身的时期。多作定语；也作名词，单数。
① The team lost all four of its preseason friendlies.
= 那支球队季前的四场友谊赛全输了。
preseason friendlies = 季前友谊赛
也写作 pre-season。
② The players report for preseason training on the first Monday in July.
= 球员们七月的头一个星期一报到参加季前训练。
preseason training = 季前训练

preselect
/ˌpriːsɪˈlekt/
核心：预先选定、预选。及物，多用被动。
① The radio has six buttons for stations that you have preselected.
= 那台收音机有六个按钮，对应你预先选好的电台。
preselect a station = 预先选好电台
多用被动或分词。
② The candidates had been preselected by a committee before the members voted.
= 会员投票之前，候选人已经由一个委员会预先选定。
be preselected = 被预先选定

presence
/ˈprezns/
核心：名词：在场、出席、存在：某人在某处这件事，跟 absence 相对，不可数：in the presence of 是当着……的面；
（某物在某处的）存在，不可数：the presence of gas；（一国军队、警察在某地的）驻扎、势力，单数：a military presence；
（人的）仪态、气场、风度，不可数：stage presence；（感觉得到却看不见的）鬼魂、神灵，可数。
习语：presence of mind 是镇定、临危不乱的机智；make your presence felt 是让人感到你的存在、显出你的分量。
① Your presence is requested at the meeting on Friday.
= 请您出席星期五的会议。
your presence = 您的出席
请柬、公函里的说法。
② The will must be signed in the presence of two witnesses.
= 遗嘱须当着两位见证人的面签署。
in the presence of = 当着……的面
in the presence of somebody = 当着某人的面
③ The test showed the presence of lead in the water.
= 化验显示水里有铅。
the presence of something = 某物的存在
反义是 the absence of。
④ There is a heavy police presence in the streets round the stadium.
= 体育场周围的街上有大批警察驻守。
a police presence = 警力驻守
a military presence = 驻军
⑤ The actress has great stage presence, and nobody looks at anyone else.
= 那位女演员台风极好，没人再看别人。
stage presence = 台风、舞台上的气场
⑥ She felt a presence in the room, though she could see nobody.
= 她觉得屋里有个什么东西在，尽管一个人也看不见。
a presence = 看不见的存在、鬼魂
⑦ The boy had the presence of mind to pull the alarm.
= 那男孩临危不乱，拉响了警报。
presence of mind = 镇定、临危不乱的机智
⑧ The new captain made her presence felt in the first ten minutes of the match.
= 新队长在比赛的头十分钟就显出了她的分量。
make your presence felt = 显出你的分量

present
/ˈpreznt/（形、名）/prɪˈzent/（动）
核心：两种读音、三个词性。形容词和名词重音在前，动词重音在后。
形容词：现在的、目前的，只作定语：the present situation、the present day；在场的、出席的，不用在名词前：be present at；存在于……里的：present in。
名词：the present 是现在、目前，单数；（语法）现在时；礼物，可数：a birthday present，英式口语也叫 pressie、prezzie。
动词：（正式地）赠送、授予：present somebody with something、present something to somebody；提出、呈交（报告、计划）；
造成、带来（问题、困难）：present a problem；（机会）present itself 是出现；（英式）主持（电视、广播节目）；（正式）引见、介绍；
上演（戏）、推出（节目）；present yourself 是（正式）到场、出面。
习语：at present 是目前、现在；for the present 是暂时、眼下；present company excepted 是在座各位除外；there is no time like the present 是择日不如撞日；
all present and correct 是（英式）全都到齐、一样不缺。
形容词 presentable 是拿得出手的、见得了人的。并入本条。
① The present situation cannot go on for much longer.
= 目前的局面维持不了多久了。
the present situation = 目前的局面
这一支只作定语，放在名词前。
② Life in the villages has changed little from the Middle Ages to the present day.
= 从中世纪到今天，那些村子里的生活没多大变化。
the present day = 今天、当代
③ Fifty people were present at the meeting.
= 有五十人出席了会议。
be present at something = 出席某事
这一支不用在名词前；反义是 absent。
④ The chemical is present in small amounts in most kinds of fruit.
= 那种化学物质在多数水果里都有少量存在。
be present in something = 存在于某物里
说物质、成分，不说人。
⑤ You must stop worrying about the past and live in the present.
= 你得别再为过去发愁，活在当下。
the present = 现在
跟 the past、the future 并列。
⑥ The story is told in the present, as if it were happening now.
= 那个故事用现在时讲，好像事情正在发生。
the present = 现在时
⑦ What can I get him for a birthday present?
= 我给他买什么生日礼物好？
a birthday present = 生日礼物
give somebody a present = 送某人礼物
⑧ The children opened their pressies before breakfast on Christmas morning.
= 圣诞节早上，孩子们早饭前就拆了礼物。
a pressie = 礼物（口语）
a prezzie = 礼物（口语）
⑨ The mayor presented the winners with silver cups.
= 市长向获胜者颁发了银杯。
present somebody with something = 把某物授予某人
动词重音在第二个音节。
⑩ The staff presented a clock to the head teacher on her last day.
= 校长任职的最后一天，教职员送了她一座钟。
present something to somebody = 把某物赠给某人
⑪ The committee will present its report to the minister next week.
= 委员会下星期向部长呈交报告。
present a report = 呈交报告
present your case = 陈述理由
⑫ The narrow stairs presented a problem for the men carrying the piano.
= 楼梯窄，给抬钢琴的人出了难题。
present a problem = 造成难题
present a threat = 构成威胁
⑬ I will take a holiday as soon as an opportunity presents itself.
= 一有机会，我就休个假。
an opportunity presents itself = 机会出现
⑭ She presents a news programme on the radio every morning.
= 她每天早上在电台主持一档新闻节目。
present a programme = 主持节目
英式说法；美式多说 host。
⑮ May I present my daughter, who has just come home from university?
= 请允许我介绍一下我女儿，她刚从大学回来。
present somebody = 引见某人
很正式；日常说 introduce。
⑯ The theatre is presenting a new comedy for the summer season.
= 那家剧院在夏季演出季推出一出新喜剧。
present a comedy = 上演喜剧
⑰ He was told to present himself at the police station at nine the next morning.
= 他被告知第二天早上九点到警察局报到。
present yourself = 到场、出面
公文、告示里的说法。
⑱ At present there are no rooms free at the hotel.
= 目前那家旅馆没有空房。
at present = 目前
比 now 正式，多见于书面。
⑲ We shall stay in the flat for the present and look for a house in the spring.
= 我们眼下先住公寓，到春天再找房子。
for the present = 暂时、眼下
含「以后可能会变」的意思。
⑳ Lawyers always talk too much, present company excepted.
= 律师总是话太多，在座各位除外。
present company excepted = 在座各位除外
㉑ "When shall we start?" "There is no time like the present."
= 「咱们什么时候动手？」「择日不如撞日。」
no time like the present = 择日不如撞日
㉒ The sergeant counted the men and reported all present and correct.
= 军士清点了人数，报告全员到齐。
all present and correct = 全都到齐
㉓ I need ten minutes to make myself presentable before we go out.
= 出门前我得花十分钟把自己收拾得见得了人。
presentable = 拿得出手的、见得了人的

presentation
/ˌpreznˈteɪʃn/
核心：名词：（正式的）报告、演示、讲解：向一群人介绍一个想法、一件产品，可数：give a presentation；
授予、颁发（的仪式），可数也可不可数：the presentation of prizes；（东西）呈现、摆出来的样子、包装，不可数；
提交、出示，不可数：on presentation of；（戏、节目的）上演，可数。
形容词 presentational 是呈现方式上的。并入本条。
① She gave a short presentation on the new product to the sales team.
= 她给销售团队作了一个关于新产品的简短演示。
give a presentation = 作演示、作报告
② The presentation of prizes will take place in the school hall at three.
= 颁奖仪式三点在学校礼堂举行。
the presentation of prizes = 颁奖
③ The food was good, but the chef had taken little trouble over its presentation.
= 菜的味道不错，可厨师没在摆盘上花心思。
the presentation of food = 菜的摆盘
④ Members are admitted free on presentation of their cards.
= 会员出示会员卡即可免费入场。
on presentation of something = 出示某物后
⑤ The theatre's new presentation of the old comedy has been a great success.
= 那家剧院新排的那出老喜剧大获成功。
a presentation of a play = 一出戏的上演
⑥ The two reports differ only in presentational details.
= 那两份报告只在呈现方式的细节上有不同。
presentational = 呈现方式上的

presenteeism
/ˌpreznˈtiːɪzəm/
核心：出勤主义：人明明病了、累了也硬撑着来上班，或者没事也耗在班上，为的是让人看见自己在。不可数；仿 absenteeism 造的词。
① The firm's culture of presenteeism means that nobody leaves before the boss.
= 那家公司盛行出勤主义，老板不走谁也不走。
a culture of presenteeism = 出勤主义的风气
② Doctors warn that presenteeism spreads colds through the whole office.
= 医生警告说，带病硬撑着上班会让感冒传遍整个办公室。
presenteeism = 带病硬撑着上班

presenter
/prɪˈzentə(r)/
核心：两支，可数。一是（英式）（电视、广播节目的）主持人，美式多说 host、anchor；二是（颁奖仪式上的）颁奖人，也指作演示报告的人。
① The presenter of the morning show has been on the radio for thirty years.
= 那档早间节目的主持人在电台干了三十年。
the presenter of a show = 节目主持人
② She began as a weather presenter on a local television station.
= 她是从地方电视台的天气预报主持人干起的。
a weather presenter = 天气预报主持人
③ Each presenter at the conference has twenty minutes and ten slides.
= 大会上每位报告人有二十分钟、十张幻灯片。
a presenter = 报告人

presentiment
/prɪˈzentɪmənt/
核心：（正式）预感：觉得要出事，多是不好的事，介词用 of、that。可数。
① She had a presentiment of disaster as the ship left the harbour.
= 船驶出港口的时候，她有一种大祸临头的预感。
a presentiment of disaster = 大祸临头的预感
② He could not shake off the presentiment that he would never return.
= 他甩不掉那个预感：自己再也回不来了。
a presentiment that = ……的预感
跟 present「礼物、现在」无关：是 pre- 加 sentiment。

presently
/ˈprezntli/
核心：副词，三支。一是（美式用得多）目前、现在：放在动词前；二是（书面）不久之后、过了一会儿：用在讲过去的事里；三是（旧式，英式）马上、一会儿：用在讲将来的事里，放在句末。
① The firm is presently looking for a new head of sales.
= 那家公司目前正在物色新的销售主管。
be presently doing something = 目前正在做某事
② Presently the door opened, and an old man looked out.
= 过了一会儿，门开了，一位老人探出头来。
presently = 过了一会儿
③ "Wait in the hall," she said. "The doctor will see you presently."
= 她说：「在门厅里等着，医生一会儿就见你。」
presently = 一会儿、马上
意思要靠位置和时态来分。

preservation
/ˌprezəˈveɪʃn/
核心：三支，不可数。一是保护、维护：让老建筑、自然环境不受损坏；二是保存、维持（的状况）：in a good state of preservation；三是（食物的）防腐保存。
主张保护老建筑、自然环境的人叫 preservationist。并入本条。
① The society works for the preservation of old buildings in the town.
= 那个协会为保护镇上的老建筑而努力。
the preservation of something = 对某物的保护
② The paintings in the tomb are in a remarkable state of preservation.
= 墓里的壁画保存得出奇地好。
a state of preservation = 保存状况
③ Salt was used for the preservation of meat before there were fridges.
= 有冰箱以前，人们用盐来保存肉。
the preservation of meat = 肉的保存
④ Preservationists are fighting to save the old station from being pulled down.
= 保护人士正在力争不让旧车站被拆掉。
a preservationist = 主张保护的人
⑤ The council has placed a preservation order on the old oak tree by the church.
= 镇议会给教堂旁边那棵老橡树下了保护令。
a preservation order = （英国）保护令
下了保护令的树、建筑不得砍伐、拆改。

preservative
/prɪˈzɜːvətɪv/
核心：防腐剂、保护剂：加在食物里防它变质的东西，涂在木头上防它腐烂的东西。可数也可不可数；也作形容词。
① The bread contains no artificial preservatives and goes stale in two days.
= 那种面包不含人工防腐剂，两天就不新鲜了。
artificial preservatives = 人工防腐剂
② He painted the fence with a wood preservative before the winter.
= 入冬前，他给栅栏刷了一遍木材防腐剂。
a wood preservative = 木材防腐剂

preserve
/prɪˈzɜːv/
核心：动词：保护、保存：让……保持原样、不受损坏：preserve a building；维持、保住（和平、名声、尊严）；
（用盐、糖、醋、烟熏）保存（食物）、做成蜜饯腌菜；be preserved 是（古物）保存下来。
名词：果酱、蜜饯，可数，多用复数；（某一类人）独占的领域、专属的活动，单数：the preserve of；（美式）自然保护区、禁猎区，可数。
保存、保护东西的人、物叫 preserver：life preserver 是（美式）救生圈、救生衣。并入本条。
① The council is trying to preserve the character of the old town.
= 镇议会在努力保住老城的风貌。
preserve the character of something = 保住某物的风貌
② The two sides are anxious to preserve the peace until the talks begin.
= 双方都急于在会谈开始前维持和平。
preserve the peace = 维持和平
③ She preserves plums in syrup every autumn.
= 她每年秋天用糖浆腌李子。
preserve fruit = 腌制水果
④ The body had been preserved in the ice for five thousand years.
= 那具遗体在冰里保存了五千年。
be preserved = 被保存下来
⑤ We had toast and strawberry preserves for breakfast.
= 我们早饭吃的是吐司配草莓果酱。
preserves = 果酱、蜜饯
⑥ Sailing used to be the preserve of the rich.
= 帆船运动从前是有钱人的专利。
the preserve of somebody = 某类人独占的领域
⑦ The lake is part of a wildlife preserve where no hunting is allowed.
= 那个湖属于一片不许打猎的野生动物保护区。
a wildlife preserve = 野生动物保护区
⑧ The sailor threw a life preserver to the man in the water.
= 水手朝落水的人扔了一个救生圈。
a life preserver = 救生圈、救生衣

preset
/ˌpriːˈset/
核心：动词：预先设定、预调：过去式、过去分词都是 preset。形容词：预先设定的。名词：（设备上的）预设档，可数。
① You can preset the oven to switch itself on at six in the evening.
= 你可以把烤箱预设成晚上六点自动开。
preset something to do something = 预设某物做某事
② The heating comes on at a preset time every morning.
= 暖气每天早上在预设的时间自动打开。
a preset time = 预设的时间
③ The radio has ten presets for the stations you listen to most.
= 那台收音机有十个预设档，存你最常听的电台。
a preset = 预设档

preside
/prɪˈzaɪd/
核心：主持、担任主席：preside at、over 加会议、仪式；preside over 也指（在某人当权期间）经历、主管（一段时期、一种局面）。不及物。
① The judge who presided at the trial retired the following year.
= 主持那场审判的法官第二年退休了。
preside at a trial = 主持审判
② The chairman presided over the meeting with great patience.
= 主席很有耐心地主持了会议。
preside over a meeting = 主持会议
③ The minister presided over the largest fall in exports for twenty years.
= 那位部长任内出口出现了二十年来最大的下滑。
preside over a fall = 任内出现下滑

presidency
/ˈprezɪdənsi/
核心：总统、主席、会长的职位；也指任期。可数，多用单数，复数 presidencies。
① He was elected to the presidency at the age of forty-three.
= 他四十三岁当选总统。
be elected to the presidency = 当选总统
② The war began in the last year of her presidency.
= 那场战争是在她任期的最后一年打起来的。
during somebody's presidency = 在某人的任期内

president
/ˈprezɪdənt/
核心：两支，可数。一是总统、国家主席：共和国的元首，用在名字前大写；二是（协会、学院、银行的）会长、院长、行长，（美式）（公司的）总裁。
口语里总统也叫 prez。形容词 presidential 是总统的、总统制的。并入本条。
① The president will address the nation on television tonight.
= 总统今晚将在电视上向全国讲话。
the president = 总统
② She was elected president of the students' union.
= 她当选了学生会主席。
president of a union = 协会的主席
③ He joined the firm as a clerk and ended as its president.
= 他进公司时是个办事员，最后当上了总裁。
the president of a firm = 公司的总裁
④ The newspaper headline read "Prez says no to new tax".
= 那份报纸的标题是「总统对新税说不」。
prez = 总统（口语）
⑤ The presidential election will be held in November.
= 总统选举将在十一月举行。
presidential = 总统的

presidium
/prɪˈsɪdiəm/
核心：主席团：尤指从前社会主义国家里的常设领导机构。可数；也拼作 praesidium。
① The decision was approved by the presidium of the party.
= 那项决定经党的主席团批准。
the presidium = 主席团
② He sat on the presidium for ten years without once speaking in public.
= 他在主席团里坐了十年，没在公开场合讲过一次话。
sit on the presidium = 在主席团任职
a praesidium = 主席团（另一种拼法）

press
/pres/
核心：本义是「用力压」。动词：按、压、挤：press a button、press A against B；把……压平、熨平；榨（汁、油）、压制（唱片）；
握紧（手、胳膊）表示亲热；（人群）挤着往前：press forward；催促、逼迫：press somebody to do、press somebody for；坚持（要求、主张）：press a claim。
名词：the press 是报界、新闻界、记者们，单数，动词单复数都行：the freedom of the press；（报刊上的）报道、评价，单数或不可数：get a good press；
印刷机，也指出版社，可数；压榨机、熨平机，可数；一按、一压，单数；（英式）人群的拥挤，单数。
短语动词：press on、press ahead (with) 是不顾困难坚决干下去；press for 是迫切要求、力争。
习语：press charges (against) 是起诉、提出指控；be pressed for time、money 是时间紧、手头紧；press the flesh 是（政客）跟民众握手拉票；
go to press 是付印；hot off the press 是刚印出来的、刚出炉的；press something home 是把（观点、优势）贯彻到底。
派生与复合词：pressed 是压制的：pressed flowers；pressing 是紧迫的，也指压制出来的一批（唱片）；press-up 是（英式）俯卧撑，美式说 push-up；
press stud 是（英式）按扣；press conference 是记者招待会；press release 是新闻稿；press cutting 是（英式）剪报；
press secretary 是新闻秘书；pressman 是（英式）记者，也指印刷工。并入本条。
① Press the green button to start the machine.
= 按绿色按钮开机。
press a button = 按按钮
② She pressed her face against the window to see inside.
= 她把脸贴在窗玻璃上往里看。
press A against B = 把甲贴在乙上
③ He pressed his trousers before the interview.
= 面试之前，他把长裤熨平了。
press your trousers = 熨长裤
④ The grapes are pressed within an hour of being picked.
= 葡萄摘下来不到一个钟头就压榨。
press grapes = 压榨葡萄
⑤ She pressed my hand and thanked me for coming.
= 她握紧我的手，谢我能来。
press somebody's hand = 握紧某人的手
⑥ The crowd pressed forward to see what was happening.
= 人群往前挤，想看出了什么事。
press forward = 往前挤
⑦ They pressed me to stay for dinner, but I had a train to catch.
= 他们一再留我吃晚饭，可我得赶火车。
press somebody to do something = 一再催某人做某事
⑧ The bank is pressing us for payment by the end of the month.
= 银行催我们月底前付款。
press somebody for something = 向某人催要某物
⑨ The story was reported in the press all over the world.
= 那件事全世界的报纸都报道了。
the press = 报界、新闻界
⑩ The freedom of the press is protected by the constitution.
= 新闻自由受宪法保护。
the freedom of the press = 新闻自由
⑪ The film got a bad press, but the cinemas were full.
= 那部片子报上评价不好，电影院却场场满座。
get a bad press = 报上评价不好
⑫ The first books in the town were printed on a wooden press.
= 镇上最早的书是用一台木头印刷机印的。
a printing press = 印刷机
⑬ She gave the doorbell a long press and waited.
= 她长长地按了一下门铃，等着。
a press = 一按
⑭ We decided to press on to the next village before dark.
= 我们决定坚持赶路，天黑前到下一个村子。
press on = 坚决干下去、坚持前进
⑮ The government is pressing ahead with its plans for a new airport.
= 政府不顾反对推进建新机场的计划。
press ahead = 坚决推进
press ahead with something = 坚决推进某事
⑯ The unions are pressing for a shorter working week.
= 工会在力争缩短每周工时。
press for something = 力争某事
⑰ The shopkeeper decided not to press charges against the boy.
= 店主决定不起诉那个男孩。
press charges = 起诉、提出指控
⑱ I am rather pressed for time this morning, so can we talk later?
= 我今天上午时间有点紧，咱们晚点再谈行吗？
pressed for time = 时间紧
⑲ The candidate spent the morning pressing the flesh in the market.
= 那位候选人一上午都在集市上跟人握手拉票。
press the flesh = 握手拉票
⑳ The results were not known when the newspaper went to press.
= 报纸付印的时候，结果还没出来。
go to press = 付印
㉑ Here is the latest copy of the magazine, hot off the press.
= 这是刚印出来的最新一期杂志。
hot off the press = 刚印出来的
㉒ The lawyer pressed home her point by reading the letter aloud.
= 律师把那封信大声念出来，把她的论点钉死了。
press home = 把……贯彻到底
press something home = 把某观点贯彻到底
㉓ She keeps pressed flowers between the pages of an old dictionary.
= 她在一本旧词典的书页间夹着压花。
pressed flowers = 压花
㉔ The most pressing problem is finding somewhere for the families to sleep.
= 最紧迫的问题是给那些人家找个睡觉的地方。
pressing = 紧迫的
㉕ He does fifty press-ups every morning before breakfast.
= 他每天早饭前做五十个俯卧撑。
a press-up = 俯卧撑
㉖ The jacket fastens with press studs instead of buttons.
= 那件上衣用按扣，不用纽扣。
a press stud = 按扣
㉗ The minister announced the plan at a press conference this morning.
= 部长今天上午在记者招待会上宣布了那个计划。
a press conference = 记者招待会
㉘ The company issued a press release denying the report.
= 那家公司发了一份新闻稿否认那篇报道。
a press release = 新闻稿
㉙ Her mother kept a box of press cuttings about her career.
= 她母亲留着一盒关于她事业的剪报。
a press cutting = 剪报
㉚ The president's press secretary refused to answer the question.
= 总统的新闻秘书拒绝回答那个问题。
a press secretary = 新闻秘书
㉛ The pressmen waited outside the court for the verdict.
= 记者们在法院外面等判决。
a pressman = 记者、印刷工
㉜ The story was sent round the world by a press agency in Paris.
= 那条消息是巴黎的一家通讯社发往全世界的。
a press agency = 通讯社
也说 news agency。
㉝ The singer's press agent refused to answer any questions about the divorce.
= 那位歌手的新闻代理人不肯回答任何关于离婚的问题。
a press agent = 新闻代理人、宣传员
替名人、机构跟媒体打交道的人。
㉞ The reporters watched the match from the press box above the main stand.
= 记者们在主看台上方的记者席上看了那场比赛。
the press box = （体育场的）记者席
㉟ The press gallery was full for the Prime Minister's statement on the crisis.
= 首相就那场危机发表声明时，记者旁听席座无虚席。
the press gallery = （议会、法庭的）记者旁听席
㊱ In those days a press gang could seize any man in the port for the navy.
= 那年头，抓丁队可以把港口里任何一个男人抓去当海军。
a press gang = （旧时的）抓丁队
动词 press-gang somebody into doing something 是硬逼某人做某事。
㊲ All enquiries from journalists should be sent to the press office at the ministry.
= 记者的一切询问请转给部里的新闻处。
a press office = 新闻处、新闻办公室
a press officer = 新闻官

pressure
/ˈpreʃə(r)/
核心：本义是「压上来的力」。名词：（物理）压力、压强：气体、液体压在表面上的力，可数也可不可数：air pressure、blood pressure 是血压；
按、压的力，不可数：apply pressure to；（让人做某事的）催逼、压力，不可数或用复数：pressure to do、pressure from；（工作、生活的）紧张、压力，不可数或用复数：the pressures of work。
动词：（美式用得多）对……施压、逼迫：pressure somebody into doing，英式多说 pressurize。
习语：under pressure 是受到压力、被逼着，也指（气体、液体）受压；put pressure on 是对……施压；pile on the pressure 是加大压力、步步紧逼。
派生与复合词：pressurize 是对……施压，也指给（机舱等）增压，英式也拼作 pressurise，名词 pressurization；
pressure cooker 是高压锅，比喻让人喘不过气的紧张环境；pressure group 是压力集团；pressure point 是（身体上的）压痛点、止血点，也指矛盾的焦点；
pressure washer 是高压清洗机；pressure suit 是（飞行员、宇航员穿的）增压服。并入本条。
① The nurse checked his blood pressure twice a day.
= 护士每天给他量两次血压。
blood pressure = 血压
high blood pressure = 高血压
② Check the pressure of the tyres before a long journey.
= 出远门前检查一下胎压。
the pressure of the tyres = 胎压
air pressure = 气压
③ Apply firm pressure to the wound until the bleeding stops.
= 用力按住伤口，直到血止住。
apply pressure to something = 按住某物
④ There is great pressure on the minister to resign.
= 要那位部长辞职的压力很大。
pressure on somebody to do something = 要某人做某事的压力
under pressure to do something = 被逼着做某事
⑤ She gave up the job because she could not stand the pressures of the work.
= 她受不了工作的压力，辞了那份差事。
the pressures of work = 工作的压力
⑥ He works best under pressure, with a deadline an hour away.
= 他在压力下干得最好：离截止还有一个钟头的时候。
under pressure = 在压力下
这一条说人；下一条说气体、液体。
⑦ The gas is stored under pressure in steel bottles.
= 那种气体加压存放在钢瓶里。
stored under pressure = 加压存放
⑧ The banks are putting pressure on the firm to sell its factories.
= 银行在逼那家公司卖掉工厂。
put pressure on = 对……施压
put pressure on somebody to do something = 逼某人做某事
⑨ The home team piled on the pressure in the last ten minutes.
= 最后十分钟，主队步步紧逼。
pile on the pressure = 加大压力
多见于体育报道。
⑩ Do not let the salesman pressure you into signing today.
= 别让推销员逼着你今天就签字。
pressure somebody into doing something = 逼某人做某事
美式的动词用法；英式多用 pressurize。
⑪ She felt that she had been pressurized into taking the job.
= 她觉得自己是被逼着接下那份工作的。
be pressurized into doing something = 被逼着做某事
pressurize = 施压、增压
⑫ The cabin of the plane is pressurized so that passengers can breathe normally.
= 飞机的客舱是增压的，好让乘客正常呼吸。
be pressurized = 被增压
pressurization = 增压
⑬ She cooks the beans in a pressure cooker in twenty minutes.
= 她用高压锅二十分钟就把豆子煮好了。
a pressure cooker = 高压锅
下一条是它的比喻用法。
⑭ The office became a pressure cooker in the week before the launch.
= 发布前的那个星期，办公室成了个高压锅。
a pressure cooker = 让人喘不过气的环境
⑮ A pressure group of local parents forced the council to keep the school open.
= 一个由当地家长组成的压力集团迫使镇议会保住了那所学校。
a pressure group = 压力集团
