㊲ Do not look so worried, for I was only pulling your leg.
= 别这么一脸担心，我不过是逗你玩。
pull somebody's leg = 跟某人开玩笑
㊳ You will have to pull your socks up if you want to pass the examination.
= 你想考及格的话，就得加把劲了。
pull your socks up = 加把劲、振作起来
这是英式口语。
㊴ The colonel pulled rank and took the best room for himself.
= 上校摆出官架子，把最好的房间占了。
pull rank = 摆官架子、拿职位压人
pull rank on somebody = 拿职位压某人
㊵ The children pulled faces at each other behind the teacher's back.
= 孩子们在老师背后互相做鬼脸。
pull a face = 做鬼脸
pull faces at somebody = 冲某人做鬼脸
㊶ The critics pulled his new play to pieces on the morning after it opened.
= 他的新戏首演第二天早上，评论家们就把它批得体无完肤。
pull to pieces = 批得体无完肤
pull something to pieces = 把某物批得体无完肤
㊷ The inspector did not pull his punches when he wrote his report on the school.
= 督学写那所学校的报告时毫不留情。
pull your punches = 手下留情
多用在否定句里。
㊸ He took a long pull at the bottle and passed it to the next man.
= 他对着瓶子喝了一大口，递给下一个人。
a pull at a bottle = 对着瓶子喝的一大口
a pull on a cigarette = 吸的一大口烟
㊹ He opened the can of lemonade by the pull tab and drank it at once.
= 他拉开柠檬汽水的拉环，一口气喝了下去。
a pull tab = （易拉罐的）拉环
英式也说 ring pull。

pullback
/ˈpʊlbæk/
核心：两支，可数，多用单数。一是（军队的）后撤；二是（价格、股市的）回落、回调。
① The general ordered a pullback of all troops from the border.
= 将军下令把所有部队从边境后撤。
a pullback of troops = 部队的后撤
a pullback = 后撤
② Share prices rose again after a brief pullback at the start of the week.
= 股价在周初短暂回落之后又涨了。
a brief pullback = 短暂的回落
这一支是金融用语。

pullet
/ˈpʊlɪt/
核心：小母鸡：不满一岁、刚开始下蛋的母鸡。可数。
① The farmer bought twenty pullets at the market in the spring.
= 开春时那个农民在集市上买了二十只小母鸡。
a pullet = 小母鸡
② The pullets laid their first small eggs at the end of the summer.
= 那些小母鸡夏末下了头一批小个儿的蛋。
a pullet lays an egg = 小母鸡下蛋
长成了叫 hen。

pulley
/ˈpʊli/
核心：滑轮、滑车：一个带槽的轮子，绳子或链条绕在上面，用来吊起重物。可数。
① The builders lifted the beams to the roof with a rope and a pulley.
= 建筑工人用绳子和滑轮把大梁吊上了屋顶。
a rope and a pulley = 绳子和滑轮
a pulley = 滑轮
② The washing hung from a rack that was raised to the ceiling on a pulley.
= 洗好的衣服挂在一个靠滑轮升到天花板的架子上。
on a pulley = 靠滑轮
a system of pulleys = 滑轮组

pullover
/ˈpʊləʊvə(r)/
核心：套头毛衣、套头衫：没有扣子、从头上套着穿的针织上衣。可数。
① He wore a thick woollen pullover under his jacket to keep out the cold.
= 他在外套里面穿了一件厚羊毛套头衫御寒。
a woollen pullover = 羊毛套头衫
a pullover = 套头毛衣
② She knitted her grandson a blue pullover for his first day at school.
= 她给孙子织了一件蓝色的套头毛衣，让他上学头一天穿。
knit a pullover = 织套头毛衣
英式也说 jumper、sweater。

pullulate
/ˈpʌljuleɪt/
核心：（正式）两支，不及物。一是迅速繁殖、大量滋生；二是 pullulate with：挤满、充斥着（活的、动的东西）。
分词 pullulating 作形容词：密密麻麻攒动的。并入本条。
① The insects pullulate in the warm mud at the edge of the lake.
= 那些虫子在湖边温暖的泥里大量滋生。
pullulate = 大量滋生
② The narrow streets of the old port pullulate with sailors and traders.
= 老港口那些窄街上挤满了水手和商人。
pullulate with something = 挤满某物
③ He pushed his way through the pullulating crowd at the gates of the station.
= 他从车站门口攒动的人群里挤了过去。
a pullulating crowd = 攒动的人群
是书面语；日常说 swarm、teem。

pulmonary
/ˈpʌlmənəri/
核心：（医学）肺的、肺部的。只放在名词前。
① The patient died of a pulmonary embolism two days after the operation.
= 那个病人术后两天死于肺栓塞。
a pulmonary embolism = 肺栓塞
pulmonary disease = 肺病
② The pulmonary artery carries blood from the heart to the lungs.
= 肺动脉把血从心脏送到肺。
the pulmonary artery = 肺动脉
是医学用词；日常说 lung。

pulp
/pʌlp/
核心：名词：（捣成、煮成的）糊状物、浆，单数或不可数；纸浆，不可数；果肉，不可数；也作定语：（书刊）低俗的、粗制滥造的。
动词：把……捣成浆、打成糊；把（没卖掉的书报）化成纸浆。习语 beat somebody to a pulp 是把某人打得稀烂。
形容词 pulpy 是糊状的、软烂的。并入本条。
① Mash the cooked fruit to a pulp and stir in the sugar.
= 把煮熟的水果捣成泥，拌进糖。
mash something to a pulp = 把某物捣成泥
a pulp = 糊状物
② The mill turns thousands of trees into wood pulp every week.
= 那家厂每个星期把成千上万棵树做成木浆。
wood pulp = 木浆
paper pulp = 纸浆
③ Cut the orange in half and scoop out the pulp with a spoon.
= 把橙子切成两半，用勺子把果肉挖出来。
the pulp of a fruit = 果肉
这一支不可数。
④ He made his living writing pulp novels about detectives and gangsters.
= 他靠写侦探和黑帮的地摊小说为生。
a pulp novel = 地摊小说
pulp magazines = 低俗杂志
⑤ The unsold copies of the book were pulped at the end of the year.
= 那本书没卖掉的在年底化成了纸浆。
be pulped = 被化成纸浆
pulp fruit = 把水果打成浆
⑥ The gang threatened to beat him to a pulp if he went to the police.
= 那伙人威胁说，他要是去报警就把他打个稀烂。
beat somebody to a pulp = 把某人打得稀烂
⑦ The pears had gone soft and pulpy at the bottom of the bowl.
= 碗底的梨已经软烂了。
pulpy = 糊状的、软烂的

pulpit
/ˈpʊlpɪt/
核心：（教堂的）讲坛、布道坛：牧师站上去讲道的高台，四周有围栏。可数。
① The priest climbed the steps of the pulpit and opened his Bible.
= 牧师登上讲坛的台阶，翻开了《圣经》。
the steps of the pulpit = 讲坛的台阶
a pulpit = 讲坛
② The new tax was condemned from the pulpit of every church in the county.
= 郡里每座教堂的讲坛上都在谴责那项新税。
from the pulpit = 在讲坛上
from the pulpit 也泛指「在布道时」。

pulsar
/ˈpʌlsɑː(r)/
核心：（天文）脉冲星：一种快速旋转、有规律地发出无线电信号的星体。可数。
① The pulsar sends out a burst of radio waves thirty times every second.
= 那颗脉冲星每秒发出三十次无线电波。
a pulsar = 脉冲星
② The first pulsar was discovered by a young astronomer in nineteen sixty-seven.
= 头一颗脉冲星是一位年轻的天文学家一九六七年发现的。
discover a pulsar = 发现脉冲星
是 pulsating star 的缩合。

pulsate
/pʌlˈseɪt/
核心：两支，不及物。一是有节奏地跳动、搏动、震动；二是 pulsate with：充满（活力、兴奋）。名词 pulsation，可数也可不可数。并入本条。
① The lights on the stage pulsated in time to the beat of the drums.
= 舞台上的灯光随着鼓点一明一灭。
pulsate = 有节奏地跳动
pulsate in time to something = 随着某物的节拍跳动
② The streets of the city pulsate with life until the early hours of the morning.
= 那座城市的街道直到凌晨都热闹非凡。
pulsate with life = 充满生气
pulsate with something = 充满某物
③ She could feel the pulsations of the engine through the floor of the boat.
= 她隔着船底感觉得到发动机的震动。
pulsations = 搏动、震动
a pulsation = 一次搏动

pulse
/pʌls/
核心：名词：脉搏，可数，多用单数：take somebody's pulse；（音乐的）节拍、律动，单数；（声、光、电的）脉冲，可数；
pulses 是豆类：豌豆、扁豆、蚕豆这类能吃的种子，多用复数。动词：搏动、跳动；pulse with 是洋溢着（活力）。
习语 have your finger on the pulse 是对最新动向了如指掌。
① The nurse took his pulse and wrote the figure on the chart.
= 护士给他把了脉，把数字记在病历上。
take somebody's pulse = 给某人把脉
take someone's pulse = 给某人把脉（somebody 也写作 someone）
feel somebody's pulse = 摸某人的脉搏
② Her pulse was racing as she waited for the results to be read out.
= 等着宣读结果的时候，她的心怦怦直跳。
a racing pulse = 急促的脉搏
a pulse rate = 脉搏数
③ The dancers moved to the steady pulse of the drums.
= 舞者们随着鼓声稳稳的节拍舞动。
the pulse of the drums = 鼓的节拍
这一支多用单数。
④ The machine sends out a pulse of light every ten seconds.
= 那台机器每十秒发出一个光脉冲。
a pulse of light = 光脉冲
a pulse = 脉冲
⑤ Pulses such as lentils and chickpeas are a cheap source of protein.
= 扁豆、鹰嘴豆这类豆子是便宜的蛋白质来源。
pulses = 豆类
这一支多用复数。
⑥ A vein pulsed in his forehead as he tried to control his anger.
= 他竭力压住怒气，额头上一根青筋直跳。
pulse = 搏动、跳动
⑦ The square pulsed with excitement on the night of the election.
= 选举之夜，广场上群情激动。
pulse with excitement = 洋溢着兴奋
pulse with life = 充满生气
⑧ A good editor has her finger on the pulse of what young people are reading.
= 好编辑对年轻人在读什么了如指掌。
have your finger on the pulse = 对最新动向了如指掌
keep a finger on the pulse = 随时掌握最新动向

pulverize
/ˈpʌlvəraɪz/
核心：两支，及物。一是（正式）把……磨成粉、粉碎；二是（口语）彻底打垮、把……打得落花流水。英式也拼作 pulverise。
① The machine pulverizes the rock into a fine grey powder.
= 那台机器把岩石磨成细细的灰色粉末。
pulverize something into powder = 把某物磨成粉
pulverize rock = 把岩石粉碎
② Our team was pulverized by seven goals to nil in the second round.
= 我们队在第二轮被人以七比零打得落花流水。
be pulverized = 被打得落花流水
pulverize an opponent = 把对手打垮

puma
/ˈpjuːmə/
核心：美洲狮：美洲的一种大型野猫，毛色黄褐。可数。美式多叫 cougar、mountain lion。
① A puma had killed three sheep on the farm during the night.
= 夜里一头美洲狮咬死了农场的三只羊。
a puma = 美洲狮
② The puma lay along a branch, watching the deer drink at the stream.
= 美洲狮趴在一根树枝上，盯着在溪边喝水的鹿。
the puma watches its prey = 美洲狮盯着猎物
英式多用这个词。

pumice
/ˈpʌmɪs/
核心：浮石、轻石：火山喷出的一种灰色石头，很轻、多孔，用来磨掉硬皮、打磨东西。不可数。也说 pumice stone，可数。
① She rubbed the hard skin on her heels with a piece of pumice.
= 她用一块浮石磨脚后跟上的硬皮。
a piece of pumice = 一块浮石
pumice = 浮石
② The beach was covered with grey pumice thrown out by the volcano.
= 海滩上铺满了火山喷出来的灰色浮石。
a pumice stone = 一块浮石
浮石能浮在水上。

pummel
/ˈpʌml/
核心：（用拳头）连续捶打、猛击。及物也可不及物。过去式 pummelled，美式 pummeled。
① The little girl pummelled her brother's chest with both fists.
= 小女孩用两只拳头捶哥哥的胸口。
pummel somebody = 连续捶打某人
pummel somebody with your fists = 用拳头捶某人
② The champion pummelled his opponent against the ropes until the bell rang.
= 冠军把对手逼在围绳上猛打，直打到铃响。
pummel an opponent = 猛击对手
③ The home side was pummelled in the press after losing four matches in a row.
= 主队连输四场之后，被报纸骂了个狗血淋头。
be pummelled in the press = 被报纸痛批
这是比喻的用法。

pump
/pʌmp/
核心：名词，可数：泵、抽水机、打气筒：a petrol pump、a bicycle pump；（英式）pumps 是轻便的帆布鞋、胶底鞋，（美式）是浅口的女式高跟鞋。
动词：用泵抽、送（液体、气体）：pump water out of；（液体）一股股地涌出；一上一下地快速摇动；（口语）盘问、套（某人的）话：pump somebody for information。
短语动词：pump in、pump into 是大量投入（钱）；pump out 是大量生产、源源不断地送出；pump up 是给……打气，也指（口语）给……鼓劲。
习语：pump iron 是（口语）举重健身；pump somebody's hand 是使劲跟某人握手；pump bullets into 是朝……连开几枪。
分词 pumped 作形容词：（口语）兴奋的、劲头十足的，也说 pumped up。并入本条。
① The villagers still draw their water from a pump in the square.
= 村民们还是从广场上的一个水泵打水。
a pump = 泵、抽水机
a water pump = 水泵
② I stopped at a petrol pump on the edge of the town to fill the tank.
= 我在城边的一个加油泵前停下来加满油。
a petrol pump = 加油泵
a bicycle pump = 自行车打气筒
③ The children changed into their pumps for the gym lesson.
= 孩子们换上胶底鞋去上体育课。
pumps = （英式）胶底帆布鞋；（美式）浅口高跟鞋
英美指的鞋不一样。
④ The firemen pumped the water out of the cellar after the flood.
= 洪水过后，消防员把地下室里的水抽了出去。
pump water out of something = 把水从某处抽出去
pump something out = 把某物抽出去
⑤ The heart pumps blood round the body about seventy times a minute.
= 心脏每分钟把血泵到全身约七十次。
pump blood = 泵血
pump something round something = 把某物泵到某处各处
⑥ Blood was pumping from a deep cut in his arm.
= 血从他胳膊上一道很深的口子里一股股地往外涌。
blood pumps from something = 血从某处涌出来
这样用不及物。
⑦ The cyclist pumped the pedals as hard as he could on the last hill.
= 最后一道坡上，那个骑车的拼命蹬着脚踏。
pump the pedals = 使劲蹬脚踏
pump the handle = 一上一下地摇把手
⑧ The reporters tried to pump the driver for information about the minister.
= 记者们想从司机嘴里套出部长的消息。
pump somebody for information = 套某人的话
这一支是口语。
⑨ The government has pumped millions of pounds into the railways.
= 政府往铁路上投了几百万英镑。
pump money into something = 往某事上大量投钱
pump into = 大量投入
pump in money = 大量投钱
⑩ The factory pumps out two thousand cars a week.
= 那家工厂一个星期出两千辆车。
pump out = 大量生产、源源不断地送出
⑪ You had better pump up the tyres before you set off.
= 你出发前最好把车胎打足气。
pump up = 给……打气
pump up a tyre = 给车胎打气
⑫ He spends two hours a day at the gym pumping iron.
= 他每天在健身房举两个小时的铁。
pump iron = 举重健身
⑬ The mayor pumped my hand and thanked me for coming.
= 市长使劲握着我的手，谢谢我来。
pump somebody's hand = 使劲跟某人握手
⑭ The players were really pumped before the final and could not sit still.
= 决赛之前球员们兴奋得很，坐都坐不住。
be pumped = 兴奋、劲头十足
pumped up = 劲头十足的
⑮ The government spent heavily on new roads as a form of pump-priming.
= 政府在新公路上大笔花钱，作为刺激经济的手段。
pump-priming = （用政府投资）刺激经济
⑯ Visitors to the old spa town used to drink the waters in the pump room.
= 以前来那座老温泉小镇的游客都在泵房里喝矿泉水。
a pump room = （温泉疗养地的）泵房、饮水厅

pumpernickel
/ˈpʌmpənɪkl/
核心：粗裸麦黑面包：一种德国式的、用粗黑麦粉做的深色面包，口感密实、略带甜味。不可数。
① She served smoked fish on thin slices of pumpernickel.
= 她把熏鱼放在薄薄的黑面包片上端了上来。
pumpernickel = 粗裸麦黑面包
a slice of pumpernickel = 一片黑面包
② The baker sells rye bread and pumpernickel as well as ordinary white loaves.
= 那家面包店除了普通的白面包，也卖黑麦面包和粗裸麦黑面包。
a loaf of pumpernickel = 一条黑面包
是德语借词。

pumpkin
/ˈpʌmpkɪn/
核心：南瓜：一种个头很大的圆形瓜，皮橙黄色，可以做菜、做馅。可数也可不可数。
① The children cut a face in a pumpkin and put a candle inside it.
= 孩子们在南瓜上刻了一张脸，里面放了一支蜡烛。
a pumpkin = 南瓜
cut a face in a pumpkin = 在南瓜上刻一张脸
② We ended the meal with pumpkin pie and cream.
= 那顿饭最后一道是南瓜派加奶油。
pumpkin pie = 南瓜派
pumpkin soup = 南瓜汤

pun
/pʌn/
核心：名词，可数：双关语、谐音的俏皮话：利用一词多义或同音字说的俏皮话。动词：说双关语：pun on。
爱说双关语的人叫 punster。习语 no pun intended 是我可不是有意说双关。并入本条。
① The headline was a pun on the name of the football club.
= 那个标题拿足球俱乐部的名字做了一句双关。
a pun on something = 拿某词做的双关
a pun = 双关语
② He cannot resist making a bad pun whenever fish is served.
= 一上鱼，他就忍不住要说句蹩脚的双关话。
make a pun = 说双关语
a bad pun = 蹩脚的双关
③ The baker said he kneaded the dough, no pun intended.
= 面包师说他「need」面团，又说可不是有意玩双关。
no pun intended = 我可不是有意说双关
④ Shakespeare loved to pun, and even his dying characters play with words.
= 莎士比亚爱说双关话，连他笔下快死的人物都在玩文字游戏。
love to pun = 爱说双关话
pun on something = 拿某词做双关
⑤ My uncle is a dreadful punster who makes the whole table groan.
= 我叔叔是个不可救药的双关迷，说得一桌人直哼哼。
a punster = 爱说双关语的人

punch
/pʌntʃ/
核心：动词：用拳猛击、打一拳；（用工具）在……上打孔：punch a hole in、punch a ticket；按（键、钮）。
名词：一拳，可数；（文字、讲话的）感染力、冲击力，不可数；打孔器，可数；潘趣酒：用酒、果汁、糖、香料调的饮料，不可数。
短语动词：punch in、punch out 是（美式）上班、下班打卡；punch something in 是把（数字）按键输进去；punch somebody out 是（美式口语）把某人打倒。
习语：pull your punches 是手下留情，多用在否定句里；pack a punch 是很有力道、很有冲击力；beat somebody to the punch 是抢在某人前头；
roll with the punches 是顺势应变、逆来顺受；punch above your weight 是做超出自己分量的事；not pull any punches 是毫不留情。
派生与复合：puncher 是打拳的人；punchcard、punched card 是（旧时电脑用的）穿孔卡片。并入本条。
① He punched the man on the nose and ran out of the bar.
= 他照那个人鼻子上打了一拳，跑出了酒吧。
punch somebody on the nose = 照某人鼻子上打一拳
punch somebody in the face = 一拳打在某人脸上
② She punched the air with both fists when her name was read out.
= 念到她的名字时，她双拳挥向空中。
punch the air = 挥拳庆祝
这是高兴、庆祝的动作。
③ The machine punches a row of holes along the edge of each sheet.
= 那台机器在每张纸的边上打一排孔。
punch a hole in something = 在某物上打孔
punch holes = 打孔
④ The inspector punched our tickets and wished us a pleasant journey.
= 检票员给我们的票打了孔，祝我们旅途愉快。
punch a ticket = 给票打孔
⑤ She punched the number into the telephone and waited.
= 她在电话上按下那个号码，等着。
punch a number into something = 把号码按进某物
punch something in = 把某物按键输进去
⑥ He gave the door a hard punch and hurt his hand.
= 他狠狠捶了门一拳，把手伤了。
a hard punch = 重重的一拳
throw a punch = 挥出一拳
⑦ The last chapter of the book lacks the punch of the first.
= 那本书的最后一章没有第一章的那股冲击力。
lack punch = 没有冲击力
这一支不可数。
⑧ Use a punch to make a new hole in the leather belt.
= 用打孔器在皮带上再打一个孔。
a punch = 打孔器
a hole punch = 打孔机
⑨ The hostess served a bowl of hot rum punch to the guests as they arrived.
= 客人到的时候，女主人端上了一大碗热的朗姆潘趣酒。
rum punch = 朗姆潘趣酒
fruit punch = 果汁潘趣
⑩ The workers punch in at seven and punch out at half past three.
= 工人们七点打卡上班，三点半打卡下班。
punch in = 上班打卡
punch out = 下班打卡
⑪ The critic did not pull any punches in her review of the film.
= 那位评论家写那部片子的影评时毫不留情。
not pull any punches = 毫不留情
pull your punches = 手下留情
⑫ The little car has a small engine, but it packs a punch on the motorway.
= 那辆小车发动机不大，上了高速却很有劲。
pack a punch = 很有力道
pack a powerful punch = 劲头十足
⑬ We planned to announce the news on Monday, but a rival beat us to the punch.
= 我们本打算星期一宣布那个消息，却被对手抢了先。
beat somebody to the punch = 抢在某人前头
⑭ A young actor has to learn to roll with the punches and keep working.
= 年轻演员得学会挨了打击照样干下去。
roll with the punches = 顺势应变、逆来顺受
⑮ The small country punches above its weight in international sport.
= 那个小国在国际体育上的成绩超出了它的分量。
punch above your weight = 做超出自己分量的事
⑯ The champion is a heavy puncher who has won most of his fights in three rounds.
= 那位冠军拳头很重，大多数比赛三个回合就赢了。
a heavy puncher = 重拳手
a puncher = 打拳的人
⑰ The first computers read their instructions from stacks of punchcards.
= 最早的电脑是从一叠叠穿孔卡片上读指令的。
a punchcard = 穿孔卡片
a punched card = 穿孔卡片
⑱ Punch the code into the keypad and the door will open.
= 在键盘上按下密码，门就会开。
punch something into something = 把某物按键输进某物
punch into = 按键输入
⑲ Two of the fans got into a punch-up outside the ground after the match.
= 比赛结束后，两个球迷在球场外面打了一架。
a punch-up = （英式口语）打架、斗殴
动词短语 punch somebody up 是揍某人。
⑳ The boxer was disqualified for a rabbit punch to the back of the neck.
= 那名拳击手因为往对手后颈打了一记兔子拳被取消了资格。
a rabbit punch = 兔子拳
打在后颈上的一拳，拳击里是犯规的。

punchbag
/ˈpʌntʃbæɡ/
核心：（英式）（练拳击用的）沙袋、吊袋。可数。也比喻出气筒。也有小些的、装在架子上的球形吊袋，叫 punchball。并入本条。
① He hung a punchbag from a beam in the garage and trained every evening.
= 他在车库的梁上吊了个沙袋，每天晚上练。
a punchbag = 沙袋
② She was tired of being used as a punchbag by her manager whenever sales fell.
= 销量一跌经理就拿她出气，她受够了。
be used as a punchbag = 被当成出气筒
这是比喻的用法。
③ The boxer practised his timing on a punchball in the corner of the gym.
= 那名拳击手在健身房角落的梨球上练出拳的时机。
a punchball = 梨球、拳击吊球

punchbowl
/ˈpʌntʃbəʊl/
核心：潘趣酒碗：盛潘趣酒的大碗，客人自己从里面舀着喝。可数。
① A silver punchbowl stood in the middle of the table with glasses round it.
= 桌子中央摆着一只银潘趣酒碗，四周围着杯子。
a punchbowl = 潘趣酒碗
a silver punchbowl = 银潘趣酒碗
② The guests gathered round the punchbowl and filled their glasses with a ladle.
= 客人们围在潘趣酒碗旁，用长柄勺往杯子里舀酒。
gather round the punchbowl = 围在潘趣酒碗旁

punchline
/ˈpʌntʃlaɪn/
核心：（笑话的）包袱、最后那句逗人笑的话。可数。也写作 punch line。
① He told the joke badly and forgot the punchline at the end.
= 那个笑话他讲得很糟，末了还把包袱给忘了。
the punchline = 包袱、笑点
forget the punchline = 忘了包袱
② Nobody laughed, because she had given away the punchline at the start.
= 没人笑，因为她一开头就把包袱抖出来了。
give away the punchline = 把包袱提前抖出来
the punchline of a joke = 笑话的包袱

punchy
/ˈpʌntʃi/
核心：（口语）（文字、讲话）简短有力的、有冲击力的。
① The advertisement needs a short, punchy slogan that people will remember.
= 那则广告要一句人家记得住的、简短有力的口号。
a punchy slogan = 简短有力的口号
a punchy headline = 有冲击力的标题
② Her speeches are always punchy and never last more than ten minutes.
= 她的讲话向来干脆有力，从不超过十分钟。
be punchy = 干脆有力
short and punchy = 简短有力

punctilious
/pʌŋkˈtɪliəs/
核心：（正式）一丝不苟的、拘泥细节的、礼数周到的：处处留心把事情做得合规矩。常说 punctilious about、in。
副词 punctiliously；名词 punctiliousness，不可数。并入本条。
① He was punctilious about paying his debts on the day they fell due.
= 他还债一丝不苟，到期当天必还。
be punctilious about something = 对某事一丝不苟
be punctilious in doing something = 做某事一丝不苟
② She is a punctilious hostess who writes every place card by hand.
= 她是位礼数周到的女主人，每张座位卡都亲手写。
a punctilious hostess = 礼数周到的女主人
③ The clerk punctiliously recorded every penny that passed through his hands.
= 那个职员把经手的每一个便士都记得一丝不苟。
punctiliously = 一丝不苟地
④ His punctiliousness over small matters of dress amused the younger officers.
= 他对衣着上的小事一丝不苟，年轻军官觉得好笑。
punctiliousness = 一丝不苟、拘泥细节

punctual
/ˈpʌŋktʃuəl/
核心：准时的、守时的：在说好的时间到、做。名词 punctuality，不可数；副词 punctually。并入本条。
① She has been punctual for work every day for twenty years.
= 二十年来她天天准时上班。
be punctual for something = 准时到某事
be punctual = 准时
② The trains in that country are clean, cheap and punctual.
= 那个国家的火车干净、便宜、准点。
a punctual train = 准点的火车
a punctual start = 准时开始
③ The headmaster expects punctuality from the teachers as well as the pupils.
= 校长要求老师跟学生一样守时。
punctuality = 守时、准时
④ The meeting began punctually at ten and ended at noon.
= 会议十点准时开始，中午结束。
punctually at ten = 十点准时
arrive punctually = 准时到

punctuate
/ˈpʌŋktʃueɪt/
核心：两支。一是 punctuate something with、by：不时打断，多用被动；二是给（文字）加标点，及物也可不及物。
① Her speech was punctuated by bursts of applause from the back of the hall.
= 她的讲话不时被大厅后面爆发的掌声打断。
be punctuated by something = 不时被某事打断
be punctuated with something = 夹杂着某物
② The silence of the night was punctuated by the bark of a distant dog.
= 远处的狗叫不时划破夜的寂静。
punctuate the silence = 划破寂静
③ The children are learning to punctuate their sentences correctly.
= 孩子们在学着给句子正确地加标点。
punctuate a sentence = 给句子加标点
punctuate correctly = 标点加得对

punctuation
/ˌpʌŋktʃuˈeɪʃn/
核心：标点、标点符号，也指加标点这件事、标点的用法。不可数。
① The teacher handed back the essay covered in red ink, with every mistake in spelling and punctuation marked.
= 老师把作文发了回来，满纸红墨水，拼写和标点的错一处处都标了出来。
mistakes in punctuation = 标点错误
punctuation = 标点
② The poet insisted that the long poem should be printed without any punctuation at all.
= 诗人坚持那首长诗印出来一个标点也不要。
without punctuation = 不加标点
the rules of punctuation = 标点的规则

puncture
/ˈpʌŋktʃə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：（英式）（车胎上扎出来的）小孔、车胎漏气；（皮肤上的）刺孔。
动词：扎破、刺穿、（车胎）被扎破；比喻一下子打掉（某人的傲气、自信）。
① I had a puncture on the way to work and had to push the bicycle.
= 上班路上我车胎扎了，只好推着自行车走。
have a puncture = 车胎扎了
mend a puncture = 补胎
② The doctor found two small puncture wounds on the child's ankle.
= 医生在孩子的脚踝上发现了两个小小的刺伤口。
a puncture wound = 刺伤的伤口
a puncture = 小孔、刺孔
③ A nail in the road punctured the front tyre of the car.
= 路上的一颗钉子把汽车的前胎扎破了。
puncture a tyre = 扎破车胎
puncture something = 刺穿某物
④ One of his ribs had broken and punctured a lung.
= 他的一根肋骨断了，刺穿了肺。
a punctured lung = 被刺穿的肺
⑤ Her cool reply punctured his confidence in a single sentence.
= 她冷冷的一句回答，一下子把他的自信戳破了。
puncture somebody's confidence = 打掉某人的自信
这是比喻的用法。

pundit
/ˈpʌndɪt/
核心：权威、行家、评论员：对某个题目懂得多、常在电视报纸上发表看法的人。可数。这一行、这类议论总称 punditry，不可数。并入本条。
① The political pundits were all wrong about the result of the election.
= 政治评论家们对选举结果全都看走了眼。
a political pundit = 政治评论家
a pundit = 权威、评论员
② He left the game at thirty-five and became a football pundit on television.
= 他三十五岁退役，当了电视上的足球评论员。
a football pundit = 足球评论员
③ The newspapers were full of punditry about what the minister would do next.
= 报上满是猜部长下一步怎么走的议论。
punditry = 评论家的议论
有时带点讥讽：好发议论的人。

pungent
/ˈpʌndʒənt/
核心：两支。一是（气味、味道）刺鼻的、辛辣的、冲的；二是（言辞、文字）尖锐的、辛辣的、一针见血的。
名词 pungency，不可数；副词 pungently。并入本条。
① The pungent smell of frying onions drifted up from the kitchen.
= 煎洋葱的冲鼻气味从厨房飘了上来。
a pungent smell = 刺鼻的气味
a pungent taste = 辛辣的味道
② The article is a pungent attack on the way the city is governed.
= 那篇文章辛辣地抨击了那座城市的治理方式。
a pungent attack = 辛辣的抨击
pungent remarks = 尖锐的话
③ The cheese is famous for the pungency of its smell.
= 那种奶酪以气味冲出名。
pungency = 刺鼻、辛辣
④ She commented pungently on the quality of the food at the hotel.
= 她对那家旅馆的饭菜质量作了辛辣的评论。
pungently = 辛辣地

punish
/ˈpʌnɪʃ/
核心：及物。一是惩罚、处罚：punish somebody for something、for doing；二是对（一种罪行）处以刑罚：punish theft with prison；
三是 punish yourself：责备自己、跟自己过不去。
形容词 punishable 是该罚的、可处……的：punishable by；punishing 是累死人的、极其辛苦的。并入本条。
① The teacher punished the whole class for the noise in the corridor.
= 走廊里吵，老师把全班都罚了。
punish somebody for something = 因某事惩罚某人
punish somebody = 惩罚某人
② He was punished for lying to his parents about where he had been.
= 他跟父母谎报去了哪里，受了罚。
be punished for doing something = 因做某事受罚
③ In those days, stealing a sheep was punished with death.
= 那年头，偷一只羊要处死。
punish something with something = 对某罪处以某刑
be punished with death = 被处死
④ Stop punishing yourself, for the accident was not your fault.
= 别再跟自己过不去了，那起事故不是你的错。
punish yourself = 责备自己、跟自己过不去
⑤ Driving without insurance is punishable by a heavy fine.
= 无保险驾车可处高额罚款。
punishable by something = 可处某种刑罚的
a punishable offence = 该罚的过错
⑥ The team has a punishing schedule of five matches in twelve days.
= 那支球队的赛程累死人：十二天打五场。
a punishing schedule = 累死人的日程
punishing = 极其辛苦的

punishment
/ˈpʌnɪʃmənt/
核心：两支。一是惩罚、处罚、刑罚，可数也可不可数：punishment for；二是（口语）粗暴的对待、折腾，不可数：take a lot of punishment。
capital punishment 是死刑；corporal punishment 是体罚。
① The punishment for cheating in the examination is to fail the whole course.
= 考试作弊的处罚是整门课不及格。
the punishment for something = 对某事的处罚
a punishment = 一种处罚
② He was sent to his room as a punishment for being rude to his aunt.
= 他对姑妈没礼貌，作为惩罚被打发回自己屋里。
as a punishment = 作为惩罚
escape punishment = 逃脱惩罚
③ The country abolished capital punishment more than fifty years ago.
= 那个国家五十多年前就废除了死刑。
capital punishment = 死刑
corporal punishment = 体罚
④ These boots can take a lot of punishment on rough ground.
= 这双靴子在高低不平的地上很经得起折腾。
take a lot of punishment = 很经得起折腾
这一支是口语，不可数。

punitive
/ˈpjuːnətɪv/
核心：（正式）两支，多放在名词前。一是惩罚性的、处罚的；二是（税、价钱）苛刻的、重得让人吃不消的。副词 punitively，并入本条。
① The government sent a punitive expedition against the rebels in the hills.
= 政府派了一支讨伐队去打山里的叛军。
a punitive expedition = 讨伐队
punitive action = 惩罚性的行动
② The court awarded punitive damages of a million dollars against the firm.
= 法院判那家公司赔一百万美元的惩罚性赔偿。
punitive damages = 惩罚性赔偿
punitive measures = 惩罚措施
③ Small shops complain about the punitive taxes they have to pay.
= 小店抱怨它们得交的税重得吃不消。
punitive taxes = 重得吃不消的税
④ The new tax falls punitively on families with only one wage.
= 新税对只有一份工资的家庭重得出奇。
punitively = 惩罚性地、重得出奇地

Punjabi
/pʌnˈdʒɑːbi/
核心：名词：旁遮普语：印度西北部和巴基斯坦旁遮普一带的语言，不可数；旁遮普人，可数。形容词：旁遮普的、旁遮普人的、旁遮普语的。首字母大写。
① Her grandparents speak Punjabi at home and English in the shop.
= 她祖父母在家说旁遮普语，在店里说英语。
speak Punjabi = 说旁遮普语
Punjabi = 旁遮普语
② The wedding was a traditional Punjabi one, with three days of music and dancing.
= 那场婚礼是传统的旁遮普式婚礼，音乐舞蹈连着三天。
a Punjabi wedding = 旁遮普式婚礼
a Punjabi = 旁遮普人

punk
/pʌŋk/
核心：名词：朋克摇滚：一九七〇年代末兴起的一种又吵又快、带反叛劲头的音乐，不可数，也说 punk rock；朋克青年：爱这种音乐、
头发染得鲜艳、衣服上别着别针的人，可数，也说 punk rocker；（美式口语）小流氓、小混混，可数。
形容词：朋克的。punky 是朋克风格的。并入本条。
① The band started out playing punk in the clubs of north London.
= 那支乐队是在伦敦北部的俱乐部里唱朋克起家的。
play punk = 演奏朋克
punk rock = 朋克摇滚
② A group of punks with green hair sat on the steps of the fountain.
= 一群绿头发的朋克青年坐在喷泉的台阶上。
a punk = 朋克青年
a punk rocker = 朋克青年
③ Some young punk tried to steal my wallet on the subway.
= 有个小混混在地铁上想偷我的钱包。
a young punk = 小混混
这一支是美式口语。
④ She cut her hair short and dyed it pink for a punky look.
= 她把头发剪短染成粉红色，弄了个朋克范儿。
punky = 朋克风格的
a punky look = 朋克范儿

punkah
/ˈpʌŋkə/
核心：（旧时印度的）布屏风扇：吊在天花板上的一大块布，由仆人拉绳子来回扇动。可数。
① A servant sat outside the door pulling the cord of the punkah all afternoon.
= 一个仆人坐在门外，拉了一下午布屏风扇的绳子。
a punkah = 布屏风扇
② The punkah swung slowly above the dining table and stirred the hot air.
= 布屏风扇在餐桌上方慢慢摆动，搅着热空气。
the punkah swings = 布屏风扇摆动
多见于写殖民时代印度的书里。

punnet
/ˈpʌnɪt/
核心：（英式）（装草莓等软水果的）小篮、小盒。可数。
① I bought two punnets of strawberries from a stall by the side of the road.
= 我在路边的摊子上买了两小盒草莓。
a punnet of strawberries = 一小盒草莓
a punnet = 小篮、小盒
② The raspberries are sold in plastic punnets with a sheet of paper underneath.
= 覆盆子装在垫了一张纸的塑料小盒里卖。
a plastic punnet = 塑料小盒
这是英式说法。

punt
/pʌnt/
核心：名词，可数：（用篙撑的）方头平底船；（英式口语）（赌钱的）一注；（橄榄球、美式足球）凌空踢出的一脚。
动词：撑方头平底船、坐这种船游河；凌空踢（球）；（英式口语）下注。撑船游河这项消遣叫 punting，不可数。并入本条。
习语 take a punt on 是（英式口语）在……上赌一把、碰碰运气。
① We hired a punt for the afternoon and drifted down the river.
= 我们租了一条平底船，顺河漂了一下午。
hire a punt = 租平底船
a punt = 方头平底船
② The students punted up the river to a pub for lunch.
= 学生们撑船沿河而上，到一家酒馆吃午饭。
punt up the river = 撑船沿河而上
go punting = 去撑船游河
③ Punting on the river is one of the pleasures of a summer in Cambridge.
= 在河上撑船是在剑桥过夏天的一大乐事。
punting = 撑船游河
④ The goalkeeper punted the ball deep into the other half of the field.
= 守门员一脚凌空把球踢到对方半场的深处。
punt the ball = 凌空踢球
a punt = 凌空的一脚
⑤ The firm took a punt on a young designer nobody had heard of.
= 那家公司在一个没人听说过的年轻设计师身上赌了一把。
take a punt on something = 在某事上赌一把
这是英式口语。

punter
/ˈpʌntə(r)/
核心：（英式口语）两支，可数。一是顾客、主顾；二是赌客、赌马的人。
① The new bar is trying to attract younger punters with live music.
= 那家新酒吧想用现场音乐招揽年轻些的顾客。
attract punters = 招揽顾客
the punters = 顾客们
② Most punters lost their money when the favourite fell at the last fence.
= 大热门在最后一道栏摔了，大多数赌客都赔了钱。
a punter = 赌客
这是英式口语。

puny
/ˈpjuːni/
核心：（含贬义）两支。一是瘦小的、弱不禁风的；二是微不足道的、不顶用的、可怜巴巴的。比较级 punier。
① He was a puny child who was always ill in the winter.
= 他小时候瘦弱，一到冬天就生病。
a puny child = 瘦弱的孩子
puny arms = 细瘦的胳膊
② The firm made a puny attempt to answer its critics.
= 那家公司对批评作了一点微不足道的回应。
a puny attempt = 微不足道的努力
a puny sum = 可怜巴巴的一点钱

pup
/pʌp/
核心：两支，可数。一是小狗，等于 puppy；二是（海豹、水獭、狼等的）幼崽。习语 sell somebody a pup 是（英式旧式口语）卖给某人不值钱的东西、让某人上当。
① The farmer's dog had six pups in the barn last night.
= 农夫的狗昨晚在谷仓里下了六只小狗。
a pup = 小狗
have pups = 下小狗
② We watched a seal pup lying on the rocks beside its mother.
= 我们看着一只小海豹躺在母亲身旁的礁石上。
a seal pup = 小海豹
a wolf pup = 小狼
③ The car broke down within a week, and I knew that I had been sold a pup.
= 那辆车不到一个星期就坏了，我知道自己上当了。
sell somebody a pup = 让某人上当
be sold a pup = 上当、买了不值钱的东西
④ We slept in a small pup tent on the side of the hill.
= 我们睡在山坡上的一顶小帐篷里。
a pup tent = 小帐篷
只够一两个人睡的那种。

pupa
/ˈpjuːpə/
核心：蛹：昆虫从幼虫变成成虫中间那个不动的阶段。可数，复数 pupae，读 /ˈpjuːpiː/。
形容词 pupal；动词 pupate 是化蛹。并入本条。
① The pupa of the moth lies in the soil through the winter.
= 那种蛾子的蛹在土里过冬。
a pupa = 蛹
the pupa of a moth = 蛾子的蛹
② The butterfly comes out of the pupa after about two weeks.
= 蝴蝶大约两个星期后从蛹里出来。
come out of the pupa = 破蛹而出
③ The insect spends the pupal stage hidden under the bark of a tree.
= 那种昆虫的蛹期是藏在树皮底下度过的。
the pupal stage = 蛹期
pupal = 蛹的
④ The caterpillars stop feeding and pupate at the end of the summer.
= 毛虫在夏末停止进食，化成蛹。
pupate = 化蛹

pupil
/ˈpjuːpl/
核心：三支，可数。一是（英式）（中小学的）学生；二是（跟名师学艺的）弟子、门生；三是瞳孔：眼睛中间那个黑色的圆孔。
（英国）pupillage 是见习大律师的实习期，不可数，也可数。并入本条。
① The school has four hundred pupils between the ages of five and eleven.
= 那所学校有四百名五到十一岁的学生。
a pupil = 学生
a pupil at a school = 某校的学生
② The painter was a pupil of Rembrandt and worked in his studio for six years.
= 那位画家是伦勃朗的弟子，在他的画室里干了六年。
a pupil of somebody = 某人的弟子
③ The pupils of your eyes grow larger in the dark to let in more light.
= 在暗处，瞳孔会放大，好多进些光。
the pupils of your eyes = 瞳孔
a pupil = 瞳孔
④ She did her pupillage in a set of chambers in London.
= 她在伦敦的一家大律师事务所做的见习。
pupillage = 见习大律师的实习期
美式说 student，不说 pupil。

puppet
/ˈpʌpɪt/
核心：两支，可数。一是木偶、布袋偶：用线牵着、或套在手上操纵的小人；二是（含贬义）傀儡：受别人操纵的人、政府。
操纵木偶的人叫 puppeteer；木偶戏这门艺术叫 puppetry，不可数。并入本条。
① The children watched a puppet show in the park on Sunday afternoon.
= 星期天下午孩子们在公园里看了一场木偶戏。
a puppet show = 木偶戏
a puppet = 木偶
② She made a glove puppet out of an old sock and two buttons.
= 她用一只旧袜子和两颗扣子做了个布袋偶。
a glove puppet = 布袋偶
a string puppet = 提线木偶
③ The prince was only a puppet of the generals who had put him on the throne.
= 那位亲王不过是把他扶上王位的那些将军的傀儡。
a puppet of somebody = 某人的傀儡
a puppet government = 傀儡政府
④ The puppeteer stood hidden behind a black curtain above the little stage.
= 操纵木偶的人藏在小戏台上方的黑幕后面。
a puppeteer = 操纵木偶的人
⑤ He learned the art of puppetry from his grandfather in Prague.
= 他是在布拉格跟爷爷学的木偶戏。
puppetry = 木偶戏艺术

puppy
/ˈpʌpi/
核心：小狗、幼犬。可数，复数 puppies。也说 pup。
① The puppy chewed one of my slippers and hid the other under the sofa.
= 小狗咬坏了我一只拖鞋，把另一只藏到了沙发底下。
a puppy = 小狗
a litter of puppies = 一窝小狗
② She followed her older brother everywhere like a puppy.
= 她像只小狗似的，哥哥走到哪儿跟到哪儿。
like a puppy = 像只小狗似的
a puppy dog = 小狗（儿语）

purchase
/ˈpɜːtʃəs/
核心：（正式）动词：购买、购置。名词：购买，不可数，也可数；买的东西，可数；（正式）抓得牢、踩得稳，不可数或单数：get a purchase on。
买主叫 purchaser；purchasing 是采购，不可数。并入本条。
① The museum purchased the painting from a private collector for two million pounds.
= 那家博物馆花两百万英镑从一位私人收藏家手里买下了那幅画。
purchase something from somebody = 从某人那里买下某物
purchase something = 购买某物
② Tickets may be purchased in advance from the box office.
= 票可以提前在售票处买。
be purchased in advance = 提前购买
比 buy 正式，多见于告示、合同。
③ Keep the receipt as proof of purchase in case you need to return the goods.
= 把收据留着当购物凭证，万一要退货用得着。
proof of purchase = 购物凭证
the date of purchase = 购买日期
④ The house was the most expensive purchase that they had ever made.
= 那栋房子是他们买过的最贵的东西。
make a purchase = 买东西
a purchase = 买的东西
⑤ She carried her purchases out to the car in two large bags.
= 她把买的东西装了两大袋，拎到车上。
her purchases = 她买的东西
⑥ The climber could not get a purchase on the wet rock with his boots.
= 登山者的靴子在湿岩石上踩不稳。
get a purchase on something = 在某物上抓牢、踩稳
这一支是正式用法。
⑦ The purchaser must pay a tenth of the price on signing the contract.
= 买方签合同时须付十分之一的价款。
the purchaser = 买方
a purchaser = 买主
⑧ He is head of purchasing for a chain of hotels in the north.
= 他是北方一家连锁旅馆的采购主管。
purchasing = 采购
head of purchasing = 采购主管
⑨ Purchase tax on luxury goods was replaced by VAT in nineteen seventy-three.
= 奢侈品的购买税在一九七三年被增值税取代了。
purchase tax = （英国旧时的）购买税

purdah
/ˈpɜːdə/
核心：深闺制度：某些穆斯林、印度教社会里妇女不见外人的习俗，靠住在单独的屋子里、遮住脸来做到。不可数。常说 in purdah。
也比喻闭门不出、与外界隔绝的一段时间。
① The women of the family lived in purdah and never went to the market.
= 那家的女眷深居闺中，从不去集市。
in purdah = 深居闺中
purdah = 深闺制度
② The minister has gone into purdah until the budget is announced next week.
= 预算下星期公布以前，那位部长闭门谢客了。
go into purdah = 闭门不出
这是比喻的用法，英国政界常说。

pure
/pjʊə(r)/
核心：形容词。一是纯的、不掺别的东西的：pure gold；二是纯净的、干净的、没有污染的；三是完全的、纯粹的，只放在名词前：pure chance；
四是（颜色、声音、光）纯正的、清澈的；五是纯洁的、清白的；六是（学科）纯理论的，只放在名词前：pure mathematics，对着说的是 applied；
七是（血统）纯的。习语 pure and simple 是不折不扣、就这么简单，放在名词后面。
① The ring is made of pure gold and weighs twenty grams.
= 那枚戒指是纯金的，重二十克。
pure gold = 纯金
pure wool = 纯羊毛
② The water from the spring is so pure that you can drink it straight away.
= 那眼泉水很纯净，可以直接喝。
pure water = 纯净的水
pure air = 洁净的空气
③ We met again by pure chance at an airport ten years later.
= 十年后我们纯属偶然地在机场又碰上了。
by pure chance = 纯属偶然
pure luck = 纯粹是运气
④ The boy sang the solo in a high, pure voice.
= 那个男孩用清亮的高音唱了独唱。
a pure voice = 清亮的嗓音
a pure white = 纯白
⑤ The saint is said to have been pure in thought and deed.
= 据说那位圣徒心思行为都清白无瑕。
pure in thought = 心思清白
pure in heart = 心地纯洁
⑥ She gave up pure mathematics for a job in a bank.
= 她放下纯数学，去银行上了班。
pure mathematics = 纯数学
pure science = 纯科学
⑦ It was jealousy, pure and simple, that made him write that letter.
= 让他写那封信的，就是嫉妒，不折不扣的嫉妒。
pure and simple = 不折不扣、就这么简单
放在所说的名词后面。
⑧ The dogs are pure Labradors, with papers to prove it.
= 那些狗是纯种的拉布拉多，有证书为凭。
a pure Labrador = 纯种拉布拉多
名词是 purity。
⑨ The farmer breeds pure-bred cattle that win prizes at every show.
= 那个农民养的纯种牛每次展会都得奖。
pure-bred = 纯种的
也说 purebred。

purée
/ˈpjʊəreɪ/
核心：名词：（水果、蔬菜煮烂后打成的）泥、酱，可数也可不可数。动词：把……打成泥。过去式 puréed。也拼作 puree。
① Stir two spoonfuls of tomato purée into the sauce and let it simmer.
= 往酱汁里搅进两勺番茄酱，小火慢炖。
tomato purée = 番茄酱
a purée = 泥、酱
② The baby is now eating apple purée and mashed banana.
= 那个婴儿现在吃苹果泥和香蕉泥了。
apple purée = 苹果泥
③ Purée the cooked carrots with a little butter until they are smooth.
= 把煮熟的胡萝卜加一点黄油打成细腻的泥。
purée something = 把某物打成泥
是法语借词。

purely
/ˈpjʊəli/
核心：完全地、纯粹地、仅仅：只因为、只涉及这一样，没有别的。副词。习语 purely and simply 是完完全全、不为别的。
① I saw the letter purely by chance when I was looking for a stamp.
= 我是找邮票时纯属偶然看到那封信的。
purely by chance = 纯属偶然
purely = 完全地、仅仅
② The decision was taken for purely financial reasons.
= 作那个决定纯粹是出于钱上的考虑。
for purely financial reasons = 纯粹出于钱上的考虑
purely personal = 纯属个人的
③ She took the job purely and simply because it was near her home.
= 她接那份工作不为别的，就因为离家近。
purely and simply = 完完全全、不为别的

purgative
/ˈpɜːɡətɪv/
核心：名词，可数：泻药：让人排便的药，比 laxative 猛。形容词：通便的、致泻的。
① The old doctor gave the patient a strong purgative and sent him to bed.
= 老大夫给病人下了一剂猛泻药，打发他去睡。
a purgative = 泻药
a strong purgative = 猛泻药
② The berries have a purgative effect and should not be eaten raw.
= 那种浆果吃了会泻肚，不可生吃。
a purgative effect = 致泻的作用
是较老派的医学用词。

purgatory
/ˈpɜːɡətri/
核心：两支，不可数。一是 Purgatory：（天主教教义里的）炼狱：人死后灵魂受苦涤罪、之后才进天堂的地方或状态；
二是（口语，或玩笑）受罪的地方、活受罪。
① The priest spoke of the souls in Purgatory who needed the prayers of the living.
= 神父讲到炼狱里需要活人为之祈祷的灵魂。
in Purgatory = 在炼狱里
Purgatory = 炼狱
② Sitting through four hours of speeches was sheer purgatory.
= 坐着听四个小时的讲话，简直是活受罪。
be purgatory = 活受罪
sheer purgatory = 简直是活受罪

purge
/pɜːdʒ/
核心：动词：清洗、清除（组织里不要的人）：purge A of B、purge B from A；purge yourself、somebody of 是（正式）涤除、洗去（坏念头、罪过）。
名词，可数：（政治上的）清洗、清除。
① The new leader purged the party of everyone who had opposed him.
= 新领袖把党内反对过他的人统统清洗了。
purge A of B = 把乙从甲里清除
purge the party = 清洗党
② Hundreds of officers were purged from the army after the failed coup.
= 政变失败后，好几百名军官被清出了军队。
be purged from something = 被从某处清除
purge somebody from something = 把某人从某处清除
③ He went on a long walk to purge himself of his anger.
= 他去走了很长的路，好把怒气消掉。
purge yourself of something = 涤除某种情绪
这一支是正式用法。
④ Thousands died in the purges of the nineteen thirties.
= 成千上万的人死于一九三〇年代的大清洗。
a purge = 清洗
a purge of somebody = 对某些人的清洗

purify
/ˈpjʊərɪfaɪ/
核心：及物。一是净化、提纯：把脏东西、杂质去掉；二是（宗教上）使洁净、涤罪：purify somebody of；三是（术语）提炼、精制。
名词 purification，不可数；purifier 是净化器，可数。并入本条。
① The tablets are used to purify water from rivers before it is drunk.
= 那种药片用来净化河水，然后才能喝。
purify water = 净化水
purify something = 净化某物
② The pilgrims bathe in the river to purify themselves of their sins.
= 朝圣者在那条河里沐浴，洗去罪孽。
purify yourself of something = 洗去某种罪过
这一支是宗教上的说法。
③ The gold is purified by heating it until the other metals burn away.
= 金子是靠加热到别的金属都烧掉来提纯的。
be purified = 被提纯
④ The town has built a new plant for the purification of its drinking water.
= 那座城新建了一座净化饮用水的厂子。
the purification of water = 水的净化
water purification = 水的净化
⑤ They bought an air purifier for the baby's room during the smoky season.
= 烟雾重的那个季节，他们给婴儿的房间买了一台空气净化器。
an air purifier = 空气净化器
a water purifier = 净水器

Purim
/ˈpʊərɪm/
核心：普珥节：犹太教的一个节日，在早春，纪念古波斯的犹太人免遭屠杀。不可数。首字母大写。
① The children dress up in costumes and give out sweets at Purim.
= 普珥节时孩子们穿上戏装分发糖果。
at Purim = 在普珥节
Purim = 普珥节
② The story of Queen Esther is read aloud in the synagogue on the evening of Purim.
= 普珥节当晚，犹太会堂里要朗读以斯帖王后的故事。
the evening of Purim = 普珥节当晚

purist
/ˈpjʊərɪst/
核心：纯粹主义者、讲究正统的人：认为语言、艺术、做事都该照老规矩来、不容走样的人。可数。这种态度叫 purism，不可数。并入本条。
① Purists object to the use of "hopefully" at the beginning of a sentence.
= 讲究正统的人反对把「hopefully」用在句子开头。
a purist = 讲究正统的人
purists object to something = 讲究正统的人反对某事
② A jazz purist would not call this music jazz at all.
= 爵士乐的正统派根本不会管这种音乐叫爵士。
a jazz purist = 爵士乐的正统派
③ His purism about grammar makes him a difficult man to write for.
= 他在语法上的洁癖让人很难给他写稿子。
purism = 纯粹主义、讲究正统

puritan
/ˈpjʊərɪtən/
核心：名词，可数：（多含贬义）清教徒式的人：道德观念极严、认为享乐不对的人；Puritan 是清教徒：十六、十七世纪英国主张简化教会仪式的新教徒。
形容词：清教徒的、清教徒式的。puritanical 是（含贬义）清教徒式的、道德上极严苛的；puritanism 是清教徒式的严苛、Puritanism 是清教主义，不可数。并入本条。
① He is a puritan who thinks that dancing on a Sunday is wicked.
= 他是个清教徒式的人，认为星期天跳舞是罪过。
a puritan = 清教徒式的人
② The Puritans sailed to America in search of freedom to worship as they chose.
= 清教徒乘船去美洲，寻求照自己的方式礼拜的自由。
the Puritans = 清教徒
首字母大写指历史上的那一派。
③ She was brought up in a puritan household where cards and novels were forbidden.
= 她生长在一个清教徒式的家庭，纸牌和小说都不许碰。
a puritan household = 清教徒式的家庭
④ Her father had a puritanical attitude to drink and never allowed it in the house.
= 她父亲对酒的态度严得像清教徒，从不许家里有酒。
a puritanical attitude = 清教徒式的态度
puritanical = 道德上极严苛的
⑤ The book describes the puritanism of the small town where he grew up.
= 那本书写的是他长大的那座小城里的清教徒式风气。
puritanism = 清教徒式的严苛

purity
/ˈpjʊərəti/
核心：纯净、纯度、纯洁：没有掺杂、没有污染、没有邪念的状态。不可数。反义 impurity。
① The laboratory tests the purity of the water in the reservoir every week.
= 实验室每个星期检测水库的水的纯度。
the purity of water = 水的纯度
purity = 纯净、纯度
② The white lily is used in paintings as a symbol of purity.
= 白百合在绘画里用作纯洁的象征。
a symbol of purity = 纯洁的象征
moral purity = 道德上的纯洁

purl
/pɜːl/
核心：（编织）名词：反针、上针：一种织法，不可数。动词：织反针。对着说的是 plain、knit（正针、下针）。
① Knit one row and purl the next until the scarf is long enough.
= 一行织正针，下一行织反针，直到围巾够长。
purl a row = 织一行反针
purl = 织反针
② The pattern is made up of two plain stitches followed by two purl.
= 那个花样是两针正针接两针反针。
a purl stitch = 反针
knit one, purl one = 一针正一针反

purlieus
/ˈpɜːljuːz/
核心：（文学）近郊、周围一带、常去的地方。复数名词。
① The poet spent his last years in the quiet purlieus of the cathedral.
= 那位诗人在大教堂周围清静的一带度过了晚年。
the purlieus of something = 某处周围一带
② Few visitors ever leave the purlieus of the main square.
= 没几个游客会走出主广场那一带。
leave the purlieus of something = 走出某处一带
是书面语，如今少见。

purloin
/pɜːˈlɔɪn/
核心：（正式，或玩笑）偷窃、顺手牵羊、私自拿走。及物。
① He had purloined a bottle of brandy from his father's cupboard.
= 他从父亲的橱柜里偷了一瓶白兰地。
purloin something from somebody = 从某人那里偷某物
purloin something = 偷窃某物
② The pen on my desk was purloined from a hotel in Vienna.
= 我桌上那支笔是从维也纳的一家旅馆顺来的。
be purloined from somewhere = 是从某处顺来的
多带玩笑口气；日常说 steal、pinch。

purple
/ˈpɜːpl/
核心：形容词：紫色的；（脸）气得、憋得发紫的；（文字）辞藻华丽的、雕琢过头的：purple prose。名词：紫色，不可数，也可数。
带点紫的说 purplish。并入本条。
① She wore a purple scarf with a grey coat to the wedding.
= 她穿着灰外套、围着紫围巾去参加了婚礼。
a purple scarf = 紫围巾
purple = 紫色的
② His face went purple with rage when he read the letter.
= 他读那封信时气得脸都紫了。
go purple with rage = 气得脸发紫
purple in the face = 脸发紫
③ The novel is spoiled by long passages of purple prose about sunsets.
= 那部小说被大段大段写落日的华丽文字糟蹋了。
purple prose = 辞藻华丽的文字
a purple passage = 雕琢过头的一段文字
④ The hills were covered in the purple of the heather in August.
= 八月里山上铺满了石南花的紫色。
the purple of something = 某物的紫色
deep purple = 深紫色
⑤ The bruise on her arm had turned a purplish colour by the morning.
= 到早上，她胳膊上那块瘀伤已经变得发紫了。
purplish = 带点紫的、发紫的

purport
/pəˈpɔːt/（动）/ˈpɜːpɔːt/（名）
核心：（正式）动词：声称、自称、据说是：purport to be、purport to do，常含「未必是真的」的意思。名词：大意、主旨，不可数。
分词 purported 作形容词：据称的、所谓的，只放在名词前；副词 purportedly。并入本条。
① The document purports to be a letter written by the queen herself.
= 那份文件自称是女王亲笔写的信。
purport to be something = 自称是某物
purport to do something = 声称做某事
② The book purports to tell the true story of the robbery.
= 那本书声称讲的是那起抢劫案的真相。
purport to tell the truth = 声称讲的是真相
常含怀疑：未必真是那么回事。
③ I could not follow every word, but the purport of the speech was clear.
= 我没能字字听懂，但那篇讲话的大意是清楚的。
the purport of something = 某事的大意
这一支是名词，重音在前。
④ The police are examining the purported confession found in his room.
= 警方正在查验在他房间里找到的那份据称是供词的东西。
purported = 据称的、所谓的
⑤ The painting was purportedly bought in Paris before the war.
= 那幅画据称是战前在巴黎买的。
purportedly = 据称

purpose
/ˈpɜːpəs/
核心：名词：目的、意图、用途，可数：the purpose of、for the purpose of；purposes 是（某种）需要、用途，复数：for tax purposes；
（人生的）意义、目标，不可数：a sense of purpose；决心、意志，不可数。
习语：on purpose 是故意地；to no purpose 是（正式）白白地、毫无结果；serve a purpose 是有用、派得上用场；
for all practical purposes 是实际上、事实上，也说 to all intents and purposes；at cross purposes 是各说各的、互相误会；
for the purposes of 是为了……、就……而言；to little purpose 是没什么用。
派生：purposeful 是有目标的、坚定的，副词 purposefully，名词 purposefulness；purposeless 是没有目的的、无意义的；
purposely 是故意地；purposive 是（正式）有目的的。并入本条。
① The purpose of the meeting is to choose a new chairman.
= 开这个会的目的是选一位新主席。
the purpose of something = 某事的目的
a purpose = 目的、用途
② The building is no longer used for its original purpose.
= 那栋楼已经不作原先的用途了。
its original purpose = 原先的用途
for this purpose = 为此
③ He went to Paris for the purpose of buying paintings for the gallery.
= 他去巴黎是为了给画廊买画。
for the purpose of doing something = 为了做某事
④ The house is treated as an office for tax purposes.
= 为报税起见，那栋房子算作办公室。
for tax purposes = 为报税起见
for the purposes of something = 为了某事、就某事而言
⑤ Since she retired, she has lost her sense of purpose.
= 退休以后，她觉得日子没了奔头。
a sense of purpose = 目标感、奔头
give somebody a purpose = 给某人一个奔头
⑥ She walked into the room with an air of great purpose.
= 她一副打定主意的样子走进屋来。
with purpose = 坚决地
这一支说的是决心，不可数。
⑦ He broke the window on purpose to get into the house.
= 他是故意砸窗户好进屋的。
on purpose = 故意地
反过来是 by accident。
⑧ We argued with the manager for an hour, but to no purpose.
= 我们跟经理争了一个小时，却毫无结果。
to no purpose = 白白地、毫无结果
to little purpose = 没什么用
⑨ The old shed is ugly, but it serves a purpose in the winter.
= 那间旧棚子难看，可是冬天派得上用场。
serve a purpose = 有用、派得上用场
serve no purpose = 毫无用处
⑩ The two towns have grown together and are, for all practical purposes, one city.
= 那两座城已经连成一片，实际上就是一座城。
for all practical purposes = 实际上、事实上
to all intents and purposes = 实际上
⑪ We were talking at cross purposes, for I meant the son and she meant the father.
= 我们各说各的：我说的是儿子，她说的是父亲。
at cross purposes = 各说各的、互相误会
⑫ She walked with a purposeful stride towards the manager's office.
= 她迈着坚定的步子朝经理办公室走去。
purposeful = 有目标的、坚定的
a purposeful stride = 坚定的步子
⑬ He moved purposefully through the crowd towards the stage.
= 他目标明确地穿过人群朝舞台走去。
purposefully = 目标明确地、坚定地
⑭ There was a new purposefulness about the team after the change of manager.
= 换了主教练之后，那支球队有了一股新的劲头。
purposefulness = 目标明确、坚定
⑮ The film is full of purposeless violence that adds nothing to the story.
= 那部片子里满是无谓的暴力，对故事毫无帮助。
purposeless = 没有目的的、无谓的
⑯ He purposely left the door unlocked so that she could get in.
= 他故意没锁门，好让她进得来。
purposely = 故意地
⑰ The study treats play as a purposive activity and not as a waste of time.
= 那项研究把玩耍看成有目的的活动，而不是浪费时间。
purposive = 有目的的
这是正式、学术用词。

purr
/pɜː(r)/
核心：动词：（猫）发出呼噜声；（机器）发出低沉平稳的轰鸣；（人）轻柔而满足地说。名词，可数：呼噜声、低沉的轰鸣。
① The cat curled up on my knee and began to purr.
= 猫蜷在我膝盖上，打起了呼噜。
a cat purrs = 猫打呼噜
purr = 发出呼噜声
② The big car purred along the empty road towards the coast.
= 那辆大车低声轰鸣着，沿空荡荡的公路朝海边开去。
purr along the road = 低声轰鸣着沿路开
an engine purrs = 发动机平稳地响
③ "What a lovely surprise," she purred, taking the flowers from him.
= 「真是个惊喜。」她接过他手里的花，娇声说道。
purr = 轻柔而满足地说
④ I could hear the purr of the cat from the other side of the room.
= 我在屋子另一头都听得见猫的呼噜声。
the purr of a cat = 猫的呼噜声
the purr of an engine = 发动机低沉的声音

purse
/pɜːs/
核心：名词，可数：（英式）（女用的）钱包：装硬币、钞票的小包；（美式）女用手提包，英式叫 handbag；（一个机构能花的）资金、财力，单数：the public purse；
（拳击等比赛的）奖金。动词：purse your lips 是噘起嘴、抿紧嘴，表示不赞成。
习语 hold the purse strings 是掌管钱财、管着钱袋子。
① She took a few coins out of her purse and gave them to the child.
= 她从钱包里掏出几枚硬币给了孩子。
a purse = （英式）钱包；（美式）女用手提包
take something out of your purse = 从钱包里掏出某物
② She put her keys in her purse and slung it over her shoulder.
= 她把钥匙放进手提包，把包往肩上一挎。
put something in your purse = 把某物放进手提包
这一支是美式的意思。
③ The repairs to the bridge will be paid for out of the public purse.
= 修那座桥的钱由公款出。
the public purse = 公款、国库
这一支用单数。
④ The two boxers will fight for a purse of five million dollars.
= 那两名拳击手要争夺五百万美元的奖金。
a purse of five million dollars = 五百万美元的奖金
⑤ The old lady pursed her lips and said nothing when she saw the mess.
= 老太太看见那一片狼藉，抿紧嘴唇，什么也没说。
purse your lips = 噘起嘴、抿紧嘴
表示不赞成、不高兴。
⑥ In that family it is the grandmother who holds the purse strings.
= 那一家管钱的是奶奶。
hold the purse strings = 掌管钱财
tighten the purse strings = 紧缩开支

purser
/ˈpɜːsə(r)/
核心：（船上的）事务长：管钱、管乘客起居事务的官员；也指（客机上的）乘务长。可数。
① The purser gave us the keys to our cabin and a plan of the ship.
= 事务长把舱房钥匙和一张全船平面图交给了我们。
the purser = 事务长
② Passengers may leave their valuables with the purser during the voyage.
= 航行期间乘客可以把贵重物品交事务长保管。
leave something with the purser = 把某物交事务长保管

pursuant
/pəˈsjuːənt/
核心：（正式，法律）pursuant to：依照、按照（法律、规定、协议）。名词 pursuance：in pursuance of 是（正式）在执行……的过程中、为了履行……。并入本条。
① The inspection was carried out pursuant to section four of the act.
= 那次检查是依照该法第四条进行的。
pursuant to something = 依照某事
② The payment was made pursuant to an agreement signed in March.
= 那笔钱是按三月签的协议付的。
pursuant to an agreement = 按照协议
是法律文书用语。
③ The officer was injured in pursuance of his duties.
= 那名警官是在执行公务时受的伤。
in pursuance of something = 在执行某事的过程中

pursue
/pəˈsjuː/
核心：及物。一是（正式）追求、从事、致力于：pursue a goal、a career；二是继续探讨、追究、把（话题）谈下去；三是追赶、追捕、紧追不舍。
追赶的人叫 pursuer。并入本条。
① She left the bank to pursue a career as a painter.
= 她离开银行，去当画家了。
pursue a career = 从事一种职业
pursue a goal = 追求一个目标
② The government is pursuing a policy of cutting taxes for small firms.
= 政府在推行给小公司减税的政策。
pursue a policy = 推行一项政策
pursue an interest = 发展一种兴趣
③ I do not wish to pursue the matter any further this evening.
= 今晚这件事我不想再往下谈了。
pursue a matter = 追究一件事
pursue the question = 把问题谈下去
④ The police pursued the stolen car through the streets for twenty minutes.
= 警察在街上追了那辆被偷的车二十分钟。
pursue a car = 追一辆车
pursue somebody = 追赶某人
⑤ He was pursued by reporters wherever he went after the trial.
= 审判之后，他走到哪儿都有记者紧追不舍。
be pursued by somebody = 被某人紧追不舍
⑥ The fox ran into the wood and lost its pursuers among the trees.
= 狐狸跑进树林，在树丛里甩掉了追它的人。
a pursuer = 追赶的人
lose your pursuers = 甩掉追兵

pursuit
/pəˈsjuːt/
核心：三支。一是追求、寻求，不可数：the pursuit of happiness；二是追赶、追捕，不可数；三是（花时间做的）消遣、活动、事务，可数，多用复数。
习语：in pursuit of 是为了追求、追着；in hot pursuit 是紧追不舍。
① Many young people leave the village in pursuit of work in the city.
= 许多年轻人离开村子，到城里找活干。
in pursuit of = 为了追求、追着
in pursuit of something = 为了追求某物
② He gave up everything in the pursuit of fame and money.
= 为了追名逐利，他什么都舍了。
the pursuit of something = 对某物的追求
the pursuit of happiness = 追求幸福
③ The thief ran down the street with two policemen in hot pursuit.
= 小偷沿街跑去，两个警察紧追不舍。
in hot pursuit = 紧追不舍
in hot pursuit of somebody = 紧追着某人
④ The guards set off in pursuit as soon as the alarm was raised.
= 警报一响，卫兵立刻追了出去。
set off in pursuit = 追出去
a pursuit = 追赶
⑤ Walking and fishing are his favourite outdoor pursuits.
= 散步和钓鱼是他最喜欢的户外消遣。
outdoor pursuits = 户外消遣
leisure pursuits = 休闲活动

purulent
/ˈpjʊərələnt/
核心：（医学）化脓的、含脓的、流脓的。
① The wound had become red, swollen and purulent after three days.
= 三天之后，伤口红肿化脓了。
a purulent wound = 化脓的伤口
purulent = 化脓的
② The patient had a fever and a purulent discharge from the ear.
= 那个病人发烧，耳朵流脓。
a purulent discharge = 脓性分泌物
是医学用词。

purvey
/pəˈveɪ/
核心：（正式）供应、提供（食品、服务、消息）。及物。供应的人、商号叫 purveyor，多用复数。并入本条。
① The shop has purveyed tea and coffee to the palace for two hundred years.
= 那家店给王宫供应茶和咖啡已经两百年了。
purvey something to somebody = 向某人供应某物
purvey something = 供应某物
② The newspaper purveys gossip about the private lives of the famous.
= 那份报纸贩卖名人私生活的闲话。
purvey gossip = 贩卖闲话
这样用带贬义。
③ The firm describes itself as purveyors of fine cheeses since eighteen fifty.
= 那家公司自称从一八五〇年起就是上等奶酪的供应商。
purveyors of something = 某物的供应商
a purveyor = 供应商

purview
/ˈpɜːvjuː/
核心：（正式）（权限、职责、活动的）范围。不可数。常说 within、outside the purview of。
① Questions of pay fall outside the purview of this committee.
= 薪水的问题不在本委员会的职权范围之内。
outside the purview of something = 不在某机构的职权范围之内
② The schools come within the purview of the county council.
= 学校归郡议会管。
within the purview of something = 在某机构的职权范围之内
是正式用词。

pus
/pʌs/
核心：脓：伤口、疖子感染后流出来的黄白色稠液。不可数。
① The nurse cleaned the pus from the wound and put on a fresh dressing.
= 护士把伤口上的脓清干净，换了一块新敷料。
pus = 脓
clean the pus from a wound = 清掉伤口上的脓
② The boil on his neck was hot, red and full of pus.
= 他脖子上的疖子又烫又红，里面全是脓。
full of pus = 全是脓
形容词是 purulent。

push
/pʊʃ/
核心：动词：推、搡：朝离开自己的方向用力，反义 pull；挤、推开人往前走：push past、push through；按（钮、键）；
逼迫、催促、鞭策：push somebody to do、into doing，push yourself 是逼自己加把劲；力推、力争、推销（主张、产品）；把（价钱、数字）推高、压低；
（口语）贩卖（毒品）。
名词，可数：推、按；（军队的）大举推进；（为达到目的作的）大力一搏、攻势：a push for。
短语动词：push about、push around 是把（某人）呼来喝去、摆布；push ahead、push forward、push on 是（不顾困难）继续推进、继续往前走；
push aside 是把……推到一边、不去想；push back 是把……往后推，也指（美式）顶回去、反对；push for 是力争、一再要求；
push in 是（英式）插队；push off 是（英式口语）走开、滚开；push out 是把（某人）排挤出去；push over 是推倒；push through 是使（法案、计划）强行通过。
习语：push your luck 是得寸进尺、再冒一次险；push the boat out 是（英式口语）不惜花钱大办一场；when push comes to shove 是到了紧要关头、真到了那一步；
give somebody the push 是（英式口语）把某人解雇、把某人甩了；at a push 是（英式口语）非要不可的话、勉强；push the envelope 是突破常规、挑战极限；
push somebody's buttons 是故意惹某人、专戳某人的痛处；be pushing forty 是快四十岁了。
① We had to push the car to the side of the road when the engine died.
= 发动机熄了火，我们只好把车推到路边。
push the car = 推车
push something to the side = 把某物推到一边
② She pushed the door open with her foot, for her hands were full.
= 她两手都占着，就用脚把门推开了。
push something open = 把某物推开
push the door shut = 把门推上
③ He pushed his way through the crowd to the front of the hall.
= 他从人群里挤到了大厅的前面。
push your way through something = 从某处挤过去
push past somebody = 从某人身边挤过去
④ Push the green button to start the machine and the red one to stop it.
= 按绿钮开机，按红钮停机。
push a button = 按钮
push = 按
⑤ Her parents pushed her into studying law, though she wanted to paint.
= 她想画画，父母却逼她去学了法律。
push somebody into doing something = 逼某人做某事
push somebody to do something = 催某人做某事
⑥ You will never win a race unless you push yourself harder in training.
= 训练时不对自己再狠一点，你永远赢不了比赛。
push yourself = 逼自己加把劲
push somebody too hard = 把某人逼得太紧
⑦ The firm is pushing its new phone with advertisements on every bus.
= 那家公司在每辆公共汽车上打广告，力推它的新手机。
push a product = 力推一种产品
push an idea = 力推一种主张
⑧ The shortage of houses has pushed prices up by a fifth in two years.
= 房子短缺，两年里把价格推高了五分之一。
push prices up = 把价格推高
push prices down = 把价格压低
⑨ He was arrested for pushing drugs outside a school.
= 他因为在学校外面贩毒被捕。
push drugs = 贩毒
这一支是口语。
⑩ Give the door a good push, for it sticks in wet weather.
= 使劲推一下门，天潮的时候它发涩。
give something a push = 推某物一下
at the push of a button = 一按钮
⑪ The army began its final push towards the capital at dawn.
= 拂晓时那支军队开始向首都作最后的推进。
a final push = 最后的推进
a push = 大举推进
⑫ There has been a push for higher wages in all the big factories.
= 各家大工厂都在力争提高工资。
a push for something = 争取某事的一搏
make a push for something = 为某事大力一搏
⑬ I am tired of being pushed around by people half my age.
= 让岁数只有我一半的人呼来喝去，我受够了。
push somebody around = 把某人呼来喝去
push somebody about = 摆布某人
⑭ The council is pushing ahead with its plans in spite of the protests.
= 镇议会不顾抗议，继续推进它的计划。
push ahead = 继续推进
push ahead with something = 继续推进某事
⑮ She pushed her doubts aside and signed the contract.
= 她把疑虑撇到一边，签了合同。
push aside = 把……推到一边、不去想
push something aside = 把某事撇开
⑯ The meeting has been pushed back from Tuesday to Friday.
= 会议从星期二推到了星期五。
push back = 把……往后推
push something back = 把某事推后
⑰ The unions are pushing for a shorter working week.
= 工会在力争缩短每周的工时。
push for something = 力争某事
push for = 力争、一再要求
⑱ We pushed forward through the snow and reached the hut before dark.
= 我们冒雪继续往前走，天黑前到了小屋。
push forward = 继续往前走
push on = 继续赶路
⑲ A man in a grey suit pushed in at the front of the queue.
= 一个穿灰西装的男人在队伍最前面插了进来。
push in = 插队
这是英式说法；美式说 cut in line。
⑳ I told the boys to push off and leave the cat alone.
= 我叫那几个男孩走开，别惹那只猫。
push off = 走开、滚开
这是英式口语，不客气。
㉑ He felt that he was being pushed out of the firm he had founded.
= 他觉得自己正被人从自己创办的公司里排挤出去。
push somebody out = 把某人排挤出去
push out = 排挤
㉒ The wind pushed over the fence at the bottom of the garden.
= 风把园子尽头的篱笆刮倒了。
push over = 推倒
push something over = 把某物推倒
㉓ The government pushed the bill through in a single week.
= 政府一个星期就让那项法案强行通过了。
push through = 使强行通过
push a bill through = 使法案强行通过
㉔ You have borrowed the car twice this week, so do not push your luck.
= 这个星期你已经借了两回车了，别得寸进尺。
push your luck = 得寸进尺
push it = 得寸进尺
㉕ They pushed the boat out for their daughter's wedding and hired a castle.
= 女儿结婚，他们不惜工本，租了一座城堡。
push the boat out = 不惜花钱大办一场
这是英式口语。
㉖ When push comes to shove, he will always side with his brother.
= 真到了紧要关头，他总会站在哥哥一边。
when push comes to shove = 到了紧要关头
㉗ The firm gave him the push after he lost its biggest customer.
= 他弄丢了公司最大的主顾，公司就把他辞了。
give somebody the push = 把某人解雇、把某人甩了
get the push = 被解雇、被甩
㉘ The hall holds two hundred people, or two hundred and twenty at a push.
= 那个大厅坐两百人，硬挤的话能坐两百二十。
at a push = 非要不可的话、勉强
这是英式口语。
㉙ The young architect likes to push the envelope with every building she designs.
= 那位年轻建筑师每设计一栋楼都爱突破常规。
push the envelope = 突破常规、挑战极限
㉚ My brother knows exactly how to push my buttons at the dinner table.
= 我弟弟清楚得很，在饭桌上怎么说能把我惹毛。
push somebody's buttons = 故意惹某人、专戳某人的痛处
㉛ He must be pushing sixty, but he still runs every morning.
= 他该快六十了，可还是天天早上跑步。
be pushing sixty = 快六十岁了
be pushing forty = 快四十岁了
㉜ The party was accused of push polling in the week before the election.
= 那个党被指在选举前一周搞诱导式民调。
push polling = 诱导式民调
㉝ The battery was flat, so we had to give the car a push-start.
= 电瓶没电了，我们只好推车发动。
a push-start = 推车发动
也作动词：push-start a car。
㉞ He does fifty push-ups every morning before breakfast.
= 他每天早饭前做五十个俯卧撑。
a push-up = （美式）俯卧撑
英式多说 press-up。

pushback
/ˈpʊʃbæk/
核心：（尤其美式）抵制、反对、反弹：对一项计划、主张的抵抗。不可数，也用单数。
① The plan to close the library met with strong pushback from local people.
= 关闭图书馆的计划遭到了当地人的强烈抵制。
strong pushback = 强烈的抵制
pushback from somebody = 某人的抵制
② There has been some pushback against the new rules on working from home.
= 在家办公的新规定引起了一些反弹。
pushback against something = 对某事的反弹
是较新的说法。

pushbike
/ˈpʊʃbaɪk/
核心：（英式旧式口语）自行车、脚踏车：跟摩托车相对而言。可数。
① My grandfather rode to work on a pushbike for forty years.
= 我爷爷骑了四十年自行车上班。
a pushbike = 自行车
ride a pushbike = 骑自行车
② He sold his motorbike and went back to an old pushbike.
= 他卖了摩托车，又骑回了一辆旧自行车。
an old pushbike = 旧自行车
如今显得老派，一般说 bike。

pushcart
/ˈpʊʃkɑːt/
核心：（街头小贩用的）手推车。可数。
① An old man sold hot chestnuts from a pushcart outside the station.
= 一个老人在车站外面推着手推车卖热栗子。
a pushcart = 手推车
sell something from a pushcart = 推着手推车卖某物
② The streets of the market were crowded with pushcarts piled high with fruit.
= 集市的街上挤满了堆得高高的水果手推车。
a pushcart piled with fruit = 堆满水果的手推车

pushchair
/ˈpʊʃtʃeə(r)/
核心：（英式）（折叠式的）婴儿推车：让会坐的小孩坐着推的那种。可数。美式叫 stroller。
① She folded the pushchair and carried it up the steps of the bus.
= 她把婴儿推车折起来，拎上了公共汽车的台阶。
a pushchair = 婴儿推车
fold a pushchair = 折起婴儿推车
② The little boy fell asleep in his pushchair on the way home from the park.
= 从公园回家的路上，小男孩在推车里睡着了。
in a pushchair = 在婴儿推车里
躺着的小婴儿用的叫 pram。

pushed
/pʊʃt/
核心：（口语）不放在名词前。一是 be pushed to do：很难做到；二是 be pushed for：缺（时间、钱）；三是忙得不可开交。
① You would be hard pushed to find a better teacher in the whole county.
= 全郡你也难找出一位更好的老师来。
be hard pushed to do something = 很难做到某事
be pushed to do something = 做某事很吃力
② I am a bit pushed for time this morning, so can we talk later?
= 我今天上午时间有点紧，回头再谈行吗？
be pushed for time = 时间紧
be pushed for money = 手头紧
③ We are rather pushed at the moment, with three people away ill.
= 眼下三个人请了病假，我们忙得很。
be pushed = 忙得不可开交
这是英式口语。

pusher
/ˈpʊʃə(r)/
核心：（口语）毒贩：卖毒品的人。可数。也说 drug pusher。
① The police arrested two pushers outside a nightclub in the centre of the city.
= 警察在市中心一家夜总会外面抓了两个毒贩。
a pusher = 毒贩
a drug pusher = 毒贩
② The street was known as a place where pushers waited for schoolchildren.
= 那条街是出了名的毒贩等学生的地方。
pushers on the street = 街头的毒贩
是口语；正式说 drug dealer。

pushover
/ˈpʊʃəʊvə(r)/
核心：（口语）两支，可数，多用单数。一是轻而易举的事、容易打败的对手；二是耳根子软的人、好说话的人。
① The first round was a pushover, but the second will be much harder.
= 头一轮轻轻松松，第二轮可要难得多。
a pushover = 轻而易举的事
be a pushover = 很容易对付
② The children know that their grandfather is a pushover and always ask him first.
= 孩子们知道爷爷耳根子软，有事总先找他。
be a pushover for somebody = 对某人有求必应
这一支说人好说话。

pushpin
/ˈpʊʃpɪn/
核心：（美式）图钉：带一个塑料或金属小帽的那种，按进软木板里钉住纸。可数。
① She fixed the map to the board with a pushpin at each corner.
= 她在四个角各按一颗图钉，把地图钉在板上。
a pushpin = 图钉
fix something with a pushpin = 用图钉钉住某物
② The wall above his desk was covered with notes held up by coloured pushpins.
= 他书桌上方的墙上满是用彩色图钉钉着的便条。
coloured pushpins = 彩色图钉
英式多说 drawing pin。

pushy
/ˈpʊʃi/
核心：（口语，含贬义）爱出风头的、咄咄逼人的、硬要别人照自己意思办的。比较级 pushier。名词 pushiness，不可数。并入本条。
① The salesman was so pushy that we walked out of the shop.
= 那个推销员太咄咄逼人，我们转身出了店。
be pushy = 咄咄逼人
a pushy salesman = 咄咄逼人的推销员
② She did not want to be one of those pushy parents who shout at the referee.
= 她不想当那种冲裁判嚷嚷的强势家长。
pushy parents = 强势的家长
带贬义。
③ His pushiness got him the job, but it did not make him any friends.
= 他靠一股硬往前拱的劲儿得到了那份工作，却没交到一个朋友。
pushiness = 咄咄逼人、爱出风头

pusillanimous
/ˌpjuːsɪˈlænɪməs/
核心：（正式）胆小怕事的、懦弱的、不敢担一点风险的。
① The newspaper attacked the government's pusillanimous response to the crisis.
= 那份报纸抨击政府对危机的应对畏首畏尾。
a pusillanimous response = 畏首畏尾的应对
pusillanimous = 胆小怕事的
② He was too pusillanimous to tell the director that the plan would fail.
= 他太胆小，不敢告诉主任那个计划会失败。
be too pusillanimous to do something = 太胆小不敢做某事
日常说 cowardly、timid。

puss
/pʊs/
核心：（尤其英式口语）猫咪：叫猫、说猫时的亲昵称呼。可数，多用单数。
① "Come here, puss," she called, rattling the box of biscuits.
= 「过来，猫咪。」她摇着饼干盒子喊道。
puss = 猫咪
Come here, puss = 过来，猫咪
② The old puss slept all day on the warm bonnet of the car.
= 那只老猫整天睡在暖和的汽车引擎盖上。
an old puss = 老猫
多用来唤猫。

pussy
/ˈpʊsi/
核心：（儿语）猫咪。可数，复数 pussies。也说 pussy cat。这个词另有很粗俗的意思，指女性的阴部，是禁忌语，要回避；所以日常说猫多用 cat、kitty。
① The little girl wanted to stroke the pussy sitting on the garden wall.
= 小女孩想摸摸蹲在院墙上的那只猫咪。
a pussy = 猫咪
stroke the pussy = 摸猫咪
② "Nice pussy," said the child, holding out a piece of fish.
= 「乖猫咪。」孩子说着，递出一块鱼。
Nice pussy = 乖猫咪
是小孩子的说法。
③ She cut a few branches of pussy willow for a vase on the table.
= 她剪了几枝猫柳，插在桌上的花瓶里。
pussy willow = 猫柳
开毛茸茸银灰色花序的一种柳树。

pussycat
/ˈpʊsikæt/
核心：两支，可数。一是（儿语）猫咪；二是（口语）温和的人、其实很好说话的人：看着凶，实际一点不厉害。
① The baby pointed at the picture and said "pussycat" for the first time.
= 婴儿指着那张图，头一回说出了「猫咪」。
a pussycat = 猫咪
② The sergeant shouts a lot, but he is a pussycat when you get to know him.
= 那位中士嗓门大，可是处熟了才知道他其实很好说话。
be a pussycat = 其实很温和

pussyfoot
/ˈpʊsifʊt/
核心：（口语，含贬义）畏首畏尾、躲躲闪闪、不敢明说明做：pussyfoot about、around。不及物。
① Stop pussyfooting around and tell me what you really think of the plan.
= 别躲躲闪闪了，告诉我你对那个计划到底怎么看。
pussyfoot around = 躲躲闪闪
pussyfoot about = 畏首畏尾
② The council has pussyfooted for years over the question of the new road.
= 在新公路的问题上，镇议会畏首畏尾了好多年。
pussyfoot over something = 在某事上畏首畏尾
带贬义：怕得罪人，不敢拿主意。

pustule
/ˈpʌstjuːl/
核心：（正式，医学）脓疱：皮肤上含脓的小疙瘩。可数。
① The rash developed into small pustules on the child's chest and back.
= 那片疹子在孩子的前胸后背上长成了一个个小脓疱。
a pustule = 脓疱
small pustules = 小脓疱
② The doctor told him not to squeeze the pustules on his face.
= 医生叫他别挤脸上的脓疱。
squeeze a pustule = 挤脓疱
是医学用词；日常说 spot、pimple。

put
/pʊt/
核心：动词，过去式、过去分词都是 put，现在分词 putting。最基本的意思是放、摆、搁：把东西移到某个位置：put something in、on、under；
由此引出：使处于（某种状态、处境）：put somebody at risk、put something right；写、标上：put your name on；说、表达：put it、put something into words；
提出（问题、建议、方案）：put a question to somebody；估计、估价：put the cost at；把（钱、精力、时间）投到：put money into、put effort into；
（体育）推（铅球）。
短语动词（只列最常用的）：put about 是传播（谣言），也指（船）掉头；put across、put over 是把（想法）讲清楚、让人明白；put aside 是搁置、存下（钱）；
put away 是收起来、存（钱）；put back 是放回原处，也指推迟；put before 是把……提交给（某人考虑），也指把……看得比……重；put behind you 是把（不快的事）抛到脑后；
put by 是存下（钱）；put down 是放下，也指镇压、（给生病的动物）实施安乐死、记下、当众贬低；put down to 是把……归因于；put down as 是把……看作；
put down for 是给……报名；put forth 是（正式）提出、（植物）长出；put forward 是提出（建议），也指推荐（某人）、把（钟）拨快；
put in 是安装、投入（时间、精力）、提出（申请）；put in for 是正式申请；put into 是把（时间、钱、精力）投进；put off 是推迟，也指让人反感、扫兴、打扰；
put on 是穿上、戴上，也指打开（灯、电器）、长（体重）、上演、装出（某种样子）；put out 是熄灭（火、灯），也指出版、发布、给人添麻烦；
put yourself out 是特地费事帮人；put through 是给……接通电话，也指让（某人）经受（磨难）；put to 是向……提出；put together 是组装、拼凑、编成；
put towards 是把（钱）用来支付一部分；put up 是举起、搭起、建造、张贴、提高（价格），也指留（人）住宿；put up for 是把……拿出来（出售）；
put up to 是唆使；put up with 是忍受、容忍；put upon 是占……的便宜，put-upon 是被人欺负的、被占便宜的。
习语：put it 是这么说吧、换个说法：to put it another way；not put it past somebody 是认为某人做得出来；put a stop to 是制止；
put your foot down 是坚决反对、说一不二，也指踩油门加速；put paid to 是（英式）断送、使落空；put somebody to the test 是考验某人；
put somebody in their place 是让某人知道自己的分量；put two and two together 是根据所见推断出来；put your feet up 是歇一歇；put down roots 是扎根、定居。
① She put the keys on the table and took off her coat.
= 她把钥匙放在桌上，脱下了外套。
put something on something = 把某物放在某物上
put = 放
② He put his hand in his pocket and found that his wallet had gone.
= 他把手伸进口袋，发现钱包不见了。
put your hand in something = 把手伸进某处
put something in something = 把某物放进某处
③ Where have you put the scissors? They are not in the drawer.
= 剪刀你放哪儿了？抽屉里没有。
put something somewhere = 把某物放在某处
后面一定要跟表示地点的词。
④ The new rules put many small shops at risk of closing.
= 新规定让许多小店面临关门的危险。
put somebody at risk = 使某人面临危险
put somebody in danger = 使某人陷入危险
⑤ It took the engineer two days to put the machine right.
= 工程师花了两天才把那台机器修好。
put something right = 把某事纠正、修好
put somebody at ease = 让某人放松
⑥ Put your name and address at the top of the form.
= 在表格顶上写上姓名和地址。
put your name on something = 在某物上写名字
put a tick = 打勾
⑦ I do not quite know how to put this, but your dog has eaten my lunch.
= 我不太知道怎么说才好，不过你的狗把我的午饭吃了。
put it = 这么说吧
how to put this = 怎么说才好
⑧ She found it hard to put her feelings into words.
= 她觉得很难把自己的感受说出来。
put something into words = 把某事用话说出来
⑨ The reporter put the same question to all three candidates.
= 那个记者向三位候选人提了同一个问题。
put a question to somebody = 向某人提问题
put an idea to somebody = 向某人提出一个想法
⑩ The insurers put the cost of the damage at about a million pounds.
= 保险公司把损失估计在一百万英镑左右。
put the cost at something = 把费用估计为某数
put a value on something = 给某物估价
⑪ Her parents put all their savings into the business.
= 她父母把全部积蓄都投进了那个生意。
put money into something = 把钱投进某事
put effort into something = 在某事上下功夫
⑫ The rumour was put about that the factory was going to close.
= 有人放风说那家工厂要关门。
put about = 传播（谣言）
put it about that… = 放风说……
⑬ He is a clever man, but he cannot put his ideas across to a class.
= 他人很聪明，可是没法把自己的想法给一班学生讲明白。
put across = 把（想法）讲清楚
put something across = 把某事讲明白
put over = 把（想法）讲清楚
⑭ They put aside a little money every month for their holiday.
= 他们每个月存一点钱，留着度假。
put aside = 搁置、存下
put something aside = 把某物存起来
⑮ Put your toys away before your grandmother arrives.
= 奶奶来之前把你的玩具收起来。
put away = 收起来
put something away = 把某物收好
⑯ Please put the books back on the shelf when you have finished with them.
= 书看完了请放回书架上。
put back = 放回原处
put something back = 把某物放回去
⑰ The meeting has been put back to next Thursday.
= 会议推到了下星期四。
put something back = 把某事推后
这一支是「推迟」。
⑱ The plan will be put before the committee at its next meeting.
= 那个计划将在下次会议上提交给委员会。
put before = 提交给……考虑
put something before somebody = 把某事提交给某人
⑲ She always puts her children before her career.
= 她总是把孩子看得比事业重。
put A before B = 把甲看得比乙重
⑳ It was a terrible year, but we have put it behind us now.
= 那是糟糕的一年，不过我们已经把它抛到脑后了。
put something behind you = 把某事抛到脑后
put behind you = 抛到脑后
㉑ He had put by enough money to buy a small boat.
= 他攒下了够买一条小船的钱。
put by = 存下（钱）
put money by = 存钱
㉒ She put down her bag and sat on the edge of the bed.
= 她放下包，坐在床沿上。
put down = 放下
put something down = 把某物放下
㉓ The army was sent in to put down the rising in the north.
= 军队被派去镇压北方的叛乱。
put down a rebellion = 镇压叛乱
put something down = 镇压某事
㉔ The old dog was in great pain, so the vet put him down.
= 那条老狗疼得厉害，兽医给它实施了安乐死。
put an animal down = 给动物实施安乐死
这是委婉的说法。
㉕ He is always putting his wife down in front of her friends.
= 他老当着他妻子朋友的面贬低她。
put somebody down = 当众贬低某人
名词 a put-down 是贬低人的话。
㉖ The doctors put his tiredness down to stress at work.
= 医生们把他的疲劳归因于工作压力。
put something down to something = 把某事归因于某事
put down to = 归因于
㉗ I had put him down as a shy man, but he spoke for an hour.
= 我原以为他是个腼腆的人，他却讲了一个小时。
put somebody down as something = 把某人看作某种人
put down as = 看作
㉘ They put their son down for the school as soon as he was born.
= 儿子一出生，他们就给他在那所学校报了名。
put somebody down for something = 给某人报名某事
put down for = 报名
㉙ The young scientist put forth a bold new theory about the origin of the moon.
= 那位年轻科学家提出了关于月球起源的一个大胆的新理论。
put forth = 提出
put forth leaves = 长出叶子
㉚ The union put forward a plan to share the work among all the staff.
= 工会提出了一个让全体员工分摊工作的方案。
put forward = 提出（建议）
put forward a plan = 提出方案
㉛ Remember to put the clocks forward an hour on Saturday night.
= 记得星期六夜里把钟拨快一个小时。
put the clocks forward = 把钟拨快
put somebody forward = 推荐某人
㉜ We are having a new kitchen put in next month.
= 下个月我们要请人装新厨房。
put in = 安装
put in a kitchen = 装厨房
㉝ She has put in a lot of hours on the project this week.
= 这个星期她在那个项目上花了很多时间。
put in hours = 投入时间
put in a claim = 提出索赔
㉞ Three of the nurses have put in for a transfer to the new hospital.
= 有三名护士申请调到新医院去。
put in for something = 正式申请某事
put in for = 申请
㉟ They have put a lot of work into the garden since they moved.
= 搬来以后，他们在园子上下了很大功夫。
put work into something = 在某事上下功夫
put into = 投进
㊱ We shall have to put off the party until Mary is better.
= 只好等玛丽好了再开派对了。
put off = 推迟
put off doing something = 推迟做某事
㊲ The smell of the kitchen put me off my dinner.
= 厨房的味道让我没了吃饭的胃口。
put somebody off something = 让某人对某物没了兴趣
put somebody off = 让人反感、扫兴
㊳ Do not talk to her while she is counting, or you will put her off.
= 她在数数的时候别跟她说话，会打扰她的。
put somebody off = 打扰某人、使某人分心
这一支是「打扰」。
㊴ He put on his coat and hat and went out into the rain.
= 他穿上大衣、戴上帽子，出门走进了雨里。
put on = 穿上（衣服、鞋、帽）
put on a coat = 穿上大衣
㊵ Put on the light, for it is too dark to read.
= 开灯吧，太暗了没法看书。
put on the light = 开灯
put the kettle on = 烧上水
㊶ She has put on weight since she gave up smoking.
= 戒烟以后她长胖了。
put on weight = 长胖
反过来是 lose weight。
㊷ The school puts on a play every Christmas.
= 那所学校每年圣诞节演一出戏。
put on a play = 上演一出戏
put on a show = 办一场演出
㊸ He put on a brave face, but we could see that he was upset.
= 他装出一副勇敢的样子，可我们看得出他很难过。
put on a brave face = 装出勇敢的样子
put on an accent = 装腔
㊹ It took the firemen three hours to put out the fire.
= 消防员花了三个小时才把火扑灭。
put out = 熄灭
put out a fire = 灭火
㊺ The police have put out a description of the man they are looking for.
= 警方发布了他们在找的那个男人的相貌特征。
put out a statement = 发布声明
put out = 发布、出版
㊻ I hope we are not putting you out by staying another night.
= 希望我们再住一晚不会给你添麻烦。
put somebody out = 给某人添麻烦
be put out = 不高兴
㊼ She put herself out to make us feel welcome.
= 她特地费了不少心让我们觉得宾至如归。
put yourself out = 特地费事帮人
㊽ Hold the line, please, and I shall put you through to the manager.
= 请别挂电话，我给您转接经理。
put somebody through to somebody = 给某人接通某人
put through = 接通电话
㊾ His parents were put through hell by the long trial.
= 漫长的审判让他父母受尽了折磨。
put somebody through something = 让某人经受某事
put somebody through hell = 让某人受尽折磨
㊿ I put it to you that you were never at home that night.
= 我认为你那天夜里根本不在家。
put it to somebody that… = 向某人提出……
这是法庭上律师质问的说法。
❶ The children put together a model plane from a kit.
= 孩子们用一套散件拼了一架飞机模型。
put together = 组装、编成
put something together = 把某物组装起来
❷ She put the twenty pounds from her aunt towards a new bicycle.
= 她把姑妈给的二十英镑用来凑买新自行车的钱。
put money towards something = 把钱用来支付某物的一部分
put towards = 用来支付一部分
❸ The tourists put up their tents in a field by the river.
= 游客们在河边的一块地里搭起了帐篷。
put up = 竖起、搭建
put up a tent = 搭帐篷
❹ Put up your hand if you know the answer, and do not shout it out.
= 知道答案的举手，别喊出来。
put up your hand = 举手
put up a notice = 贴出通知
❺ The landlord has put up the rent by ten per cent.
= 房东把房租提高了百分之十。
put up the rent = 提高房租
put up prices = 提价
❻ Can you put me up for a night when I come to London?
= 我去伦敦的时候能在你那儿住一晚吗？
put somebody up = 留某人住宿
❼ They have put the house up for sale and are moving to Spain.
= 他们把房子挂出去卖了，要搬到西班牙去。
put something up for sale = 把某物拿出来卖
put up for = 拿出来（出售）
❽ Who put you up to this? You would never have thought of it yourself.
= 是谁唆使你这么干的？你自己绝想不出来。
put somebody up to something = 唆使某人做某事
put up to = 唆使
❾ I do not know how you put up with the noise from the flat upstairs.
= 我不知道你怎么受得了楼上那套房里的吵闹。
put up with = 忍受、容忍
put up with something = 忍受某事
❿ His put-upon wife did all the work while he played golf.
= 他打高尔夫，他那被欺负的妻子把活全干了。
put-upon = 被人欺负的、被占便宜的
⓫ I would not put it past him to sell the car without telling us.
= 他背着我们把车卖了，这种事我看他做得出来。
not put it past somebody = 认为某人做得出来
⓬ The new head teacher soon put a stop to the fighting in the playground.
= 新校长很快就制止了操场上的打架。
put a stop to = 制止
put a stop to something = 制止某事
⓭ When the children asked for a dog, their father put his foot down.
= 孩子们要养狗，他们父亲坚决不答应。
put your foot down = 坚决反对、说一不二
也指踩油门加速。
⓮ The injury put paid to her hopes of running in the Olympics.
= 那次受伤断送了她参加奥运会赛跑的希望。
put paid to = 断送、使落空
put paid to something = 断送某事
⓯ The long climb put our fitness to the test.
= 那段长长的爬坡考验了我们的体力。
put somebody to the test = 考验某人
put something to the test = 检验某事
⓰ He was rude to the old waiter until she put him in his place.
= 他对那个老侍者无礼，直到她让他明白了自己的分量。
put somebody in their place = 让某人知道自己的分量
⓱ When I saw the ring, I put two and two together.
= 看见那枚戒指，我就猜到是怎么回事了。
put two and two together = 根据所见推断出来
⓲ You have worked hard all week, so put your feet up this evening.
= 你辛苦了一个星期，今晚歇一歇吧。
put your feet up = 歇一歇
⓳ After years of moving round the world, they put down roots in a small Welsh town.
= 在世界各地辗转多年之后，他们在威尔士的一个小镇扎下了根。
put down roots = 扎根、定居
⓴ She came fourth in the shot put at the school sports.
= 她在学校运动会的铅球比赛里得了第四。
the shot put = 铅球
put the shot = 推铅球
⓵ The children put the pictures onto the wall with sticky tape.
= 孩子们用胶带把画贴到墙上。
put something onto something = 把某物放到某物上
put onto = 放到……上
⓶ She has decided to put herself up for election to the council.
= 她决定参选镇议员。
put yourself up for something = 自荐参选某职
⓷ She has always put her duty to her patients above her own comfort.
= 她一向把对病人的责任看得比自己的安逸重。
put above = 把……看得比……重
put A above B = 把甲看得比乙重

putative
/ˈpjuːtətɪv/
核心：（正式）公认的、推定的、被认为是的：大家这么认为，但未必确证。只放在名词前。
① The putative father of the child refused to take a blood test.
= 那个孩子的推定父亲拒绝验血。
the putative father = 推定的父亲
putative = 推定的、被认为是的
② The scientists are studying a putative link between the virus and the disease.
= 科学家们在研究那种病毒和那种病之间据认为存在的联系。
a putative link = 据认为存在的联系
是正式、学术用词。

putonghua
/ˌpuːtɒŋˈhwɑː/
核心：普通话：现代汉语的标准语。不可数。也写作 Putonghua。
① She learned Putonghua at school and Cantonese at home in Hong Kong.
= 她在香港，在学校学普通话，在家说粤语。
learn Putonghua = 学普通话
Putonghua = 普通话
② The news on the national channel is read in standard putonghua.
= 全国频道的新闻用标准普通话播报。
standard putonghua = 标准普通话
英文里也常说 Mandarin。

putrefy
/ˈpjuːtrɪfaɪ/
核心：（正式）（尸体、植物）腐烂、腐败、发臭。不及物。过去式 putrefied。名词 putrefaction，不可数。并入本条。
① The fish had been left in the sun and had begun to putrefy.
= 那些鱼被扔在太阳底下，已经开始腐烂。
begin to putrefy = 开始腐烂
putrefy = 腐烂
② The smell of putrefying meat drifted from the abandoned butcher's shop.
= 废弃的肉铺里飘出腐肉的臭味。
putrefying meat = 腐烂的肉
③ The cold of the cave had slowed the putrefaction of the body.
= 洞里的寒气减缓了尸体的腐败。
putrefaction = 腐烂、腐败
the putrefaction of something = 某物的腐败

putrid
/ˈpjuːtrɪd/
核心：两支。一是腐烂的、发臭的；二是（口语）极差的、糟透的、令人作呕的。
① A putrid smell came from the drain behind the restaurant.
= 餐馆后面的下水道里冒出一股腐臭味。
a putrid smell = 腐臭味
putrid meat = 腐肉
② The water in the old tank had turned green and putrid.
= 旧水箱里的水变绿发臭了。
turn putrid = 变臭
③ The walls of the hotel room were painted a putrid shade of yellow.
= 那间旅馆客房的墙刷成了一种难看透顶的黄色。
a putrid shade of yellow = 难看透顶的黄色
这一支是口语。

putsch
/pʊtʃ/
核心：政变、暴动：一小伙人想用武力突然夺权的行动，多指失败了的。可数。是德语借词。
① The army officers who led the putsch were arrested within a day.
= 领头搞政变的那些军官一天之内就被抓了。
a putsch = 政变
lead a putsch = 领头搞政变
② The putsch failed because the soldiers in the capital refused to join it.
= 首都的士兵不肯参加，那场政变就失败了。
a failed putsch = 失败的政变
跟 coup 意思相近。

putt
/pʌt/
核心：（高尔夫）动词：推杆、轻击（球）入洞：把球轻轻打过果岭滚进洞，及物也可不及物。名词，可数：推杆、轻击。
① She putted the ball straight into the hole from ten metres.
= 她从十米外一杆把球推进了洞。
putt the ball = 推球
putt = 推杆
② He needed to sink a short putt on the last hole to win the championship.
= 他得在最后一洞推进一记短推，才能赢得冠军。
a short putt = 短推
sink a putt = 推球入洞
③ He missed a putt of less than a metre and lost the match.
= 他一记不到一米的推杆没进，输了比赛。
miss a putt = 推杆没进

putter
/ˈpʌtə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：（高尔夫的）推杆：轻击球入洞用的球杆。动词：（美式）putter about、around 是悠闲地做些零碎事、磨蹭，英式说 potter。
① He bought a new putter, but his putting did not get any better.
= 他买了一根新推杆，推杆的水平却一点没长进。
a putter = 推杆
② She spent the morning puttering around in the garden.
= 她一上午都在园子里东摸西弄。
putter around = 悠闲地做些零碎事
putter about = 磨蹭
这一支是美式说法。

putty
/ˈpʌti/
核心：油灰、腻子：一种软软的糊状物，用来把玻璃嵌进窗框、填墙上的缝，干了就变硬。不可数。
习语 be putty in somebody's hands 是对某人言听计从、任人摆布。
① He fixed the new pane of glass into the frame with putty.
= 他用油灰把新玻璃嵌进了窗框。
fix something with putty = 用油灰把某物嵌住
putty = 油灰、腻子
② Fill the holes in the wood with putty before you paint the door.
= 刷门之前，先用腻子把木头上的洞填平。
fill something with putty = 用腻子填平某处
③ He was putty in her hands and would do anything she asked.
= 他对她言听计从，她要什么他都照办。
be putty in somebody's hands = 对某人言听计从

putz
/pʌts/
核心：（美式俚语，冒犯）蠢货、窝囊废。可数。动词 putz around 是（美式口语）瞎忙、闲荡。
① Some putz in a big car took my parking space.
= 某个开大车的蠢货占了我的停车位。
a putz = 蠢货
② He spent the whole weekend putzing around in the garage.
= 他整个周末都在车库里瞎忙。
putz around = 瞎忙、闲荡
是意第绪语来的俚语，说人时很不客气。

puzzle
/ˈpʌzl/
核心：名词，可数：智力游戏、谜、拼图：a crossword puzzle、a jigsaw puzzle；谜一般的事、费解的人，多用单数：be a puzzle to somebody。
动词：使困惑、使摸不着头脑；puzzle over、about 是苦苦思索；puzzle out 是琢磨出、想明白。
习语 a piece of the puzzle 是（弄清全局要的）一条线索、其中的一块。
派生：puzzled 是困惑的、摸不着头脑的；puzzling 是令人费解的；puzzlement 是困惑，不可数；puzzler 是（口语）难题、让人费解的事。并入本条。
① My grandfather does the crossword puzzle in the paper every morning.
= 我爷爷每天早上做报上的纵横字谜。
a crossword puzzle = 纵横字谜
do a puzzle = 做智力游戏
② The children spent the afternoon on a jigsaw puzzle of a castle.
= 孩子们花了一下午拼一幅城堡的拼图。
a jigsaw puzzle = 拼图
③ Why she left so suddenly is still a puzzle to all of us.
= 她为什么走得那么突然，我们大家至今都想不通。
be a puzzle to somebody = 让某人想不通
a puzzle = 谜一般的事
④ What puzzles me is how the thief got into the house.
= 让我纳闷的是，那个贼是怎么进屋的。
puzzle somebody = 使某人困惑
what puzzles me is… = 让我纳闷的是……
⑤ The scientists have puzzled over the strange signal for years.
= 科学家们为那个奇怪的信号苦苦琢磨了好些年。
puzzle over something = 苦苦思索某事
puzzle about something = 琢磨某事
⑥ It took me an hour to puzzle out the instructions for the new oven.
= 我花了一个小时才弄懂新烤箱的说明书。
puzzle out = 琢磨出、想明白
puzzle something out = 把某事琢磨明白
⑦ The letter found in the attic is another piece of the puzzle.
= 阁楼里找到的那封信，又是一条线索。
a piece of the puzzle = 一条线索
the missing piece of the puzzle = 缺的那块拼图
⑧ She looked puzzled when I asked her about the money.
= 我问到那笔钱时，她一脸困惑。
look puzzled = 一脸困惑
be puzzled by something = 被某事弄糊涂
⑨ It is puzzling that nobody heard the shot.
= 竟然没人听见枪声，真令人费解。
puzzling = 令人费解的
a puzzling question = 费解的问题
⑩ He stared at the strange machine in puzzlement.
= 他困惑地盯着那台怪机器。
in puzzlement = 困惑地
puzzlement = 困惑
⑪ The disappearance of the ship remains a real puzzler.
= 那艘船的失踪至今仍是个大谜团。
a puzzler = 难题、让人费解的事

pygmy
/ˈpɪɡmi/
核心：名词，可数，复数 pygmies：Pygmy 是俾格米人：中非等地一些身材特别矮小的民族的人，这个叫法有人觉得冒犯；（含贬义）无足轻重的人、侏儒：a political pygmy。
形容词，只放在名词前：（动植物）矮小品种的：a pygmy hippo。也拼作 pigmy。
① The Pygmy peoples of the rainforest live by hunting and gathering.
= 雨林里的俾格米人靠打猎和采集为生。
the Pygmy peoples = 俾格米人
如今多用各族自己的名称，这个总称有人觉得冒犯。
② Compared with his father, he was a political pygmy.
= 跟他父亲比起来，他在政治上只是个小角色。
a political pygmy = 政治上的小角色
a pygmy = 无足轻重的人
③ The zoo has a pair of pygmy hippos that were born in the forests of Liberia.
= 那家动物园有一对生在利比里亚森林里的倭河马。
a pygmy hippo = 倭河马
a pygmy goat = 侏儒山羊
④ The explorer's old diary describes a pigmy chief who met him deep in the forest.
= 那位探险家的旧日记写到在密林深处迎接他的一位矮人首领。
pigmy = 旧拼法，同 pygmy

pyjamas
/pəˈdʒɑːməz/
核心：睡衣裤：一套宽松的上衣和裤子，睡觉时穿。复数名词，一套说 a pair of pyjamas。作定语用单数 pyjama：pyjama bottoms。美式拼作 pajamas。
① The children were already in their pyjamas when the visitors arrived.
= 客人到的时候，孩子们已经穿上睡衣了。
in your pyjamas = 穿着睡衣
a pair of pyjamas = 一套睡衣
② He came down to breakfast in his pyjama bottoms and an old jumper.
= 他穿着睡裤、套着一件旧毛衣下楼吃早饭。
pyjama bottoms = 睡裤
a pyjama top = 睡衣上衣
③ She bought her son a pair of pajamas with rockets on them.
= 她给儿子买了一套印着火箭的睡衣。
pajamas = （美式拼法）睡衣裤

pylon
/ˈpaɪlən/
核心：（高压输电线的）电缆塔、高压线铁塔：很高的金属架子。可数。也指（标示航线的）塔标。
① A line of pylons carries the cables across the valley.
= 一排高压线塔把电缆架过了山谷。
a pylon = 电缆塔
a line of pylons = 一排电缆塔
② Local people protested against plans to put up pylons near the school.
= 当地人抗议在学校附近竖高压线塔的计划。
put up pylons = 竖电缆塔
an electricity pylon = 高压线铁塔

pyorrhoea
/ˌpaɪəˈrɪə/
核心：（医学，旧式）牙周病、齿槽脓溢：牙龈发炎化脓、牙齿松动的病。不可数。美式拼作 pyorrhea。
① He lost several teeth to pyorrhoea in his forties.
= 他四十来岁时因为牙周病掉了好几颗牙。
pyorrhoea = 牙周病
lose teeth to pyorrhoea = 因牙周病掉牙
② Old advertisements for toothpaste warned that pyorrhoea could strike anyone.
= 老牙膏广告警告说谁都可能得牙周病。
a warning about pyorrhoea = 牙周病的警告
如今通称 gum disease、periodontitis。

pyramid
/ˈpɪrəmɪd/
核心：三支，可数。一是金字塔：古埃及等地方底、四个三角形侧面的巨大石砌陵墓；二是（几何）棱锥、角锥；也指金字塔形的一堆东西；
三是金字塔式的结构：上面少、下面多的组织。形容词 pyramidal 是金字塔形的、角锥状的。
pyramid scheme、pyramid selling 是金字塔式传销骗局。并入本条。
① The great pyramids of Egypt were built more than four thousand years ago.
= 埃及的大金字塔建于四千多年前。
the pyramids of Egypt = 埃及金字塔
a pyramid = 金字塔
② The shop assistant built a pyramid of tins in the window.
= 店员在橱窗里用罐头垒了一座金字塔。
a pyramid of something = 金字塔形的一堆东西
③ The firm is a pyramid, with a few managers at the top and hundreds of workers below.
= 那家公司是金字塔结构，上头几个经理，下面几百个工人。
a pyramid = 金字塔式的结构
the top of the pyramid = 塔尖
④ Thousands of people lost their savings in the pyramid scheme.
= 成千上万的人在那场金字塔式传销骗局里赔光了积蓄。
a pyramid scheme = 金字塔式传销骗局
pyramid selling = 金字塔式传销
⑤ The tower has a pyramidal roof covered with copper.
= 那座塔有个包铜的金字塔形屋顶。
a pyramidal roof = 金字塔形的屋顶
pyramidal = 金字塔形的、角锥状的

pyre
/ˈpaɪə(r)/
核心：（火化尸体用的）柴堆、火葬堆。可数。也说 funeral pyre。
① The body of the old king was burned on a pyre by the river.
= 老国王的遗体在河边的柴堆上火化了。
a pyre = 火葬堆
burn something on a pyre = 在柴堆上焚化某物
② The villagers built a funeral pyre of dry branches on the hill.
= 村民们在山上用干树枝堆了一座火葬堆。
a funeral pyre = 火葬堆

pyrethrum
/paɪˈriːθrəm/
核心：两支。一是除虫菊：一种开白、红色花的菊科植物，可数；二是除虫菊粉：用这种花晒干磨成的杀虫剂，不可数。
① The farmers grow pyrethrum on the slopes of the mountain.
= 农民在山坡上种除虫菊。
grow pyrethrum = 种除虫菊
pyrethrum = 除虫菊
② The spray contains pyrethrum and is safe to use near children.
= 那种喷剂含除虫菊粉，在小孩旁边用也安全。
contain pyrethrum = 含除虫菊粉
是天然的杀虫成分。

pyrites
/paɪˈraɪtiːz/
核心：（矿物）黄铁矿：一种金黄色、有金属光泽的矿石，常被误认为金子，俗称 fool's gold。不可数。
① The miners were excited until they realized that the shining rock was only pyrites.
= 矿工们一阵兴奋，后来才明白那块闪光的石头不过是黄铁矿。
pyrites = 黄铁矿
iron pyrites = 黄铁矿
② Pyrites is often called fool's gold because it looks so much like the real thing.
= 黄铁矿常被叫作「愚人金」，因为它看着太像真金了。
fool's gold = 愚人金（黄铁矿）

pyromania
/ˌpaɪrəʊˈmeɪniə/
核心：纵火狂：控制不住想放火的病态冲动。不可数。有这种毛病的人叫 pyromaniac，可数；口语简称 pyro，可数，也指（玩笑）特爱玩火、玩烟花的人。并入本条。
① The doctors said that his pyromania began in childhood.
= 医生们说他的纵火狂从童年就开始了。
pyromania = 纵火狂
② The fires in the forest were started by a pyromaniac who lived nearby.
= 林子里那几场火是住在附近的一个纵火狂放的。
a pyromaniac = 纵火狂
③ My brother is a bit of a pyro and always lights the bonfire himself.
= 我哥哥有点爱玩火，篝火总是他亲手点。
a pyro = （口语）爱玩火的人、纵火狂
这是口语，多带玩笑口气。

pyrotechnics
/ˌpaɪrəˈteknɪks/
核心：两支。一是烟火、烟花表演，复数；也指放烟火的技术，不可数；二是（正式）（说话、演奏的）卖弄技巧、炫技，复数。
形容词 pyrotechnic 是烟火的，也指炫技的。并入本条。
① The festival ended with a display of pyrotechnics over the lake.
= 节日最后在湖上放了一场烟火。
a display of pyrotechnics = 一场烟火表演
pyrotechnics = 烟火
② The pianist's pyrotechnics thrilled the audience but left the critics cold.
= 那位钢琴家的炫技让观众激动，评论家却不为所动。
the pyrotechnics of somebody = 某人的炫技
verbal pyrotechnics = 卖弄口才
③ The concert ended with a pyrotechnic display that lit up the whole sky.
= 音乐会以一场照亮整个天空的烟火表演收场。
a pyrotechnic display = 烟火表演
pyrotechnic = 烟火的、炫技的

python
/ˈpaɪθən/
核心：蟒、蟒蛇：一种不带毒的大蛇，靠缠绕把猎物勒死。可数。（计算机）Python 是一种编程语言，首字母大写。
① The python coiled itself round the goat and slowly squeezed it to death.
= 那条蟒缠住山羊，慢慢把它勒死了。
a python = 蟒蛇
a python coils round something = 蟒蛇缠住某物
② She taught herself to write programs in Python during the summer holidays.
= 她暑假里自学了用 Python 写程序。
Python = （一种编程语言）

qi
/tʃiː/
核心：气：中国传统医学、武术里说的流转全身的生命能量。不可数。也拼作 chi。
① The teacher said that the slow movements help qi to flow through the body.
= 老师说那些缓慢的动作能让气在全身流转。
qi = 气
the flow of qi = 气的流转
② Acupuncture is said to work by unblocking the qi in certain parts of the body.
= 据说针灸的原理是疏通身体某些部位的气。
unblock the qi = 疏通气
是汉语借词，读作 chee。

qua
/kweɪ/
核心：（正式）作为、以……的身份：只就……这一面来说。介词，放在两个相同或相关的名词之间。
① The book is interesting as history, but it fails as a novel qua novel.
= 那本书作为历史读物有意思，单作为小说来看却不成功。
a novel qua novel = 单作为小说来看
qua = 作为、就……而言
② The minister was speaking qua minister and not as a private citizen.
= 部长是以部长的身份讲话，不是以普通公民的身份。
speak qua minister = 以部长的身份讲话
是拉丁语借词，用于很正式的论述。

quack
/kwæk/
核心：动词：（鸭子）嘎嘎叫。名词，可数：鸭叫声；（含贬义）江湖郎中、庸医：冒充医生、卖假药的人；（英式口语，玩笑）医生。
也作定语：a quack remedy 是骗人的偏方。江湖骗术、庸医的做法叫 quackery，不可数。并入本条。
① The ducks quacked loudly as the children threw bread into the pond.
= 孩子们往池塘里扔面包，鸭子嘎嘎地叫个不停。
a duck quacks = 鸭子嘎嘎叫
quack = 嘎嘎叫
