a ravening army = 凶残的军队

ravenous
/ˈrævənəs/
核心：极饿的、饿坏了的；（胃口）极大的。副词 ravenously，并入本条。
① After the long walk across the hills we were all ravenous.
= 在山里走了很长的路，我们都饿坏了。
be ravenous = 饿坏了
ravenous = 极饿的
② The teenage boys came home from football with ravenous appetites.
= 那几个十几岁的男孩踢完球回家，胃口大得惊人。
a ravenous appetite = 极大的胃口
③ The puppy ate its dinner ravenously and looked round for more.
= 小狗狼吞虎咽地吃完晚饭，还四处张望想要。
eat ravenously = 狼吞虎咽地吃
ravenously = 饥饿地

raver
/ˈreɪvə(r)/
核心：（口语）两支，可数。一是锐舞派对的常客；二是（英式旧式）爱玩、爱泡夜店的人。
① The ravers danced in the field until the sun came up.
= 锐舞派对上的人一直跳到太阳升起。
a raver = 锐舞派对的常客
② His aunt was a bit of a raver in her youth and went dancing every night.
= 他姑妈年轻时挺爱玩，天天晚上去跳舞。
a bit of a raver = 挺爱玩的人

ravine
/rəˈviːn/
核心：峡谷、深谷：两边又陡又窄的深沟。可数。
① The bus came off the road and fell into a deep ravine.
= 那辆公共汽车冲出公路，掉进了一道深谷。
a deep ravine = 深谷
a ravine = 峡谷
② A narrow bridge crosses the ravine high above the river.
= 一座窄桥在河上方高高地跨过峡谷。
cross the ravine = 跨过峡谷

raving
/ˈreɪvɪŋ/
核心：形容词、副词：（口语）疯狂的、胡言乱语的：raving mad、a raving lunatic；十足的、极度的：a raving beauty。
名词 ravings 是胡言乱语、疯话，复数。并入本条。
① He went raving mad after his family was killed in the war.
= 家人在战争中丧生后，他彻底疯了。
raving mad = 彻底疯了
a raving lunatic = 疯子
② Nobody took any notice of the ravings of the old man on the bus.
= 车上那个老头胡言乱语，谁也没理他。
the ravings of somebody = 某人的胡言乱语
③ His sister was a raving beauty who had dozens of admirers.
= 他妹妹是个绝色美人，追求者好几十个。
a raving beauty = 绝色美人

ravioli
/ˌræviˈəʊli/
核心：意大利饺子：小方块面皮里包肉、奶酪、菠菜等馅的意大利面食。不可数。
① We had ravioli filled with spinach and cheese for supper.
= 我们晚饭吃了菠菜奶酪馅的意大利饺子。
ravioli = 意大利饺子
② She made the ravioli by hand and served it with butter and sage.
= 她亲手包了意大利饺子，配黄油和鼠尾草上桌。
make ravioli = 包意大利饺子

ravish
/ˈrævɪʃ/
核心：（文学）两支，及物。一是使着迷、使陶醉，多用被动；二是（旧式）强奸、强暴。
① The audience was ravished by the beauty of her singing.
= 观众被她优美的歌声迷住了。
be ravished by something = 被某物迷住
ravish = 使陶醉
② In the old ballad, the knight rescues a lady who is about to be ravished by bandits.
= 在那首古老的民谣里，骑士救下了一位就要遭强盗强暴的女子。
be ravished = 遭强暴
这一支是旧式文学用法。

ravishing
/ˈrævɪʃɪŋ/
核心：（女人、风景）极美的、迷人的、令人陶醉的。副词 ravishingly，并入本条。
① She looked ravishing in a long red dress at the ball.
= 她在舞会上穿一条红色长裙，美得惊人。
look ravishing = 美得惊人
ravishing = 极美的
② From the top of the hill there is a ravishing view of the lakes.
= 从山顶上望去，湖光美得让人陶醉。
a ravishing view = 令人陶醉的景色
③ The bride was ravishingly beautiful in her grandmother's lace dress.
= 新娘穿着祖母的蕾丝裙，美得让人陶醉。
ravishingly beautiful = 美得让人陶醉

raw
/rɔː/
核心：形容词。一是（食物）生的、没煮过的；二是（材料）未加工的、天然的：raw material、raw cotton；（数据）未经处理的；三是（伤口、皮肤）擦破的、红肿疼痛的；
四是（感情）强烈而露骨的、未加掩饰的；五是（人）没经验的、生手的：a raw recruit；六是（天气）阴冷潮湿的。名词 rawness，不可数。并入本条。
习语：a raw deal 是不公平的待遇；touch a raw nerve、hit a raw nerve 是触到痛处；in the raw 是（口语）赤裸的、原汁原味的。
复合词 raw-boned 是骨瘦如柴的、骨架大的。
① In Japan it is common to eat raw fish.
= 在日本吃生鱼很普遍。
raw fish = 生鱼
raw = 生的
② The country exports raw materials such as wood and iron.
= 那个国家出口木材、铁这类原材料。
raw material = 原材料
raw cotton = 原棉
③ The scientists have not yet looked at the raw data from the survey.
= 科学家们还没看那次调查的原始数据。
raw data = 原始数据
④ Her hands were raw from washing clothes in cold water.
= 她的手在冷水里洗衣服洗得又红又疼。
raw hands = 红肿疼痛的手
a raw wound = 擦破的伤口
⑤ The film shows the raw emotion of the families after the disaster.
= 那部片子展现了灾难过后那些家庭毫不掩饰的悲痛。
raw emotion = 毫不掩饰的感情
⑥ The army was full of raw recruits who had never fired a gun.
= 军队里满是从没开过枪的新兵。
a raw recruit = 新兵
⑦ It was a raw November morning, with a wind off the sea.
= 那是十一月一个阴冷的早晨，海上吹来阵阵风。
a raw morning = 阴冷的早晨
⑧ The workers in the factory feel they have been given a raw deal.
= 工厂里的工人觉得受到了不公平的待遇。
a raw deal = 不公平的待遇
⑨ His question about money touched a raw nerve, and she left the room.
= 他问到钱的事，触到了她的痛处，她走出了房间。
touch a raw nerve = 触到痛处
⑩ The documentary shows life in the city in the raw, without any music or comment.
= 那部纪录片不配音乐、不加评论，原汁原味地展现城市生活。
in the raw = 原汁原味的、赤裸的
⑪ The rawness of the wind made our eyes water.
= 风冷得刺骨，吹得我们直流眼泪。
rawness = 阴冷、生涩
⑫ He was a tall, raw-boned farmer with huge hands.
= 他是个高个子、骨架粗大、手很大的农夫。
raw-boned = 骨架大的、骨瘦如柴的

rawhide
/ˈrɔːhaɪd/
核心：生牛皮：未经鞣制的牛皮；也指用它做的鞭子、绳子。不可数，也可数。
① The cowboy's whip was made of plaited rawhide.
= 那个牛仔的鞭子是用编起来的生牛皮做的。
rawhide = 生牛皮
plaited rawhide = 编起来的生牛皮
② The dog chewed a rawhide bone for hours on the kitchen floor.
= 那条狗在厨房地上啃一根生牛皮做的骨头，啃了好几个小时。
a rawhide bone = 生牛皮骨头

ray
/reɪ/
核心：名词，可数：（光、热的）光线、射线：a ray of light、X-rays；（希望、安慰的）一线、一丝：a ray of hope；鳐：一种扁平的海鱼，长着长尾巴。
习语：a ray of hope 是一线希望；a ray of sunshine 是（口语）给人带来快乐的人或事。
① A ray of light came through a hole in the roof of the barn.
= 一道光线从谷仓屋顶上的洞里射进来。
a ray of light = 一道光线
a ray = 光线
② Sun cream protects the skin from the harmful rays of the sun.
= 防晒霜保护皮肤不受太阳有害射线的伤害。
the rays of the sun = 太阳的射线
③ The new drug has given the patients a ray of hope.
= 新药给病人们带来了一线希望。
a ray of hope = 一线希望
④ Their little daughter is a ray of sunshine in their lives.
= 他们的小女儿是他们生活里的开心果。
a ray of sunshine = 开心果、给人带来快乐的人
⑤ The divers saw a huge ray gliding along the sea bed.
= 潜水员看见一条巨大的鳐鱼沿着海底滑行。
a ray = 鳐

rayon
/ˈreɪɒn/
核心：人造丝、人造纤维：用木浆等纤维素做的、像丝一样的纺织品。不可数。
① The dress is made of rayon and can be washed by hand.
= 那条裙子是人造丝做的，可以手洗。
be made of rayon = 是人造丝做的
rayon = 人造丝
② Rayon was first used as a cheap substitute for silk.
= 人造丝最早是用来代替丝绸的廉价品。
a substitute for silk = 丝绸的代用品

raze
/reɪz/
核心：彻底摧毁、夷平（城镇、建筑），多用被动：raze something to the ground。
① The old village was razed to the ground to make way for the new dam.
= 为了给新水坝腾地方，那个老村子被夷为平地。
be razed to the ground = 被夷为平地
raze = 夷平
② The invading army razed every town in its path.
= 入侵的军队把沿途的每一座城镇都夷平了。
raze a town = 夷平一座城镇

razor
/ˈreɪzə(r)/
核心：剃刀、剃须刀：刮胡子、剃毛用的工具。可数。
习语 on a razor's edge、on the razor's edge 是处在危险的边缘、千钧一发。
复合词：razor blade 是剃须刀片；razor-sharp 是锋利无比的，也指（头脑）极敏锐的；razor-thin 是极薄的、极微小的；razor wire 是带刺的铁丝网；
razor shell 是（英式）竹蛏；razorbill 是刀嘴海雀。
① He shaved with an old-fashioned razor that had belonged to his grandfather.
= 他用一把祖父留下来的老式剃刀刮胡子。
an old-fashioned razor = 老式剃刀
a razor = 剃刀
② She bought a pack of razor blades and a tube of shaving cream.
= 她买了一包剃须刀片和一管剃须膏。
a razor blade = 剃须刀片
③ The chef keeps his knives razor-sharp.
= 那位厨师把刀磨得锋利无比。
razor-sharp = 锋利无比的
a razor-sharp mind = 极敏锐的头脑
④ The government won the vote by a razor-thin majority.
= 政府以极其微弱的多数赢得了那次表决。
a razor-thin majority = 极其微弱的多数
⑤ The prison is surrounded by a high fence topped with razor wire.
= 那座监狱四周是顶上装着带刺铁丝网的高墙。
razor wire = 带刺的铁丝网
⑥ The children collected razor shells along the beach at low tide.
= 孩子们趁退潮在海滩上捡竹蛏壳。
a razor shell = 竹蛏
⑦ Razorbills and puffins nest on the cliffs of the island.
= 刀嘴海雀和海鹦在岛上的悬崖上筑巢。
a razorbill = 刀嘴海雀
⑧ For a week the peace talks were on a razor's edge.
= 有一个星期，和谈处在千钧一发的关头。
on a razor's edge = 千钧一发

razz
/ræz/
核心：（美式口语）取笑、嘲弄、拿……开心。及物。
① His friends razzed him about his new haircut all evening.
= 朋友们一晚上都拿他的新发型取笑他。
razz somebody about something = 拿某事取笑某人
razz = 取笑
② The fans razzed the referee every time he blew his whistle.
= 裁判每吹一次哨，球迷们就嘲弄他一番。
razz the referee = 嘲弄裁判

razzle
/ˈræzl/
核心：（英式旧式口语）on the razzle 是在外面狂欢、出去花天酒地。
① The young sailors went out on the razzle as soon as the ship reached port.
= 船一到港，那些年轻水手就出去狂欢了。
go out on the razzle = 出去狂欢
on the razzle = 在外面狂欢
② He spent his whole first wage packet on a night on the razzle.
= 他把第一份工资全花在一晚上的狂欢上了。
a night on the razzle = 一晚上的狂欢

razzmatazz
/ˌræzməˈtæz/
核心：（口语）热闹的场面、大张旗鼓的宣传：为了引人注意搞的花哨排场。不可数。也拼作 razzamatazz。
① The new car was launched with all the usual razzmatazz of music and lights.
= 那款新车发布时照例搞了一番音乐灯光的热闹排场。
razzmatazz = 热闹的场面
all the razzmatazz = 所有那些花哨排场
② Behind the razzmatazz of the election campaign there was very little real debate.
= 竞选活动热热闹闹，背后却没多少真正的辩论。
the razzmatazz of something = 某事的热闹排场

reach
/riːtʃ/
核心：动词：到达、抵达（某地）：reach the coast；（伸手）够到、够得着：reach the top shelf；伸手去拿：reach for、reach out；（数量、程度）达到：reach a hundred；
（经过讨论）达成（协议、决定）：reach an agreement；（用电话等）联系上：reach somebody；（信息、节目）传到、影响到（人）。
名词：（手臂、能力、影响的）所及范围，不可数或单数：within reach、out of reach；the reaches 是（河流的）河段、（组织的）层级，复数。
短语动词 reach out 是伸出手，也指主动接触、伸出援手：reach out to somebody。
习语：out of reach 是够不着、力所不及；within reach 是够得着、力所能及；within easy reach 是很近、很方便到达。
① We reached the coast just as the sun was going down.
= 太阳快落山时，我们到了海边。
reach the coast = 到达海边
reach = 到达
② The little girl could not reach the biscuits on the top shelf.
= 小女孩够不着最上层架子上的饼干。
reach the top shelf = 够到最上层的架子
reach = 够到
③ He reached for his glasses on the table beside the bed.
= 他伸手去拿床边桌上的眼镜。
reach for something = 伸手去拿某物
④ The temperature reached forty degrees in the shade.
= 背阴处气温达到了四十度。
reach forty degrees = 达到四十度
⑤ After three days of talks the two sides reached an agreement.
= 经过三天的谈判，双方达成了协议。
reach an agreement = 达成协议
reach a decision = 作出决定
⑥ I tried to reach you by phone all afternoon.
= 我一下午都在打电话找你。
reach somebody = 联系上某人
⑦ The programme reaches millions of viewers every week.
= 那档节目每周有几百万观众收看。
reach viewers = 传到观众
⑧ Keep all medicines out of the reach of children.
= 所有药品都要放在儿童够不着的地方。
out of reach = 够不着
out of the reach of children = 儿童够不着的地方
⑨ After years of saving, a house of their own was at last within reach.
= 攒了多年钱，他们终于买得起自己的房子了。
within reach = 够得着、力所能及
⑩ The hotel is within easy reach of the beach and the shops.
= 那家旅馆离海滩和商店都很近。
within easy reach of something = 离某处很近
⑪ The boat sailed slowly up the upper reaches of the river.
= 那条船慢慢驶向河的上游河段。
the upper reaches = 上游河段
⑫ The charity is reaching out to young people who have left home.
= 那个慈善机构在主动接触离家出走的年轻人。
reach out to somebody = 主动接触某人
reach out = 伸出援手

reachable
/ˈriːtʃəbl/
核心：能到达的、够得着的、联系得上的。
① The village is reachable only by boat in the winter.
= 冬天那个村子只能坐船去。
be reachable by boat = 坐船可以到达
reachable = 能到达的
② She keeps her phone on so that she is always reachable at weekends.
= 她周末一直开着手机，好让人随时联系得上。
always reachable = 随时联系得上

reacquaint
/ˌriːəˈkweɪnt/
核心：reacquaint yourself with 是重新熟悉、再次了解：（隔了很久之后）重新认识某事、某人。
① Before the exam I reacquainted myself with the poems we had studied in the first year.
= 考试前我把一年级学过的诗重新温习了一遍。
reacquaint yourself with something = 重新熟悉某事
② After ten years abroad he had to reacquaint himself with the streets of his home town.
= 在国外十年之后，他得重新熟悉家乡的街道。
reacquaint yourself with somewhere = 重新熟悉某处

react
/riˈækt/
核心：不及物。一是（对……）作出反应：react to；二是（因不满而）反抗、反对：react against；三是（化学）起反应：react with；四是（身体对药物、食物）过敏、起不良反应：react to。
① How did your parents react to the news?
= 你父母听到这个消息有什么反应？
react to something = 对某事作出反应
react = 反应
② The police reacted quickly and closed the roads around the bank.
= 警方反应很快，封锁了银行周围的道路。
react quickly = 反应很快
③ Many young people reacted against the strict rules of their parents.
= 许多年轻人反抗父母的严厉规矩。
react against something = 反抗某事
④ Iron reacts with water and air to form rust.
= 铁跟水和空气起反应，生成铁锈。
react with = 与…起反应
⑤ Some patients react badly to the drug and come out in a rash.
= 有些病人对那种药反应很大，起了皮疹。
react badly to something = 对某物反应很大

reaction
/riˈækʃn/
核心：名词：反应：react 的名词，可数也可不可数：a reaction to；反感、抵制，单数：a reaction against；（化学）反应，可数；（身体的）不良反应、过敏，可数；
reactions 是反应能力、反应速度，复数：quick reactions；（政治上的）保守、反动，不可数。复合词 reaction time 是反应时间。
① What was his reaction when you told him?
= 你告诉他的时候，他什么反应？
somebody's reaction = 某人的反应
reaction = 反应（名词）
② The new law has caused an angry reaction from farmers.
= 新法律引起了农民愤怒的反应。
a reaction from somebody = 某人的反应
a reaction to something = 对某事的反应
③ The fashion for short hair was a reaction against the long styles of the sixties.
= 流行短发是对六十年代长发风格的一种反叛。
a reaction against something = 对某事的反叛
④ Heat speeds up the chemical reaction.
= 加热能加快化学反应。
a chemical reaction = 化学反应
⑤ She had a bad reaction to the antibiotics and spent a night in hospital.
= 她对抗生素有严重的不良反应，在医院住了一晚。
a reaction to a drug = 对药物的不良反应
an allergic reaction = 过敏反应
⑥ A goalkeeper needs very quick reactions.
= 守门员需要非常快的反应。
quick reactions = 反应快
⑦ The test measures the driver's reaction time.
= 那项测试测量司机的反应时间。
reaction time = 反应时间
⑧ The party was accused of standing for the forces of reaction.
= 那个党被指代表反动势力。
the forces of reaction = 反动势力

reactionary
/riˈækʃənri/
核心：（含贬义）形容词：反动的、极端保守的：反对政治、社会变革的。名词，可数：反动分子、保守派。
① The newspaper attacked the government's reactionary policies on education.
= 那份报纸抨击政府在教育上的保守政策。
reactionary policies = 保守的政策
reactionary = 反动的、极端保守的
② The old general was a reactionary who wanted to bring back the king.
= 那个老将军是个保守派，想让国王复位。
a reactionary = 反动分子、保守派

reactivate
/riˈæktɪveɪt/
核心：使恢复活动、重新启用、再次激活。及物。名词 reactivation，不可数。并入本条。
① The bank reactivated my old account when I moved back to the town.
= 我搬回那个镇以后，银行重新启用了我的旧账户。
reactivate an account = 重新启用账户
reactivate = 重新启用
② Stress can reactivate the virus years after the first infection.
= 首次感染多年之后，压力也可能让那种病毒再度活跃。
reactivate a virus = 使病毒再度活跃
③ The reactivation of the old railway line will bring tourists back to the valley.
= 那条老铁路线的重新启用会把游客带回山谷。
the reactivation of something = 某物的重新启用

reactive
/riˈæktɪv/
核心：两支。一是被动应付的、只是作出反应的：事情来了才处理，对着说的是 proactive；二是（化学）易起反应的、活泼的。名词 reactivity，不可数。并入本条。
① The government's policy has been reactive rather than planned.
= 政府的政策一直是被动应付，没有规划。
be reactive = 被动应付
reactive = 被动应付的
② Sodium is a highly reactive metal and must be kept in oil.
= 钠是一种非常活泼的金属，必须保存在油里。
highly reactive = 非常活泼的
③ The reactivity of the metal makes it dangerous to handle.
= 那种金属很活泼，拿着很危险。
reactivity = 反应性、活泼性

reactor
/riˈæktə(r)/
核心：（核）反应堆：产生核能的装置；也说 nuclear reactor。可数。
① The power station has two nuclear reactors on the coast.
= 那座发电站在海边有两座核反应堆。
a nuclear reactor = 核反应堆
a reactor = 反应堆
② The reactor was shut down after a leak was found in the cooling system.
= 冷却系统被发现有泄漏之后，反应堆停了机。
shut down the reactor = 让反应堆停机

read
/riːd/
核心：动词，过去式、过去分词 read /red/：读、阅读：read a book、read about；朗读、念：read aloud、read something to somebody；看懂（文字、乐谱、地图、表情）：read music、read a map；
（仪表）显示、读数为；（英式）（在大学）攻读：read history；（文字）读起来、写着：read well、the sign reads；理解、解读：read something as；（电脑）读取；（英式）（议会）审读（议案）。
名词：（口语）读物：a good read，单数；阅读，单数：have a read。
短语动词：read back 是复述、回读；read into 是（对话语）过度解读；read on 是接着往下读；read out 是大声读出；read over、read through 是从头到尾读一遍；
read up on 是（为了解某事）研读、查资料。
习语：read between the lines 是体会言外之意；read somebody's mind 是看透某人的心思；take something as read 是（英式）认为某事理所当然、无须再提；
read somebody like a book 是对某人了如指掌。复合词 read-only 是只读的；read-write 是可读写的；read-in 是（数据）读入。
① She reads a book every week on the train to work.
= 她每周坐火车上班的路上读一本书。
read a book = 读书
read = 读
② I read about the accident in the newspaper this morning.
= 我今天早上在报纸上看到了那起事故。
read about something = 读到某事
③ He read the letter aloud to the whole family.
= 他把信大声念给全家人听。
read something aloud = 大声朗读某物
read something to somebody = 把某物读给某人听
④ The children can read music and play the recorder.
= 孩子们会看乐谱，会吹竖笛。
read music = 看乐谱
read a map = 看地图
⑤ The thermometer read thirty degrees at noon.
= 中午温度计显示三十度。
read thirty degrees = 显示三十度
⑥ She read history at Cambridge before she became a lawyer.
= 她当律师之前在剑桥读历史。
read history = 攻读历史
这一支是英式说法。
⑦ The sign by the gate reads "No entry".
= 门边的牌子上写着「禁止入内」。
the sign reads… = 牌子上写着……
⑧ The novel reads well in English, though it was written in Russian.
= 那部小说虽然原文是俄文，译成英文读起来也不错。
read well = 读起来不错
⑨ His silence was read as a sign that he agreed.
= 他的沉默被理解为他同意了。
be read as something = 被理解为某意思
⑩ The old computer cannot read the files on the new discs.
= 那台旧电脑读不了新光盘上的文件。
read a file = 读取文件
⑪ His new novel is a good read for a long journey.
= 他的新小说很适合在长途旅行中读。
a good read = 好读物
have a read = 读一读
⑫ The operator read the number back to me to make sure it was right.
= 接线员把号码复述给我听，确认没错。
read back = 复述、回读
⑬ Do not read too much into his remarks, for he says that to everyone.
= 别对他的话过度解读，他对谁都那么说。
read into = 过度解读
read too much into something = 对某事过度解读
⑭ The story gets better if you read on.
= 往下读，故事会更精彩。
read on = 接着往下读
⑮ The teacher read out the names of the winners.
= 老师大声念出了获奖者的名字。
read out = 大声读出
read something out = 把某物大声读出
⑯ Read over your answers before you hand in the paper.
= 交卷之前把答案通读一遍。
read over = 从头到尾读一遍
read through = 通读
⑰ Before the trip she read up on the history of the city.
= 旅行之前她查资料了解了那座城市的历史。
read up on something = 研读某事
read up = 查资料
⑱ Her letter was polite, but if you read between the lines you could tell she was angry.
= 她的信很客气，但体会一下言外之意，就知道她生气了。
read between the lines = 体会言外之意
⑲ "You want a cup of tea." "How did you read my mind?"
= 「你想喝杯茶。」「你怎么看透我的心思的？」
read somebody's mind = 看透某人的心思
⑳ We can take it as read that everyone agrees with the plan.
= 我们可以认为大家都同意那个计划，不用再提了。
take something as read = 认为某事理所当然
㉑ I have known him for so long that I can read him like a book.
= 我认识他太久了，他想什么我一清二楚。
read somebody like a book = 对某人了如指掌
㉒ The files on the disc are read-only and cannot be changed.
= 那张光盘上的文件是只读的，不能改。
read-only = 只读的
read-write = 可读写的
㉓ The read-in of the data takes about ten minutes.
= 数据读入大约要十分钟。
the read-in = （数据）读入

readability
/ˌriːdəˈbɪləti/
核心：可读性、易读性：文字好不好读、读起来顺不顺。不可数。
① The editor cut the long sentences to improve the readability of the report.
= 编辑把长句子砍短，让那份报告更好读。
the readability of something = 某物的可读性
readability = 可读性
② The new typeface was chosen for its readability on small screens.
= 选那种新字体，是因为它在小屏幕上好认。
readability on screens = 在屏幕上的易读性

readable
/ˈriːdəbl/
核心：两支。一是（书、文章）好读的、读起来有趣的；二是（字迹、印刷）清楚易认的、看得清的。
① Her history of the war is scholarly but very readable.
= 她写的那部战争史很有学术分量，却非常好读。
very readable = 非常好读
readable = 好读的
② The old letter was faded but still readable in places.
= 那封旧信褪了色，有些地方还看得清。
still readable = 还看得清
③ The files are stored in a form that is readable by any computer.
= 那些文件以任何电脑都能读取的格式保存。
readable by a computer = 电脑能读取的

readdress
/ˌriːəˈdres/
核心：两支，及物。一是（在信封上）改写地址、转寄（信件）；二是重新处理、再次讨论（问题）。
① Please readdress any letters for me to my new flat in Leeds.
= 请把给我的信改寄到我在利兹的新公寓。
readdress letters = 改寄信件
readdress = 改写地址
② The committee will readdress the question of school meals next month.
= 委员会下个月会再讨论学校午餐的问题。
readdress a question = 再次讨论问题

reader
/ˈriːdə(r)/
核心：可数。一是读者、读书的人：a keen reader；二是（英国）（大学的）高级讲师：职位在教授之下；三是读本、课本：a reader for beginners；四是阅读器、读取器：a card reader、an e-reader；
五是（出版社的）审稿人：看投稿、决定用不用的人。
① She has always been a keen reader of detective stories.
= 她一向爱读侦探小说。
a keen reader = 爱读书的人
a reader = 读者
② The newspaper has lost a third of its readers in five years.
= 那份报纸五年里流失了三分之一的读者。
its readers = 它的读者
③ He is a reader in physics at the University of London.
= 他是伦敦大学的物理学高级讲师。
a reader in something = 某学科的高级讲师
这是英国大学的职称。
④ The children are learning from a simple reader with large pictures.
= 孩子们用一本配有大图的简易读本学习。
a reader = 读本
⑤ Swipe your card through the reader to open the door.
= 在读卡器上刷一下卡开门。
a card reader = 读卡器
an e-reader = 电子阅读器
⑥ The publisher's reader said that the novel was far too long.
= 出版社的审稿人说那部小说太长了。
a publisher's reader = 出版社审稿人

readership
/ˈriːdəʃɪp/
核心：两支。一是（报刊的）读者群、读者人数，可数，多用单数：a readership of；二是（英国大学的）高级讲师职位，可数。
① The magazine has a readership of about two hundred thousand.
= 那本杂志有大约二十万读者。
a readership of something = 某数量的读者
readership = 读者群
② The paper is trying to attract a younger readership.
= 那份报纸想吸引年轻一些的读者群。
a younger readership = 年轻一些的读者群

readily
/ˈredɪli/
核心：两支。一是爽快地、乐意地、毫不犹豫地：agree readily；二是容易地、轻易地、随时：readily available。
① She readily agreed to look after the children while we were away.
= 我们不在的时候，她爽快地答应帮忙照看孩子。
readily agree = 爽快地答应
readily = 爽快地
② Fresh fish is readily available in the markets by the harbour.
= 港口边的市场随时都买得到新鲜的鱼。
readily available = 随时可得的
③ I readily admit that I made a mistake about the date.
= 我坦率承认把日期弄错了。
readily admit something = 坦率承认某事

readiness
/ˈredinəs/
核心：两支，不可数。一是准备就绪：in readiness for；二是乐意、愿意：a readiness to do。
① The hall was decorated in readiness for the party.
= 大厅布置好了，准备开派对。
in readiness for something = 为某事作好准备
readiness = 准备就绪
② She showed a readiness to listen to other people's ideas.
= 她表现出愿意听取别人想法的态度。
a readiness to do something = 乐意做某事

reading
/ˈriːdɪŋ/
核心：名词：阅读、读书，不可数：reading and writing；读物、阅读材料，不可数：light reading；（公开的）朗诵会、宣读，可数：a poetry reading；
（仪表的）读数，可数：a meter reading；理解、解读，可数：my reading of the situation；（英国议会对议案的）一读、二读、三读，可数。形容词：阅读的、阅读用的。
复合词 reading room 是阅览室。
① Reading is her favourite way to relax after work.
= 读书是她下班后最喜欢的放松方式。
reading = 阅读
② The children are learning reading and writing in their first year at school.
= 孩子们上学第一年学读书写字。
reading and writing = 读书写字
③ I took some light reading for the long flight.
= 我带了些轻松的读物在长途飞行时看。
light reading = 轻松的读物
④ The poet gave a reading of her new poems at the library.
= 那位诗人在图书馆朗诵了她的新诗。
a poetry reading = 诗歌朗诵会
a reading = 朗诵会
⑤ The man from the gas company came to take a meter reading.
= 煤气公司的人来抄表。
a meter reading = 仪表读数
⑥ My reading of the situation is that they will accept the offer.
= 依我看，他们会接受那个提议。
somebody's reading of something = 某人对某事的理解
⑦ The bill passed its second reading in parliament by twenty votes.
= 那项议案以二十票之差在议会通过了二读。
the second reading = 二读
⑧ The students study quietly in the reading room of the old library.
= 学生们在老图书馆的阅览室里安静地学习。
a reading room = 阅览室

readjust
/ˌriːəˈdʒʌst/
核心：两支。一是重新适应：readjust to，不及物；二是（重新）调整、校准：及物。名词 readjustment，可数也可不可数。并入本条。
① It took him months to readjust to life outside prison.
= 他花了好几个月才重新适应狱外的生活。
readjust to something = 重新适应某事
readjust = 重新适应
② She readjusted the mirror before she drove off.
= 她开车之前重新调了调镜子。
readjust the mirror = 重新调镜子
③ The return to normal life after the war was a painful readjustment.
= 战后回到正常生活，是一个痛苦的重新适应过程。
a readjustment = 重新适应、重新调整

readmit
/ˌriːədˈmɪt/
核心：重新接纳、再次准许进入：（尤其）让病人再次住院、让学生复学。及物，过去式 readmitted，多用被动。名词 readmission，不可数也可数。并入本条。
① She was readmitted to hospital a week after the operation.
= 手术一个星期后，她又住进了医院。
be readmitted to hospital = 再次住院
readmit = 重新接纳
② The school agreed to readmit the boy if his behaviour improved.
= 学校同意，只要那个男孩表现好转，就让他复学。
readmit somebody = 让某人复学
③ The number of readmissions after surgery has fallen this year.
= 今年术后再次住院的人数下降了。
readmission = 再次住院、重新接纳

ready
/ˈredi/
核心：形容词，不放在名词前：准备好的、预备好的：be ready for、be ready to do；（东西）准备好的、可以用了的：dinner is ready；乐意的、愿意的：ready to help；
就要、快要：ready to cry、ready to drop。放在名词前：迅速的、现成的、随时可用的：a ready answer、ready cash。
动词：（正式）使作好准备：ready yourself for。名词 the ready 是（英式口语）现金。
习语：ready and waiting 是准备就绪、只等着；get ready 是作准备。
复合词：ready-made 是现成的；ready reckoner 是（英式）计算便查表；ready-to-wear 是（衣服）成衣的、现成的。
① Are you ready to go, or do you need more time?
= 你准备好走了吗，还是要再等一会儿？
be ready to do = 准备好做某事
ready = 准备就绪的
② The team is ready for the final on Saturday.
= 球队已为星期六的决赛做好了准备。
be ready for something = 为某事做好准备
③ Hurry up and get ready, for the taxi will be here in five minutes.
= 快点做准备，出租车五分钟后就到。
get ready = 做准备
get ready for something = 为某事作准备
④ Dinner is ready, so come and sit down.
= 饭好了，过来坐吧。
dinner is ready = 饭好了
⑤ She is always ready to help her neighbours.
= 她总是乐意帮邻居的忙。
be ready to help = 乐意帮忙
⑥ I was so tired that I was ready to drop.
= 我累得快要倒下了。
be ready to drop = 累得快倒下
⑦ He always has a ready answer for every question.
= 每个问题他都有现成的答案。
a ready answer = 现成的答案
ready cash = 现金
⑧ The soldiers readied themselves for the attack.
= 士兵们作好了进攻准备。
ready yourself for something = 为某事作好准备
⑨ The car was ready and waiting outside the door.
= 车已经在门口等着了。
ready and waiting = 准备就绪、只等着
⑩ They bought ready-made curtains instead of having them made.
= 他们买了现成的窗帘，没去订做。
ready-made = 现成的
⑪ The shop sells ready-to-wear clothes by famous designers.
= 那家店卖名设计师的成衣。
ready-to-wear = 成衣的
⑫ The old shopkeeper used a ready reckoner to work out the prices.
= 那个老店主用计算便查表算价钱。
a ready reckoner = 计算便查表

reaffirm
/ˌriːəˈfɜːm/
核心：重申、再次确认（立场、承诺）。及物。名词 reaffirmation，可数也可不可数。并入本条。
① The government reaffirmed its promise to build a new hospital in the town.
= 政府重申了在那个镇新建一家医院的承诺。
reaffirm a promise = 重申承诺
reaffirm = 重申
② The two leaders reaffirmed that their countries would work together.
= 两位领导人重申两国将会合作。
reaffirm that… = 重申……
③ The meeting ended with a reaffirmation of the club's old rules.
= 会议最后再次确认了俱乐部的老规矩。
a reaffirmation of something = 对某事的再次确认

reafforestation
/ˌriːəˌfɒrɪˈsteɪʃn/
核心：（英式）重新造林、再造林：在砍伐过的地方重新种树。不可数。也说 reforestation。
① The government has started a programme of reafforestation in the hills.
= 政府在山区启动了一项重新造林计划。
a programme of reafforestation = 重新造林计划
reafforestation = 重新造林
② Reafforestation will help to stop the soil from being washed away.
= 重新造林有助于防止水土流失。
reafforestation will help = 重新造林有帮助

reagent
/riˈeɪdʒənt/
核心：（化学）试剂：用来检测、制造其他物质的化学物质。可数。
① The laboratory ran out of the reagent needed for the blood test.
= 实验室验血要用的试剂用完了。
a reagent = 试剂
② Add a few drops of the reagent and watch the colour change.
= 加几滴试剂，看颜色怎么变。
add the reagent = 加试剂

real
/ˈriːəl/
核心：形容词：真的、真正的、真实的：存在的、不是想象的；真心的、实实在在的：real friends；纯正的、不是仿造的：real leather；（用来强调）十足的、真正的：a real problem；
（经济）实际的、扣除物价因素后的：real wages。副词：（美式口语）非常、真：real good。
习语：for real 是（口语）真的、当真的；get real 是（口语）现实点吧、别做梦了；the real thing 是真货、真爱。
复合词 real tennis 是（英式）室内网球；real time 是实时：in real time；real-world 是现实世界的。
① Is this a real diamond or a piece of glass?
= 这是真钻石还是一块玻璃？
a real diamond = 真钻石
real = 真的
② The children were frightened, though the danger was not real.
= 孩子们吓坏了，其实危险并不真实存在。
be real = 真实存在
③ She has lots of friends at school but no real friends.
= 她在学校里朋友很多，却没有真心朋友。
real friends = 真心朋友
④ The bag is made of real leather, not plastic.
= 那个包是真皮的，不是塑料的。
real leather = 真皮
⑤ Finding a parking space is a real problem in the town centre.
= 在镇中心找停车位真是个大难题。
a real problem = 真正的难题
⑥ Real wages have fallen for the third year running.
= 实际工资连续第三年下降。
real wages = 实际工资
⑦ The food was real good, so we went back the next day.
= 饭菜真好吃，我们第二天又去了。
real good = 真好
这是美式口语。
⑧ Is this offer for real, or is it a joke?
= 这个提议是真的，还是开玩笑？
for real = 真的、当真的
⑨ You think he will pay you back? Get real!
= 你以为他会还你钱？现实点吧！
get real = 现实点吧
⑩ After years of cheap copies, she finally bought the real thing.
= 买了多年便宜仿制品，她终于买了真货。
the real thing = 真货
⑪ The results of the election were shown on the screen in real time.
= 选举结果实时显示在屏幕上。
in real time = 实时
real time = 实时
⑫ The course teaches students to solve real-world problems.
= 那门课教学生解决现实世界中的问题。
real-world = 现实世界的
⑬ He plays real tennis on an old indoor court in London.
= 他在伦敦一个老式室内场打室内网球。
real tennis = 室内网球
⑭ He was really pleased with the result.
= 他对结果真的很满意。
really = 真的、非常（副词）
⑮ It took him years to accept the reality of his situation.
= 他花了好几年才接受自己处境的现实。
reality = 现实（名词）

realign
/ˌriːəˈlaɪn/
核心：两支。一是重新排列、重新对齐：把东西调回正确的位置；二是（政治上）重新组合、调整立场：realign yourself with。名词 realignment，可数也可不可数。并入本条。
① The mechanic realigned the front wheels of the car.
= 修车工重新校正了汽车前轮的位置。
realign the wheels = 校正车轮
realign = 重新对齐
② After the election several small parties realigned themselves with the government.
= 选举之后，好几个小党转而跟政府站到了一起。
realign yourself with somebody = 转而跟某人站到一起
③ The war led to a realignment of power in the region.
= 那场战争导致了那个地区势力的重新组合。
a realignment of something = 某事的重新组合
realignment = 重新组合、重新调整

realism
/ˈriːəlɪzəm/
核心：两支，不可数。一是现实态度、务实：面对实际情况、不抱幻想；二是（艺术、文学的）现实主义、写实：如实描绘生活。
① There is a new sense of realism about what the team can achieve this season.
= 对球队这个赛季能取得什么成绩，大家有了新的现实看法。
a sense of realism = 现实态度
realism = 务实
② The film was praised for its realism and its fine acting.
= 那部片子因写实和精湛的表演而受到称赞。
its realism = 它的写实
③ The novels of the period show a move towards social realism.
= 那个时期的小说表现出转向社会现实主义的趋势。
social realism = 社会现实主义

realist
/ˈriːəlɪst/
核心：可数。一是现实的人、务实的人：看清实际情况、不抱幻想的人；二是（艺术、文学的）现实主义者、写实派。也作形容词：现实主义的。
① I am a realist, and I know we cannot win every match.
= 我是个务实的人，知道我们不可能场场都赢。
a realist = 现实的人
② He was one of the great realist painters of the nineteenth century.
= 他是十九世纪伟大的写实派画家之一。
a realist painter = 写实派画家
realist = 现实主义的

realistic
/ˌriːəˈlɪstɪk/
核心：三支。一是现实的、切合实际的：a realistic target；二是（看法、态度）实事求是的、务实的：be realistic about；三是逼真的、栩栩如生的。反义 unrealistic。
副词 realistically，并入本条。
① We need to set a realistic target for the number of new members.
= 我们得给新会员人数定一个切合实际的目标。
a realistic target = 切合实际的目标
realistic = 现实的
② Let us be realistic about how much we can do with so little money.
= 钱这么少，我们能做多少事，还是实事求是一点吧。
be realistic about something = 对某事实事求是
③ The dinosaurs in the film looked frighteningly realistic.
= 片子里的恐龙逼真得吓人。
look realistic = 看起来逼真
④ Realistically, the house will not be finished before the summer.
= 实事求是地说，那栋房子夏天以前完不了工。
realistically = 实事求是地说、现实地

reality
/riˈæləti/
核心：两支。一是现实、实际情况，不可数：face reality；二是事实、现实情况，可数：the realities of life、become a reality。习语 in reality 是实际上、事实上。
复合词 reality TV、reality television 是真人秀节目。
① He refused to face reality and went on spending money he did not have.
= 他不肯面对现实，继续花他没有的钱。
face reality = 面对现实
reality = 现实（名词）
② The film gives a true picture of the harsh realities of life in prison.
= 那部片子真实地展现了监狱生活的残酷现实。
the realities of something = 某事的现实情况
③ After years of planning, the new bridge has become a reality.
= 规划了多年，新桥终于成了现实。
become a reality = 成为现实
④ He seemed confident, but in reality he was terrified.
= 他看起来很自信，实际上吓得要命。
in reality = 实际上
⑤ She became famous after appearing on a reality TV show.
= 她上了一档真人秀节目之后出了名。
reality TV = 真人秀节目

realizable
/ˈriːəlaɪzəbl/
核心：（正式）两支。一是能实现的、可以做到的；二是（资产）能变现的、可以换成现金的。英式也拼 realisable。
① The plan is ambitious but realizable within five years.
= 那个计划雄心勃勃，但五年内能够实现。
realizable within five years = 五年内能够实现
realizable = 能实现的
② The firm's realizable assets were not enough to pay its debts.
= 那家公司可变现的资产不够还债。
realizable assets = 可变现的资产

realization
/ˌriːəlaɪˈzeɪʃn/
核心：三支。一是意识到、认识到，不可数也可单数：the realization that；二是（愿望、计划的）实现，不可数：the realization of；三是（资产的）变现，不可数。英式也拼 realisation。
① There is a growing realization that the old methods no longer work.
= 人们越来越认识到老办法已经不管用了。
a growing realization that… = 越来越认识到……
realization = 意识到
② The sudden realization that he was lost made him stop.
= 他突然意识到自己迷路了，就停了下来。
the sudden realization = 突然意识到
③ The new hospital is the realization of a dream she had for thirty years.
= 那家新医院实现了她三十年的梦想。
the realization of a dream = 梦想的实现

realize
/ˈriːəlaɪz/
核心：及物。一是意识到、认识到、明白：realize that、realize how；二是实现（愿望、计划）：realize a dream、realize your potential；
三是（正式）（卖东西）换得、卖得（钱）；四是使（担心的事）成为现实，多用被动：our worst fears were realized。英式也拼 realise。
① I did not realize that you were waiting for me outside.
= 我没意识到你在外面等我。
realize that… = 意识到……
realize = 意识到
② She suddenly realized how late it was and ran for the bus.
= 她突然意识到时间那么晚了，就跑去赶公共汽车。
realize how… = 意识到多么……
③ He finally realized his dream of sailing round the world.
= 他终于实现了驾帆船环游世界的梦想。
realize a dream = 实现梦想
realize your potential = 发挥自己的潜力
④ The painting realized two million pounds at the auction.
= 那幅画在拍卖会上卖了两百万英镑。
realize two million pounds = 卖得两百万英镑
⑤ Our worst fears were realized when the letter arrived.
= 信一到，我们最担心的事成了现实。
be realized = 成为现实

reallocate
/riːˈæləkeɪt/
核心：重新分配、重新调拨（钱、资源、人员）。及物。名词 reallocation，可数也可不可数。并入本条。
① The council has reallocated money from roads to schools.
= 镇议会把钱从修路改拨给了学校。
reallocate money = 重新调拨资金
reallocate = 重新分配
② The reallocation of offices caused a lot of arguments among the staff.
= 重新分配办公室在员工中引起了很多争吵。
the reallocation of something = 某物的重新分配

really
/ˈriːəli/
核心：副词。一是真的、确实：用来强调说的是事实：I really did see him；二是非常、很：really good；三是（用在否定句里减轻语气）不太：not really；
四是（表示惊讶、感兴趣）真的吗：Really?；五是（表示不满）真是的：Really, you are so careless!。
① I really did see a fox in the garden last night.
= 我昨晚真的在园子里看见了一只狐狸。
really = 真的
② The food at the new restaurant is really good.
= 那家新餐馆的饭菜真的很好。
really good = 非常好
③ "Do you like jazz?" "Not really, I prefer classical music."
= 「你喜欢爵士乐吗？」「不太喜欢，我更喜欢古典音乐。」
not really = 不太
④ "She has left her job and gone to live in Spain." "Really?"
= 「她辞了工作，去西班牙住了。」「真的吗？」
Really? = 真的吗？
⑤ Really, you might have told me you were coming home late!
= 真是的，你晚回来也该跟我说一声！
Really, … = 真是的……

realm
/relm/
核心：两支，可数。一是（正式）领域、范围：the realm of science；二是（文学）王国、国度。习语 within the realms of possibility 是有可能的。
① The idea belongs to the realm of science fiction, not science.
= 那个想法属于科幻小说的范畴，不属于科学。
the realm of something = 某事的领域
a realm = 领域
② A cure for the disease within ten years is within the realms of possibility.
= 十年内治好那种病，是有可能的。
within the realms of possibility = 有可能的
③ The king travelled to every corner of his realm.
= 国王走遍了他王国的每一个角落。
his realm = 他的王国

realpolitik
/reɪˈɑːlpɒlɪtiːk/
核心：现实政治、实力政治：以实际利益而不是道德原则为依据的政治。不可数。是德语借词。
① The decision to sell arms to the dictator was a piece of cold realpolitik.
= 卖军火给那个独裁者的决定，是冷冰冰的现实政治。
cold realpolitik = 冷冰冰的现实政治
realpolitik = 现实政治
② In the world of realpolitik, friendships between nations rarely last.
= 在现实政治的世界里，国家之间的友谊很少能长久。
the world of realpolitik = 现实政治的世界

realty
/ˈriːəlti/
核心：（美式，法律）房地产、不动产。不可数。
① He works for a realty firm that sells houses in the suburbs.
= 他在一家卖郊区房子的房地产公司上班。
a realty firm = 房地产公司
realty = 房地产
② Taxes on realty have gone up again this year.
= 今年不动产税又涨了。
taxes on realty = 不动产税

ream
/riːm/
核心：名词：令：纸的计量单位，约五百张，可数；reams 是（口语）大量（的文字）：reams of notes，复数。动词：ream somebody out 是（美式口语）痛骂某人。
① On the way to the office I bought a ream of paper for the new printer.
= 去办公室的路上，我给新打印机买了一令纸。
a ream of paper = 一令纸
a ream = 令
② She wrote reams of notes during the lecture but could not read her own writing afterwards.
= 讲座期间她记了大量笔记，事后却认不出自己写的字。
reams of something = 大量的某物
③ The coach reamed out the players after they lost the game.
= 球队输了之后，教练把球员们痛骂了一顿。
ream somebody out = 痛骂某人
ream out = 痛骂

reanimate
/riˈænɪmeɪt/
核心：（正式）使复活、使恢复生机；使重新活跃。及物。
① In the story the scientist tries to reanimate a dead body with electricity.
= 故事里那个科学家想用电让一具尸体复活。
reanimate a body = 让尸体复活
reanimate = 使复活
② The new director has reanimated the old theatre with a series of modern plays.
= 新总监用一系列现代戏剧让那家老剧院重新焕发了生机。
reanimate something = 使某物重新活跃

reap
/riːp/
核心：两支，及物。一是收割（庄稼）；二是获得、得到（好处、报应）：reap the benefits、reap the rewards。习语 you reap what you sow 是种瓜得瓜、自食其果。
① The farmers reaped the corn by hand in those days.
= 那年头农民用手收割玉米。
reap the corn = 收割庄稼
reap = 收割
② If you work hard now, you will reap the rewards later.
= 你现在努力，以后会得到回报。
reap the rewards = 得到回报
reap the benefits = 得到好处
③ He was cruel to everyone, and in the end he reaped what he had sown.
= 他对谁都很残忍，最后落得个自食其果。
reap what you sow = 自食其果

reaper
/ˈriːpə(r)/
核心：两支，可数。一是收割机；（旧式）收割者；二是 the Grim Reaper：死神：拿着大镰刀、象征死亡的人形。
① The old reaper stood rusting in the corner of the field.
= 那台旧收割机在田地一角锈着。
a reaper = 收割机
② In the painting the Grim Reaper stands at the foot of the bed.
= 画里死神站在床脚边。
the Grim Reaper = 死神

reappear
/ˌriːəˈpɪə(r)/
核心：再次出现、重新出现。不及物。名词 reappearance，可数也可不可数。并入本条。
① The sun reappeared from behind the clouds just as the race began.
= 比赛刚开始，太阳又从云后面出来了。
reappear = 再次出现
② The missing dog reappeared three days later, tired and hungry.
= 那条走失的狗三天后又出现了，又累又饿。
reappear later = 后来又出现
③ The reappearance of the disease has worried the doctors.
= 那种病又出现了，医生们很担心。
the reappearance of something = 某物的再次出现

reapply
/ˌriːəˈplaɪ/
核心：两支。一是再次申请：reapply for，不及物；二是再涂、再抹（霜、油漆），及物。过去式 reapplied。
① She failed the first time but reapplied for the job a year later.
= 她第一次没录上，一年后又申请了那份工作。
reapply for something = 再次申请某事
reapply = 再次申请
② Reapply the sun cream every two hours when you are on the beach.
= 在海滩上要每两个小时再涂一次防晒霜。
reapply sun cream = 再涂防晒霜

reappoint
/ˌriːəˈpɔɪnt/
核心：重新任命、再次委任。及物。名词 reappointment，可数也可不可数。并入本条。
① The board reappointed her as chairman for another three years.
= 董事会再次任命她当三年主席。
reappoint somebody as something = 再次任命某人为某职
reappoint = 重新任命
② His reappointment as head of the school was welcomed by the parents.
= 他再次被任命为校长，家长们表示欢迎。
somebody's reappointment = 某人的再次任命

reappraise
/ˌriːəˈpreɪz/
核心：（正式）重新评价、重新审视。及物。名词 reappraisal，可数也可不可数。并入本条。
① The crisis forced the government to reappraise its policy on energy.
= 那场危机迫使政府重新审视它的能源政策。
reappraise a policy = 重新审视政策
reappraise = 重新评价
② The exhibition led to a reappraisal of the painter's early work.
= 那次展览让人们重新评价那位画家的早期作品。
a reappraisal of something = 对某事的重新评价

rear
/rɪə(r)/
核心：名词：后部、后面，单数：the rear of；（口语）屁股，可数。形容词：后部的、后面的，只放在名词前：the rear window。
动词：养育、抚养（孩子）、饲养（动物），多用被动；（马）用后腿直立：rear up；（建筑、山）高耸；rear its head 是（坏事）出现、露头：rear its ugly head。
习语 bring up the rear 是走在最后、排在末尾。
① The kitchen is at the rear of the house, looking onto the garden.
= 厨房在房子后部，朝着园子。
at the rear of something = 在某物的后部
the rear = 后部
② He sat on the floor and fell on his rear.
= 他往地上一坐，屁股着了地。
his rear = 他的屁股
③ Someone had smashed the rear window of the car.
= 有人砸碎了汽车的后窗。
the rear window = 后窗
a rear wheel = 后轮
④ She was born in Wales but reared in London.
= 她生在威尔士，在伦敦长大。
be reared = 被养大
rear children = 养孩子
⑤ The farm rears pigs and chickens for the local market.
= 那个农场养猪养鸡，供应本地市场。
rear pigs = 养猪
⑥ The horse reared up and threw its rider to the ground.
= 那匹马用后腿立起，把骑手摔到地上。
rear up = （马）用后腿直立
rear on = （文学）扬起前蹄冲向
⑦ Just when the team seemed safe, the old problem of injuries reared its head again.
= 正当球队似乎安全了，伤病这个老问题又冒了出来。
rear its head = （坏事）露头
rear its ugly head = 冒出来
⑧ The youngest children brought up the rear of the long line of walkers.
= 最小的孩子们走在长长的徒步队伍最后。
bring up the rear = 走在最后

rearguard
/ˈrɪəɡɑːd/
核心：（军队的）后卫部队：撤退时在最后面掩护主力的士兵。单数，动词单复数都行。
① The rearguard held the bridge while the rest of the army escaped.
= 后卫部队守住桥，让大部队撤走。
the rearguard = 后卫部队
② The general left a small rearguard to slow down the enemy.
= 将军留下一小支后卫部队拖住敌人。
a small rearguard = 一小支后卫部队

rearing
/ˈrɪərɪŋ/
核心：养育、抚养（孩子）；饲养（动物）。不可数。常构成复合词：child-rearing、cattle rearing。
① She wrote a book on child-rearing that sold millions of copies.
= 她写了一本育儿的书，卖了几百万册。
child-rearing = 育儿
rearing = 养育
② Sheep rearing is the main business of the hill farms.
= 山区农场的主业是养羊。
sheep rearing = 养羊

rearm
/riˈɑːm/
核心：重新武装、重整军备：再次装备武器，及物也可不及物。名词 rearmament，不可数。并入本条。
① The country began to rearm in the years before the war.
= 那个国家在战前那几年开始重整军备。
rearm = 重新武装
② The rebels were rearmed with weapons smuggled across the border.
= 叛军用从边境走私进来的武器重新武装起来。
be rearmed with something = 用某物重新武装
③ The government spent a fortune on rearmament.
= 政府在重整军备上花了一大笔钱。
rearmament = 重整军备

rearmost
/ˈrɪəməʊst/
核心：最后面的、最后部的。只放在名词前。
① We sat in the rearmost seats of the bus so that we could see the road behind.
= 我们坐在公共汽车最后排，好看后面的路。
the rearmost seats = 最后排的座位
rearmost = 最后面的
② The rearmost carriage of the train came off the rails.
= 火车最后一节车厢出轨了。
the rearmost carriage = 最后一节车厢

rearrange
/ˌriːəˈreɪndʒ/
核心：两支，及物。一是重新排列、重新布置：rearrange the furniture；二是重新安排（时间）、改期：rearrange a meeting。名词 rearrangement，可数也可不可数。并入本条。
① We rearranged the furniture to make more room for the piano.
= 我们重新摆了家具，给钢琴腾出更多地方。
rearrange the furniture = 重新摆家具
rearrange = 重新布置
② The match has been rearranged for next Tuesday because of the rain.
= 因为下雨，比赛改到了下星期二。
be rearranged for something = 改期到某时
③ The rearrangement of the timetable caused confusion among the students.
= 课表重新安排，让学生们一头雾水。
the rearrangement of something = 某事的重新安排

rearward
/ˈrɪəwəd/
核心：形容词：向后的、在后部的。副词：向后、朝后：也说 rearwards。
① The pilot made a rearward glance to check the other planes.
= 飞行员往后瞥了一眼，确认其他飞机的位置。
a rearward glance = 往后瞥一眼
rearward = 向后的
② The seats in the train can face forward or rearward.
= 火车上的座位可以朝前也可以朝后。
face rearward = 朝后

reason
/ˈriːzn/
核心：名词：理由、原因，可数：the reason for、the reason why、the reason that；理智、理性、道理，不可数：lose your reason、listen to reason。
动词：推理、推断：reason that；reason with 是跟……讲道理、劝说；reason out 是（通过思考）想出、推究出。
习语：within reason 是在合理范围内；it stands to reason 是显而易见、理所当然；for some reason 是不知为什么；see reason 是听人劝、明白过来；
by reason of 是（正式）由于；with good reason 是有充分理由。
① What was the reason for his sudden decision to leave?
= 他突然决定离开，是什么理由？
the reason for something = 某事的理由
reason = 理由
② The reason why I am late is that the train broke down.
= 我迟到是因为火车坏了。
the reason why = …的原因
③ Give me one good reason why I should lend you the money.
= 给我一个借钱给你的好理由。
reason = 理由（用来解释）
④ The reason he gave for the accident was the bad weather, but the real cause was his speed.
= 他说那起事故的原因是天气不好，真正的起因却是他开得太快。
cause = 起因（造成结果的）
⑤ The old man had lost his reason after years alone in the house.
= 那个老人在屋里独自待了多年，已经失去了理智。
lose your reason = 失去理智
reason = 理智
⑥ She would not listen to reason and drove off in the storm.
= 她不听劝，冒着暴风雨开车走了。
listen to reason = 听人劝
⑦ He reasoned that the thief must have had a key to the house.
= 他推断贼一定有那栋房子的钥匙。
reason that… = 推断……
⑧ I tried to reason with him, but he would not change his mind.
= 我试着跟他讲道理，他却不肯改变主意。
reason with somebody = 跟某人讲道理
⑨ She sat down with a pencil and reasoned out the answer to the puzzle.
= 她拿着铅笔坐下，推究出了那道谜题的答案。
reason out = 推究出
⑩ You can eat whatever you like, within reason.
= 在合理范围内，你想吃什么都行。
within reason = 在合理范围内
⑪ It stands to reason that a tired driver is a dangerous driver.
= 显而易见，疲劳的司机就是危险的司机。
it stands to reason = 显而易见
⑫ For some reason the lights in the hall would not come on.
= 不知为什么，门厅里的灯就是不亮。
for some reason = 不知为什么
⑬ After a long talk with his father, he finally saw reason.
= 跟父亲长谈之后，他终于明白过来了。
see reason = 明白过来、听人劝
⑭ She was excused from the exam by reason of illness.
= 她因病免考。
by reason of something = 由于某事
⑮ The parents were worried, and with good reason.
= 家长们很担心，而且有充分理由。
with good reason = 有充分理由

reasonable
/ˈriːznəbl/
核心：三支。一是合理的、有道理的、讲道理的：a reasonable request；二是（价格）公道的、不贵的；三是相当好的、还不错的：a reasonable chance。
副词 reasonably 是合理地、相当地；名词 reasonableness，不可数。并入本条。
① It is a reasonable request, so I think we should agree to it.
= 那是个合理的要求，我想我们应该答应。
a reasonable request = 合理的要求
reasonable = 合理的
② Be reasonable, for we cannot finish the work in a single day.
= 讲点道理吧，我们一天干不完这活。
be reasonable = 讲道理
③ The hotel was clean and the prices were very reasonable.
= 那家旅馆很干净，价格也很公道。
reasonable prices = 公道的价格
④ There is a reasonable chance that it will stay dry tomorrow.
= 明天很有可能不下雨。
a reasonable chance = 相当大的可能
⑤ The house is reasonably priced for such a good area.
= 在这么好的地段，那栋房子价格算公道。
reasonably priced = 价格公道
⑥ I am reasonably sure that I locked the back door.
= 我相当肯定我锁了后门。
reasonably sure = 相当肯定
⑦ Everyone admired the reasonableness of her answer.
= 人人都佩服她的回答合情合理。
reasonableness = 合理

reasoned
/ˈriːznd/
核心：（论证、意见）经过推理的、有理有据的、深思熟虑的。只放在名词前。
① She gave a calm and reasoned reply to the angry letter.
= 她对那封气冲冲的信作了冷静而有理有据的回复。
a reasoned reply = 有理有据的回复
reasoned = 经过推理的
② The report contains a reasoned argument for building the new road.
= 报告里有一篇有理有据的、主张修新路的论证。
a reasoned argument = 有理有据的论证

reasoning
/ˈriːzənɪŋ/
核心：推理、推论、论证：思考问题、得出结论的过程。不可数。
① I am afraid I cannot follow your reasoning on the question of the new car park.
= 在新停车场的问题上，恐怕我跟不上你的推理。
somebody's reasoning = 某人的推理
reasoning = 推理
② The reasoning behind the decision was explained in the letter.
= 那封信解释了那个决定背后的理由。
the reasoning behind something = 某事背后的理由

reassemble
/ˌriːəˈsembl/
核心：两支。一是重新组装、重新装配：把拆开的东西装回去，及物；二是重新集合、再次聚集，不及物。
① He took his grandfather's old clock apart on the kitchen table and could not reassemble it.
= 他在厨房桌上把爷爷的旧钟拆开了，却装不回去。
reassemble something = 重新组装某物
reassemble = 重新组装
② After lunch the whole class reassembled in the school hall to hear the results.
= 午饭后全班又在学校礼堂集合，听宣布结果。
reassemble = 重新集合

reassert
/ˌriːəˈsɜːt/
核心：重申、再次坚持（主张、权利）；reassert yourself 是重新确立自己的地位、重新起作用。名词 reassertion，可数也可不可数。并入本条。
① The new manager quickly reasserted her authority over the staff.
= 新经理很快重新确立了她在员工中的威信。
reassert your authority = 重新确立威信
reassert = 再次坚持
② After a few days his old habits began to reassert themselves.
= 几天之后，他的老习惯又开始冒头了。
reassert themselves = 重新起作用
③ The speech was a reassertion of the party's old values.
= 那篇讲话重申了那个党的传统价值观。
a reassertion of something = 对某事的重申

reassess
/ˌriːəˈses/
核心：重新评估、重新考虑。及物。名词 reassessment，可数也可不可数。并入本条。
① After the accident she reassessed her whole way of life.
= 那次事故之后，她重新审视了自己的整个生活方式。
reassess something = 重新评估某事
reassess = 重新评估
② The bank will reassess the value of the house next year.
= 银行明年会重新评估那栋房子的价值。
reassess the value = 重新评估价值
③ The crisis led to a reassessment of the country's defence policy.
= 那场危机导致那个国家重新考虑国防政策。
a reassessment of something = 对某事的重新评估

reassign
/ˌriːəˈsaɪn/
核心：重新分配、调派：把（人）调到别的岗位，把（任务）交给别人。及物。名词 reassignment，可数也可不可数。并入本条。
① The officer was reassigned to a desk job after the complaint.
= 遭投诉之后，那名警官被调去坐办公室了。
be reassigned to something = 被调到某岗位
reassign = 调派
② The work was reassigned to a team in another city.
= 那份工作被重新交给了另一个城市的团队。
reassign work = 重新分配工作
③ She asked for a reassignment because she was unhappy in her department.
= 她在部门里干得不开心，申请调岗。
a reassignment = 调岗

reassure
/ˌriːəˈʃʊə(r)/
核心：使放心、使安心、打消（某人的）疑虑：reassure somebody that、about。及物。
名词 reassurance 是安慰、使人放心的话，可数也可不可数；形容词 reassuring 是令人放心的，副词 reassuringly。并入本条。
① The doctor reassured her that the operation was quite safe.
= 医生让她放心，说手术很安全。
reassure somebody that… = 让某人放心……

② The minister tried to reassure farmers about the price of milk.
= 部长想让农民在牛奶价格上放心。
reassure somebody about something = 让某人在某事上放心
③ The children needed constant reassurance during the long journey.
= 长途旅行中孩子们需要不断安慰。
reassurance = 安慰
④ It is reassuring to know that a doctor lives next door.
= 知道隔壁住着一位医生，真让人放心。
reassuring = 令人放心的
⑤ She smiled reassuringly and took the frightened child's hand.
= 她安慰地笑了笑，拉住了那个受惊孩子的手。
reassuringly = 令人放心地

reawaken
/ˌriːəˈweɪkən/

① The smell of the sea reawakened memories of her childhood.
= 大海的气味重新唤起了她童年的回忆。

rebarbative
/rɪˈbɑːbətɪv/

rebate
/ˈriːbeɪt/

rebel
/ˈrebl/（名）/rɪˈbel/（动）
核心：名词，可数，重音在前：叛乱者、反叛者、起义者；（对权威、常规的）反抗者、叛逆者。动词，重音在后，过去式 rebelled：反叛、造反、反抗：rebel against。
① The rebels took control of the capital after a week of fighting.
= 激战一个星期之后，叛军控制了首都。
the rebels = 叛军
a rebel = 叛乱者
② She was a rebel at school and often broke the rules.
= 她在学校里是个叛逆的学生，常常违反校规。
a rebel = 叛逆者
③ The people rebelled against the new tax on salt.
= 人们起来反抗新的盐税。
rebel against something = 反抗某事
rebel = 反叛
④ Teenagers often rebel against their parents.
= 十几岁的孩子常常跟父母对着干。
rebel against somebody = 跟某人对着干

rebellion
/rɪˈbeljən/
核心：两支。一是叛乱、反叛、造反，可数也可不可数：armed rebellion；二是（对权威、常规的）反抗、叛逆，不可数也可数：teenage rebellion。
① The army crushed the rebellion in the north within a month.
= 军队一个月内就镇压了北方的叛乱。
crush a rebellion = 镇压叛乱
a rebellion = 叛乱
② Dyeing her hair green was an act of rebellion against her strict parents.
= 把头发染成绿色，是她对严厉的父母的一种反抗。
an act of rebellion = 反抗的举动
rebellion against somebody = 对某人的反抗

rebellious
/rɪˈbeljəs/
核心：两支。一是叛逆的、不服管教的、难对付的；二是反叛的、造反的。副词 rebelliously；名词 rebelliousness，不可数。并入本条。
① He was a rebellious teenager who left home at sixteen.
= 他是个叛逆的少年，十六岁就离家了。
a rebellious teenager = 叛逆的少年
rebellious = 叛逆的
② The king sent troops to the rebellious provinces in the south.
= 国王派兵前往南方那些造反的省份。
rebellious provinces = 造反的省份
③ She stuck out her chin rebelliously and refused to apologize.
= 她倔强地扬起下巴，拒不道歉。
rebelliously = 倔强地、叛逆地
④ The rebelliousness of his early songs made him famous.
= 他早期歌曲里的反叛精神让他出了名。
rebelliousness = 叛逆

rebirth
/ˌriːˈbɜːθ/
核心：两支，单数。一是复兴、再生、重新兴起：the rebirth of；二是（宗教上的）转世、重生。rebirthing 是重生疗法：一种模拟出生过程的心理治疗，不可数。并入本条。
① The festival marked the rebirth of the old town after years of decline.
= 那个节日标志着衰落多年的老城重新兴起。
the rebirth of something = 某物的复兴
rebirth = 复兴
② Many religions teach that the soul goes through death and rebirth.
= 许多宗教都教导说，灵魂会经历死亡与重生。
death and rebirth = 死亡与重生
③ She tried rebirthing as a way of dealing with her fears.
= 她试过用重生疗法来应对自己的恐惧。
rebirthing = 重生疗法

reboot
/ˌriːˈbuːt/
核心：动词：（电脑）重新启动、重启，及物也可不及物；（口语）重新开始、重振。名词，可数：重启；（电影、系列的）重拍、翻拍。
① Save your work and reboot the computer when the new program has been installed.
= 新程序装好后，先存盘再重启电脑。
reboot the computer = 重启电脑
reboot = 重新启动
② The new manager hopes to reboot the club after three bad seasons.
= 新经理希望在连续三个糟糕的赛季之后重振那家俱乐部。

③ The studio is making a reboot of the old science fiction series.

reborn
/ˌriːˈbɔːn/
核心：（只用作表语）重生的、获得新生的；复兴的：be reborn as。
① After the long operation and three months of rest, she felt as if she had been reborn.
= 经过那场大手术和三个月的休养，她觉得自己像重生了一样。

② The old cotton factory by the river has been reborn as an arts centre with a café.
= 河边那座老棉纺厂改成了带咖啡馆的艺术中心，获得了新生。

rebound
/rɪˈbaʊnd/（动）/ˈriːbaʊnd/（名）
核心：动词：（球等）弹回、反弹；（价格、数量）回升；rebound on、upon 是（坏事）反过来害了（自己）。
名词，可数：反弹、回升；（篮球）篮板球。习语 on the rebound 是（因失恋而）情绪低落的时候：在刚分手、心里难受时急着开始新恋情。
① The ball rebounded off the post and into the net.
= 球打在门柱上弹进了网。
rebound off something = 从某物上弹回
rebound = 弹回
② Share prices rebounded after a sharp fall on Monday.
= 星期一大跌之后，股价回升了。
prices rebound = 价格回升
③ His lies rebounded on him when the truth came out.
= 真相大白之后，他的谎言反过来害了他自己。
rebound on somebody = 反过来害了某人
④ There has been a rebound in sales since the new year.
= 新年以来销售有所回升。
a rebound in something = 某事的回升
⑤ The tall centre took twelve rebounds in the first half.
= 那个高大的中锋上半场抢了十二个篮板。
a rebound = 篮板球
⑥ She married him on the rebound, only a month after her engagement was broken off.
= 订婚解除才一个月，她就在心里难受的时候嫁给了他。
on the rebound = 失恋后情绪低落时

rebrand
/ˌriːˈbrænd/

rebuff

rebuke

rebus
/ˈriːbəs/

① The children worked out the rebus: a picture of an eye, a heart and a sheep meant "I love you".

rebut

recalcitrant
/rɪˈkælsɪtrənt/
核心：（正式）不听话的、难对付的、桀骜不驯的：拒绝服从、不肯合作。名词 recalcitrance，不可数。并入本条。
① The teacher sent the recalcitrant pupils to see the head.
= 老师把那几个不听话的学生送去见校长。
recalcitrant pupils = 不听话的学生

② The government is trying to deal with a few recalcitrant members of its own party.
= 政府正设法对付自己党内几个难缠的议员。

③ The recalcitrance of the unions delayed the deal for months.
= 工会不肯合作，把那笔交易拖了好几个月。
